JEREMIAH — LATIN–ENGLISH (CLEMENTINE VULGATE)
Captured from the Saints.Bible staging Parallel Reader, 1 November 2026.
Verse numbers follow the Saints (Greek/Hebrew) column; 'vulgate_reference' in verses.json gives the Vulgate's own number where it differs.

JEREMIAH 1

1:1  LA: Verba Jeremiæ filii Helciæ, de sacerdotibus qui fuerunt in Anathoth, in terra Benjamin.
     EN: The words of Jeremiah son of Hilkiah, one of the priests who were in Anathoth, in the land of Benjamin.

1:2  LA: Quod factum est verbum Domini ad eum in diebus Josiæ filii Amon, regis Juda, in tertiodecimo anno regni ejus.
     EN: The word of the Lord came to him in the days of Josiah son of Amon, king of Judah, in the thirteenth year of his reign.

1:3  LA: Et factum est in diebus Joakim filii Josiæ, regis Juda, usque ad consummationem undecimi anni Sedeciæ, filii Josiæ, regis Juda, usque ad transmigrationem Jerusalem, in mense quinto.
     EN: It also came in the days of Jehoiakim son of Josiah, king of Judah, until the end of the eleventh year of Zedekiah son of Josiah, king of Judah, until Jerusalem's deportation in the fifth month.

1:4  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
     EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

1:5  LA: [Priusquam te formarem in utero, novi te, et antequam exires de vulva, sanctificavi te, et prophetam in gentibus dedi te.]
     EN: “Before I formed you in the womb, I knew you; before you came out of the womb, I consecrated you and appointed you a prophet among the nations.”

1:6  LA: Et dixi : [A, a, a, Domine Deus, ecce nescio loqui, quia puer ego sum.]
     EN: And I said, “Ah, ah, ah, Lord God! Look, I do not know how to speak, for I am a boy.”

1:7  LA: Et dixit Dominus ad me : [Noli dicere : Puer sum : quoniam ad omnia quæ mittam te ibis, et universa quæcumque mandavero tibi loqueris.
     EN: The Lord said to me, “Do not say, ‘I am a boy,’ for you shall go to everyone to whom I send you, and you shall speak everything that I command you.

1:8  LA: Ne timeas a facie eorum, quia tecum ego sum ut eruam te, dicit Dominus.]
     EN: Do not be afraid of them, for I am with you to rescue you, says the Lord.”

1:9  LA: Et misit Dominus manum suam, et tetigit os meum, et dixit Dominus ad me : [Ecce dedi verba mea in ore tuo :
     EN: The Lord stretched out His hand and touched my mouth. And the Lord said to me, “Look, I have put My words in your mouth.

1:10  LA: ecce constitui te hodie super gentes et super regna, ut evellas, et destruas, et disperdas, et dissipes, et ædifices, et plantes.]
      EN: Look, today I have set you over nations and over kingdoms, to uproot and destroy, to ruin and scatter, and to build and plant.”

1:11  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens : [Quid tu vides, Jeremia ?] Et dixi : [Virgam vigilantem ego video.]
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying, “What do you see, Jeremiah?” And I said, “I see a watching branch.”

1:12  LA: Et dixit Dominus ad me : [Bene vidisti : quia vigilabo ego super verbo meo, ut faciam illud.]
      EN: The Lord said to me, “You have seen well, for I will watch over My word to carry it out.”

1:13  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini secundo ad me, dicens : [Quid tu vides ?] Et dixi : [Ollam succensam ego video, et faciem ejus a facie aquilonis.]
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me a second time, saying, “What do you see?” And I said, “I see a boiling pot, with its opening facing from the north.”

1:14  LA: Et dixit Dominus ad me : [Ab aquilone pandetur malum super omnes habitatores terræ :
      EN: The Lord said to me, “From the north disaster will be unleashed upon all the inhabitants of the land.

1:15  LA: quia ecce ego convocabo omnes cognationes regnorum aquilonis, ait Dominus : et venient, et ponent unusquisque solium suum in introitu portarum Jerusalem, et super omnes muros ejus in circuitu, et super universas urbes Juda :
      EN: For look, I will summon all the clans of the northern kingdoms, says the Lord. They will come, and each will set his throne at the entrance of Jerusalem's gates, against all its surrounding walls, and against all the cities of Judah.

1:16  LA: et loquar judicia mea cum eis super omnem malitiam eorum qui dereliquerunt me, et libaverunt diis alienis, et adoraverunt opus manuum suarum.
      EN: I will pronounce My judgments against them for all the wickedness of those who have abandoned Me, poured out offerings to foreign gods, and worshiped the work of their own hands.

1:17  LA: Tu ergo, accinge lumbos tuos, et surge, et loquere ad eos omnia quæ ego præcipio tibi. Ne formides a facie eorum, nec enim timere te faciam vultum eorum.
      EN: So gird your loins, rise, and speak to them everything that I command you. Do not be terrified of them, for I will not make you fear their faces.

1:18  LA: Ego quippe dedi te hodie in civitatem munitam, et in columnam ferream, et in murum æreum, super omnem terram, regibus Juda, principibus ejus, et sacerdotibus, et populo terræ.
      EN: For today I have made you a fortified city, an iron pillar, and a bronze wall against the whole land: against the kings of Judah, its rulers, its priests, and the people of the land.

1:19  LA: Et bellabunt adversum te, et non prævalebunt, quia ego tecum sum, ait Dominus, ut liberem te.]
      EN: They will fight against you, but they will not prevail, for I am with you, says the Lord, to deliver you.”

JEREMIAH 2

2:2  LA: [Vade, et clama in auribus Jerusalem, dicens : Hæc dicit Dominus : Recordatus sum tui, miserans adolescentiam tuam, et caritatem desponsationis tuæ, quando secuta es me in deserto, in terra quæ non seminatur.
     EN: “Go and cry in Jerusalem's hearing: Thus says the Lord: I have remembered you, taking pity on your youth and on the love of your betrothal, when you followed Me in the wilderness, in a land that is not sown.

2:3  LA: Sanctus Israël Domino, primitiæ frugum ejus : omnes qui devorant eum delinquunt : mala venient super eos, dicit Dominus.
     EN: Israel is holy to the Lord, the firstfruits of His harvest. All who devour him incur guilt; disasters will come upon them, says the Lord.

2:4  LA: Audite verbum Domini, domus Jacob, et omnes cognationes domus Israël.
     EN: Hear the word of the Lord, house of Jacob, and all the clans of the house of Israel.

2:5  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Quid invenerunt patres vestri in me iniquitatis, quia elongaverunt a me, et ambulaverunt post vanitatem, et vani facti sunt ?
     EN: Thus says the Lord: What wrong did your fathers find in Me, that they withdrew from Me, followed emptiness, and became empty themselves?

2:6  LA: Et non dixerunt : Ubi est Dominus qui ascendere nos fecit de terra Ægypti ; qui traduxit nos per desertum, per terram inhabitabilem et inviam, per terram sitis, et imaginem mortis, per terram in qua non ambulavit vir, neque habitavit homo ?
     EN: They did not say, ‘Where is the Lord, who brought us up from the land of Egypt, who led us through the wilderness, through an uninhabitable and trackless land, through a land of thirst and the image of death, through a land where no man walked and no human lived?’

2:7  LA: Et induxi vos in terram Carmeli, ut comederetis fructum ejus et optima illius : et ingressi contaminastis terram meam, et hæreditatem meam posuistis in abominationem.
     EN: I brought you into the land of Carmel to eat its fruit and its finest produce. But when you entered, you defiled My land and made My inheritance an abomination.

2:8  LA: Sacerdotes non dixerunt : Ubi est Dominus ? et tenentes legem nescierunt me, et pastores prævaricati sunt in me, et prophetæ prophetaverunt in Baal, et idola secuti sunt.
     EN: The priests did not say, ‘Where is the Lord?’ Those who handled the law did not know Me; the shepherds rebelled against Me; the prophets prophesied by Baal and followed idols.

2:9  LA: Propterea adhuc judicio contendam vobiscum, ait Dominus, et cum filiis vestris disceptabo.
     EN: Therefore I will still contend with you in judgment, says the Lord, and I will dispute with your children.

2:10  LA: Transite ad insulas Cethim, et videte : et in Cedar mittite, et considerate vehementer : et videte si factum est hujuscemodi :
      EN: Cross to the islands of Kittim and look; send to Kedar and examine closely. See whether anything like this has happened:

2:11  LA: si mutavit gens deos suos, et certe ipsi non sunt dii : populus vero meus mutavit gloriam suam in idolum.
      EN: has a nation changed its gods, though they certainly are not gods? But My people have exchanged their glory for an idol.

2:12  LA: Obstupescite, cæli, super hoc, et portæ ejus, desolamini vehementer, dicit Dominus.
      EN: Be astounded at this, heavens; let their gates be utterly desolate, says the Lord.

2:13  LA: Duo enim mala fecit populus meus : me dereliquerunt fontem aquæ vivæ, et foderunt sibi cisternas, cisternas dissipatas, quia continere non valent aquas.
      EN: For My people have done two evils: they have abandoned Me, the fountain of living water, and dug themselves cisterns, broken cisterns that cannot hold water.

2:14  LA: Numquid servus est Israël, aut vernaculus ? quare ergo factus est in prædam ?
      EN: Is Israel a slave, or a household-born servant? Why, then, has he become plunder?

2:15  LA: Super eum rugierunt leones, et dederunt vocem suam : posuerunt terram ejus in solitudinem. Civitates ejus exustæ sunt, et non est qui habitet in eis.
      EN: Lions have roared against him and raised their voices. They have made his land a wilderness; his cities have been burned, and no one lives in them.

2:16  LA: Filii quoque Mempheos et Taphnes constupraverunt te usque ad verticem.
      EN: The sons of Memphis and Tahpanhes have also violated you, even to the crown of your head.

2:17  LA: Numquid non istud factum est tibi, quia dereliquisti Dominum Deum tuum eo tempore quo ducebat te per viam ?
      EN: Has this not happened to you because you abandoned the Lord your God at the very time He was leading you along the way?

2:18  LA: Et nunc quid tibi vis in via Ægypti, ut bibas aquam turbidam ? et quid tibi cum via Assyriorum, ut bibas aquam fluminis ?
      EN: And now, what do you want on the road to Egypt, to drink muddy water? What have you to do with the road to the Assyrians, to drink the river's water?

2:19  LA: Arguet te malitia tua, et aversio tua increpabit te. Scito et vide quia malum et amarum est reliquisse te Dominum Deum tuum, et non esse timorem mei apud te, dicit Dominus Deus exercituum.
      EN: Your own wickedness will convict you, and your turning away will rebuke you. Know and see how evil and bitter it is that you have abandoned the Lord your God and have no fear of Me, says the Lord God of hosts.

2:20  LA: A sæculo confregisti jugum meum : rupisti vincula mea, et dixisti : Non serviam. In omni enim colle sublimi, et sub omni ligno frondoso, tu prosternebaris meretrix.
      EN: Long ago you broke My yoke and tore off My bonds. You said, ‘I will not serve.’ For on every high hill and under every leafy tree you lay down as a prostitute.

2:21  LA: Ego autem plantavi te vineam electam, omne semen verum : quomodo ergo conversa es mihi in pravum, vinea aliena ?
      EN: Yet I planted you as a choice vine, wholly of sound stock. How, then, have you changed before Me into a corrupt, foreign vine?

2:22  LA: Si laveris te nitro, et multiplicaveris tibi herbam borith, maculata es in iniquitate tua coram me, dicit Dominus Deus.
      EN: Even if you wash yourself with natron and use much soapwort, you remain stained in your guilt before Me, says the Lord God.

2:23  LA: Quomodo dicis : Non sum polluta ; post Baalim non ambulavi ? Vide vias tuas in convalle ; scito quid feceris : cursor levis explicans vias suas.
      EN: How can you say, ‘I am not defiled; I have not followed the Baals’? Look at your ways in the valley; know what you have done: a swift runner, spreading out her paths!

2:24  LA: Onager assuetus in solitudine, in desiderio animæ suæ attraxit ventum amoris sui : nullus avertet eam : omnes qui quærunt eam non deficient : in menstruis ejus invenient eam.
      EN: A wild donkey accustomed to the wilderness, in her soul's desire she draws in the wind of her passion. No one will turn her aside. All who seek her will not grow weary; they will find her in her monthly season.

2:25  LA: Prohibe pedem tuum a nuditate, et guttur tuum a siti. Et dixisti : Desperavi : nequaquam faciam : adamavi quippe alienos, et post eos ambulabo.]
      EN: Keep your foot from going bare and your throat from thirst. But you said, ‘There is no hope; I will not do it. For I have loved strangers, and I will follow them.’

2:26  LA: [Quomodo confunditur fur quando deprehenditur, sic confusi sunt domus Israël, ipsi et reges eorum, principes, et sacerdotes, et prophetæ eorum,
      EN: As a thief is ashamed when caught, so the house of Israel has been put to shame: they, their kings, their rulers, their priests, and their prophets,

2:27  LA: dicentes ligno : Pater meus es tu : et lapidi : Tu me genuisti. Verterunt ad me tergum et non faciem, et in tempore afflictionis suæ dicent : Surge, et libera nos.
      EN: who say to wood, ‘You are my father,’ and to a stone, ‘You gave me birth.’ They have turned their backs to Me, not their faces. Yet in the time of their distress they will say, ‘Rise up and deliver us.’

2:28  LA: Ubi sunt dii tui quos fecisti tibi ? surgant, et liberent te in tempore afflictionis tuæ : secundum numerum quippe civitatum tuarum erant dii tui, Juda.
      EN: Where are your gods that you made for yourself? Let them rise and deliver you in the time of your distress! For your gods were as many as your cities, Judah.

2:29  LA: Quid vultis mecum judicio contendere ? omnes dereliquistis me, dicit Dominus.
      EN: Why do you wish to contend with Me in judgment? You have all abandoned Me, says the Lord.

2:30  LA: Frustra percussi filios vestros : disciplinam non receperunt. Devoravit gladius vester prophetas vestros : quasi leo vastator
      EN: In vain I struck your children; they would not accept correction. Your own sword devoured your prophets. Like a ravaging lion

2:31  LA: generatio vestra. Videte verbum Domini : numquid solitudo factus sum Israëli, aut terra serotina ? quare ergo dixit populus meus : Recessimus ; non veniemus ultra ad te ?
      EN: is your generation. Consider the word of the Lord: Have I become a wilderness to Israel, or a land that yields late? Why, then, have My people said, ‘We have gone away; we will come to You no more’?

2:32  LA: Numquid obliviscetur virgo ornamenti sui, aut sponsa fasciæ pectoralis suæ ? populus vero meus oblitus est mei diebus innumeris.
      EN: Does a maiden forget her ornaments, or a bride her breastband? Yet My people have forgotten Me for days beyond counting.

2:33  LA: Quid niteris bonam ostendere viam tuam ad quærendam dilectionem, quæ insuper et malitias tuas docuisti vias tuas,
      EN: Why do you strive to make your way seem good in your search for love, when you have even taught your ways your own wickedness,

2:34  LA: et in alis tuis inventus est sanguis animarum pauperum et innocentum ? non in fossis inveni eos, sed in omnibus quæ supra memoravi.
      EN: and on your skirts is found the lifeblood of the poor and innocent? I did not find them in pits, but in all the things I have mentioned above.

2:35  LA: Et dixisti : Absque peccato et innocens ego sum, et propterea avertatur furor tuus a me. Ecce ego judicio contendam tecum, eo quod dixeris : Non peccavi.
      EN: Yet you said, ‘I am without sin and innocent; therefore let Your fury turn away from me.’ Look, I will contend with you in judgment because you have said, ‘I have not sinned.’

2:36  LA: Quam vilis facta es nimis, iterans vias tuas ! et ab Ægypto confunderis, sicut confusa es ab Assur.
      EN: How utterly contemptible you have become, changing your ways again and again! You will be put to shame by Egypt, just as you were put to shame by Assyria.

2:37  LA: Nam et ab ista egredieris, et manus tuæ erunt super caput tuum : quoniam obtrivit Dominus confidentiam tuam, et nihil habebis prosperum in ea.]
      EN: For you will leave that land too, with your hands upon your head, because the Lord has crushed the one in whom you trusted, and you will have no success through it.”

JEREMIAH 3

3:1  LA: Vulgo dicitur : Si dimiserit vir uxorem suam, et recedens ab eo duxerit virum alterum, numquid revertetur ad eam ultra ? numquid non polluta et contaminata erit mulier illa ? Tu autem fornicata es cum amatoribus multis : tamen revertere ad me, dicit Dominus, et ego suscipiam te.
     EN: It is commonly said: “If a man sends away his wife, and she leaves him and marries another man, will he return to her again? Will that woman not be polluted and defiled? But you have prostituted yourself with many lovers; nevertheless, return to Me, says the Lord, and I will receive you.

3:2  LA: Leva oculos tuos in directum, et vide ubi non prostrata sis. In viis sedebas, exspectans eos quasi latro in solitudine : et polluisti terram in fornicationibus tuis, et in malitiis tuis.
     EN: Lift your eyes straight ahead and see where you have not lain down. You sat along the roads waiting for them like a robber in the wilderness, and you polluted the land with your prostitution and your wickedness.

3:3  LA: Quam ob rem prohibitæ sunt stillæ pluviarum, et serotinus imber non fuit. Frons mulieris meretricis facta est tibi ; noluisti erubescere.
     EN: Therefore the drops of rain have been withheld, and there has been no late rain. You have acquired a prostitute's forehead; you have refused to blush.

3:4  LA: Ergo saltem amodo voca me : Pater meus, dux virginitatis meæ tu es :
     EN: So at least from now on call to Me, ‘My Father, You are the guide of my virgin youth.

3:5  LA: numquid irasceris in perpetuum, aut perseverabis in finem ? ecce locuta es, et fecisti mala, et potuisti.]
     EN: Will You be angry forever, or persist to the end?’ Look, you have spoken, and you have done evil and had your way.”

3:6  LA: Et dixit Dominus ad me in diebus Josiæ regis : [Numquid vidisti quæ fecerit aversatrix Israël ? Abiit sibimet super omnem montem excelsum, et sub omni ligno frondoso, et fornicata est ibi.
     EN: The Lord said to me in the days of King Josiah, “Have you seen what apostate Israel has done? She went up every high mountain and under every leafy tree, and there she prostituted herself.

3:7  LA: Et dixi, cum fecisset hæc omnia : Ad me revertere : et non est reversa. Et vidit prævaricatrix soror ejus Juda
     EN: After she had done all these things, I said, ‘Return to Me,’ but she did not return. Her faithless sister Judah saw

3:8  LA: quia pro eo quod mœchata esset aversatrix Israël, dimisissem eam, et dedissem ei libellum repudii : et non timuit prævaricatrix Juda soror ejus, sed abiit, et fornicata est etiam ipsa :
     EN: that, because apostate Israel had committed adultery, I had sent her away and given her a certificate of divorce. Yet her faithless sister Judah was not afraid, but went and prostituted herself as well.

3:9  LA: et facilitate fornicationis suæ contaminavit terram, et mœchata est cum lapide et ligno :
     EN: By the ease with which she prostituted herself, she defiled the land and committed adultery with stone and wood.

3:10  LA: et in omnibus his non est reversa ad me prævaricatrix soror ejus Juda in toto corde suo, sed in mendacio, ait Dominus.]
      EN: And despite all this, her faithless sister Judah did not return to Me with her whole heart, but deceitfully, says the Lord.”

3:11  LA: Et dixit Dominus ad me : Justificavit animam suam aversatrix Israël, comparatione prævaricatricis Judæ.
      EN: The Lord said to me, “Apostate Israel has shown herself righteous in comparison with faithless Judah.

3:12  LA: Vade, et clama sermones istos contra aquilonem, et dices : [Revertere, aversatrix Israël, ait Dominus, et non avertam faciem meam a vobis, quia sanctus ego sum, dicit Dominus, et non irascar in perpetuum.
      EN: Go and proclaim these words toward the north, and say: Return, apostate Israel, says the Lord, and I will not turn My face away from you, for I am holy, says the Lord, and I will not be angry forever.

3:13  LA: Verumtamen scito iniquitatem tuam, quia in Dominum Deum tuum prævaricata es, et dispersisti vias tuas alienis sub omni ligno frondoso, et vocem meam non audisti, ait Dominus.
      EN: Only acknowledge your guilt, because you have rebelled against the Lord your God, scattered your paths among strangers under every leafy tree, and not listened to My voice, says the Lord.

3:14  LA: Convertimini, filii revertentes, dicit Dominus, quia ego vir vester : et assumam vos unum de civitate, et duos de cognatione, et introducam vos in Sion.
      EN: Return, you returning children, says the Lord, for I am your husband. I will take you, one from a city and two from a clan, and bring you into Zion.

3:15  LA: Et dabo vobis pastores juxta cor meum, et pascent vos scientia et doctrina.
      EN: I will give you shepherds according to My heart, and they will feed you with knowledge and instruction.

3:16  LA: Cumque multiplicati fueritis, et creveritis in terra in diebus illis, ait Dominus, non dicent ultra : Arca testamenti Domini : neque ascendet super cor, neque recordabuntur illius, nec visitabitur, nec fiet ultra.
      EN: When you have multiplied and increased in the land in those days, says the Lord, they will no longer say, ‘The ark of the Lord's covenant.’ It will not come to mind; they will not remember it or visit it, and it will not be made again.

3:17  LA: In tempore illo vocabunt Jerusalem solium Domini : et congregabuntur ad eam omnes gentes in nomine Domini in Jerusalem, et non ambulabunt post pravitatem cordis sui pessimi.
      EN: At that time they will call Jerusalem the throne of the Lord, and all nations will gather to it in the name of the Lord, in Jerusalem. They will no longer follow the perversity of their utterly evil hearts.

3:18  LA: In diebus illis ibit domus Juda ad domum Israël, et venient simul de terra aquilonis ad terram quam dedi patribus vestris.
      EN: In those days the house of Judah will go to the house of Israel, and together they will come from the land of the north to the land that I gave your fathers.

3:19  LA: Ego autem dixi : Quomodo ponam te in filios, et tribuam tibi terram desiderabilem, hæreditatem præclaram exercituum gentium ? Et dixi : Patrem vocabis me, et post me ingredi non cessabis.
      EN: But I said, ‘How shall I place you among the children and give you a desirable land, a splendid inheritance among the hosts of the nations?’ And I said, ‘You will call Me Father and will not cease following Me.’

3:20  LA: Sed quomodo si contemnat mulier amatorem suum, sic contempsit me domus Israël, dicit Dominus.
      EN: But as a woman scorns her lover, so the house of Israel has scorned Me, says the Lord.

3:21  LA: Vox in viis audita est, ploratus et ululatus filiorum Israël : quoniam iniquam fecerunt viam suam ; obliti sunt Domini Dei sui.
      EN: A voice is heard along the roads, the weeping and wailing of the children of Israel, because they have made their way unjust and forgotten the Lord their God.

3:22  LA: Convertimini, filii revertentes, et sanabo aversiones vestras. Ecce nos venimus ad te : tu enim es Dominus Deus noster.
      EN: Return, you returning children, and I will heal your turning away. ‘Here we are; we come to You, for You are the Lord our God.

3:23  LA: Vere mendaces erant colles, et multitudo montium : vere in Domino Deo nostro salus Israël.
      EN: Truly the hills were deceptive, and the multitude of mountains; truly in the Lord our God is Israel's salvation.

3:24  LA: Confusio comedit laborem patrum nostrorum ab adolescentia nostra : greges eorum, et armenta eorum, filios eorum, et filias eorum.
      EN: Shame has consumed the labor of our fathers since our youth: their flocks and their herds, their sons and their daughters.

3:25  LA: Dormiemus in confusione nostra, et operiet nos ignominia nostra : quoniam Domino Deo nostro peccavimus nos, et patres nostri, ab adolescentia nostra usque ad diem hanc : et non audivimus vocem Domini Dei nostri.]
      EN: We will lie down in our shame, and our disgrace will cover us, for we and our fathers have sinned against the Lord our God from our youth to this very day, and we have not listened to the voice of the Lord our God.’”

JEREMIAH 4

4:1  LA: Si reverteris, Israël, ait Dominus, ad me convertere : si abstuleris offendicula tua a facie mea, non commoveberis.
     EN: “If you return, Israel, says the Lord, return to Me. If you remove your abominations from My presence, you will not be shaken.

4:2  LA: Et jurabis : Vivit Dominus in veritate, et in judicio, et in justitia : et benedicent eum gentes, ipsumque laudabunt.
     EN: You shall swear, ‘As the Lord lives,’ in truth, in judgment, and in justice; the nations will bless Him and praise Him.”

4:3  LA: Hæc enim dicit Dominus viro Juda et Jerusalem : Novate vobis novale, et nolite serere super spinas.
     EN: For thus says the Lord to the men of Judah and Jerusalem: “Break up fresh ground for yourselves, and do not sow among thorns.

4:4  LA: Circumcidimini Domino, et auferte præputia cordium vestrorum, viri Juda, et habitatores Jerusalem : ne forte egrediatur ut ignis indignatio mea, et succendatur, et non sit qui extinguat, propter malitiam cogitationum vestrarum.]
     EN: Be circumcised for the Lord, and remove the foreskins of your hearts, men of Judah and inhabitants of Jerusalem, lest My indignation come forth like fire and burn, with no one to quench it, because of the wickedness of your thoughts.

4:5  LA: [Annuntiate in Juda, et in Jerusalem auditum facite : loquimini, et canite tuba in terra, clamate fortiter, et dicite : Congregamini, et ingrediamur civitates munitas.
     EN: Declare it in Judah and make it heard in Jerusalem. Speak and sound the trumpet in the land; cry out loudly and say, ‘Gather together, and let us enter the fortified cities.’

4:6  LA: Levate signum in Sion ; confortamini, nolite stare : quia malum ego adduco ab aquilone, et contritionem magnam.
     EN: Raise a signal in Zion; take courage, do not stand still, for I am bringing disaster from the north and great destruction.

4:7  LA: Ascendit leo de cubili suo, et prædo gentium se levavit : egressus est de loco suo ut ponat terram tuam in solitudinem : civitates tuæ vastabuntur, remanentes absque habitatore.
     EN: The lion has come up from his den, and the plunderer of nations has risen. He has left his place to make your land a wilderness; your cities will be laid waste, left without an inhabitant.

4:8  LA: Super hoc accingite vos ciliciis ; plangite, et ululate : quia non est aversa ira furoris Domini a nobis.
     EN: For this, clothe yourselves in sackcloth, mourn and wail, for the fierce anger of the Lord has not turned away from us.

4:9  LA: Et erit in die illa, dicit Dominus : peribit cor regis, et cor principum, et obstupescent sacerdotes, et prophetæ consternabuntur.]
     EN: On that day, says the Lord, the king will lose heart, and so will the rulers; the priests will be astounded and the prophets dismayed.”

4:10  LA: Et dixi : [Heu ! heu ! heu ! Domine Deus, ergone decepisti populum istum et Jerusalem, dicens : Pax erit vobis : et ecce pervenit gladius usque ad animam ?]
      EN: And I said, “Alas, alas, alas, Lord God! Have You then deceived this people and Jerusalem, saying, ‘You will have peace,’ when now the sword has reached their very lives?”

4:11  LA: [In tempore illo dicetur populo huic et Jerusalem : Ventus urens in viis quæ sunt in deserto viæ filiæ populi mei, non ad ventilandum et ad purgandum.
      EN: “At that time it will be said to this people and to Jerusalem: A scorching wind on the paths in the wilderness, on the way of the daughter of My people, neither to winnow nor to cleanse!

4:12  LA: Spiritus plenus ex his veniet mihi, et nunc ego loquar judicia mea cum eis.
      EN: A wind in full force from these places will come for Me; and now I will pronounce My judgments against them.

4:13  LA: Ecce quasi nubes ascendet, et quasi tempestas currus ejus : velociores aquilis equi illius. Væ nobis, quoniam vastati sumus.
      EN: Look, he will rise like a cloud, and his chariots like a storm; his horses are swifter than eagles. Woe to us, for we have been devastated!

4:14  LA: Lava a malitia cor tuum, Jerusalem, ut salva fias : usquequo morabuntur in te cogitationes noxiæ ?
      EN: Wash your heart free of wickedness, Jerusalem, so that you may be saved. How long will harmful thoughts lodge within you?

4:15  LA: Vox enim annuntiantis a Dan, et notum facientis idolum de monte Ephraim.
      EN: For a voice announces from Dan and makes the idol known from Mount Ephraim.

4:16  LA: Dicite gentibus : Ecce auditum est in Jerusalem custodes venire de terra longinqua, et dare super civitates Juda vocem suam :
      EN: Tell the nations: Look, it has been heard in Jerusalem that besiegers are coming from a distant land and raising their voices against the cities of Judah.

4:17  LA: quasi custodes agrorum facti sunt super eam in gyro, quia me ad iracundiam provocavit, dicit Dominus.
      EN: Like keepers of fields they surround her, because she has provoked Me to anger, says the Lord.

4:18  LA: Viæ tuæ et cogitationes tuæ fecerunt hæc tibi : ista malitia tua, quia amara, quia tetigit cor tuum.
      EN: Your ways and your thoughts have brought these things upon you. This is your wickedness: how bitter it is, for it has touched your heart!

4:19  LA: Ventrem meum, ventrem meum doleo ; sensus cordis mei turbati sunt in me. Non tacebo, quoniam vocem buccinæ audivit anima mea, clamorem prælii.
      EN: My belly, my belly aches! The feelings of my heart are disturbed within me. I will not be silent, for my soul has heard the sound of the trumpet, the battle cry.

4:20  LA: Contritio super contritionem vocata est, et vastata est omnis terra : repente vastata sunt tabernacula mea ; subito pelles meæ.
      EN: Destruction upon destruction has been announced, and the whole land has been devastated. Suddenly my tents have been destroyed, my tent cloths in an instant.

4:21  LA: Usquequo videbo fugientem ; audiam vocem buccinæ ?
      EN: How long shall I see one fleeing and hear the trumpet's sound?

4:22  LA: Quia stultus populus meus me non cognovit : filii insipientes sunt et vecordes : sapientes sunt ut faciant mala, bene autem facere nescierunt.
      EN: For My foolish people have not known Me; they are senseless, witless children. They are wise in doing evil, but do not know how to do good.

4:23  LA: Aspexi terram, et ecce vacua erat et nihili ; et cælos, et non erat lux in eis.
      EN: I looked at the earth, and it was empty and nothing; at the heavens, and there was no light in them.

4:24  LA: Vidi montes, et ecce movebantur : et omnes colles conturbati sunt.
      EN: I saw the mountains, and they were shaking, and all the hills were in turmoil.

4:25  LA: Intuitus sum, et non erat homo : et omne volatile cæli recessit.
      EN: I looked, and there was no human being, and every bird of heaven had gone away.

4:26  LA: Aspexi, et ecce Carmelus desertus, et omnes urbes ejus destructæ sunt a facie Domini, et a facie iræ furoris ejus.
      EN: I looked, and Carmel was a wilderness, and all its cities were destroyed before the Lord, before His fierce anger.

4:27  LA: Hæc enim dicit Dominus : Deserta erit omnis terra, sed tamen consummationem non faciam.
      EN: For thus says the Lord: The whole land will be desolate, but I will not bring it to a complete end.

4:28  LA: Lugebit terra, et mœrebunt cæli desuper, eo quod locutus sum. Cogitavi, et non pœnituit me, nec aversus sum ab eo.
      EN: The earth will mourn, and the heavens above will grieve, because I have spoken. I have resolved it and have not relented, nor have I turned away from it.

4:29  LA: A voce equitis et mittentis sagittam fugit omnis civitas : ingressi sunt ardua, et ascenderunt rupes : universæ urbes derelictæ sunt, et non habitat in eis homo.
      EN: At the sound of the horseman and the archer, every city has fled. They have gone into the steep places and climbed the rocks; all the cities have been abandoned, and no human lives in them.

4:30  LA: Tu autem vastata, quid facies ? cum vestieris te coccino, cum ornata fueris monili aureo, et pinxeris stibio oculos tuos, frustra componeris : contempserunt te amatores tui ; animam tuam quærent.
      EN: And you, devastated one, what will you do? Though you dress in scarlet, adorn yourself with a gold necklace, and paint your eyes with antimony, you beautify yourself in vain. Your lovers have despised you; they will seek your life.

4:31  LA: Vocem enim quasi parturientis audivi, angustias ut puerperæ : vox filiæ Sion intermorientis, expandentisque manus suas : Væ mihi, quia defecit anima mea propter interfectos !]
      EN: For I have heard a voice like a woman in labor, anguish like a woman giving birth: the voice of daughter Zion, fainting and stretching out her hands: ‘Woe to me, for my soul has failed because of the slain!’”

JEREMIAH 5

5:1  LA: Circuite vias Jerusalem, et aspicite, et considerate, et quærite in plateis ejus, an inveniatis virum facientem judicium, et quærentem fidem : et propitius ero ei.
     EN: “Go around the streets of Jerusalem, look and consider; search its squares to see whether you can find a man who acts justly and seeks faithfulness, and I will be merciful to it.

5:2  LA: Quod si etiam : Vivit Dominus, dixerint, et hoc falso jurabunt.
     EN: Even if they say, ‘As the Lord lives,’ they will swear falsely even in this.”

5:3  LA: Domine, oculi tui respiciunt fidem : percussisti eos, et non doluerunt ; attrivisti eos, et renuerunt accipere disciplinam : induraverunt facies suas supra petram, et noluerunt reverti.
     EN: Lord, Your eyes look for faithfulness. You struck them, but they felt no pain; You crushed them, but they refused correction. They made their faces harder than rock and would not return.

5:4  LA: Ego autem dixi : Forsitan pauperes sunt et stulti, ignorantes viam Domini, judicium Dei sui.
     EN: But I said, “Perhaps these are poor and foolish, not knowing the way of the Lord, the judgment of their God.

5:5  LA: Ibo igitur ad optimates, et loquar eis : ipsi enim cognoverunt viam Domini, judicium Dei sui : et ecce magis hi simul confregerunt jugum ; ruperunt vincula.
     EN: So I will go to the great and speak to them, for they have known the way of the Lord, the judgment of their God.” But look, these all the more had together broken the yoke and torn off the bonds.

5:6  LA: Idcirco percussit eos leo de silva ; lupus ad vesperam vastavit eos : pardus vigilans super civitates eorum : omnis qui egressus fuerit ex eis capietur : quia multiplicatæ sunt prævaricationes eorum ; confortatæ sunt aversiones eorum.
     EN: Therefore a lion from the forest has struck them; a wolf has ravaged them at evening; a leopard watches over their cities. Everyone who leaves them will be caught, because their rebellions have multiplied and their apostasies have grown strong.

5:7  LA: Super quo propitius tibi esse potero ? filii tui dereliquerunt me, et jurant in his qui non sunt dii. Saturavi eos, et mœchati sunt, et in domo meretricis luxuriabantur.
     EN: “How can I be merciful to you? Your children have abandoned Me and swear by those who are not gods. I filled them, and they committed adultery and indulged themselves in a prostitute's house.

5:8  LA: Equi amatores et emissarii facti sunt : unusquisque ad uxorem proximi sui hinniebat.
     EN: They have become lustful, roaming stallions; each neighed after his neighbor's wife.

5:9  LA: Numquid super his non visitabo, dicit Dominus, et in gente tali non ulciscetur anima mea ?
     EN: Shall I not punish these things, says the Lord, and shall My soul not take vengeance on such a nation?

5:10  LA: Ascendite muros ejus, et dissipate : consummationem autem nolite facere : auferte propagines ejus, quia non sunt Domini.
      EN: Climb its walls and destroy, but do not make a complete end. Remove its vine shoots, for they are not the Lord's.

5:11  LA: Prævaricatione enim prævaricata est in me domus Israël, et domus Juda, ait Dominus.
      EN: For the house of Israel and the house of Judah have utterly betrayed Me, says the Lord.

5:12  LA: Negaverunt Dominum, et dixerunt : Non est ipse : neque veniet super nos malum : gladium et famem non videbimus.
      EN: They have denied the Lord and said, ‘It is not He; no disaster will come upon us; we will see neither sword nor famine.

5:13  LA: Prophetæ fuerunt in ventum locuti, et responsum non fuit in eis : hæc ergo evenient illis.
      EN: The prophets have spoken into the wind, and no divine answer was in them. So let these things happen to them!’

5:14  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus exercituum : Quia locuti estis verbum istud, ecce ego do verba mea in ore tuo in ignem, et populum istum in ligna, et vorabit eos.
      EN: Thus says the Lord God of hosts: Because you have spoken this word, look, I am making My words in your mouth a fire, and this people wood; it will devour them.

5:15  LA: Ecce ego adducam super vos gentem de longinquo, domus Israël, ait Dominus : gentem robustam, gentem antiquam, gentem cujus ignorabis linguam, nec intelliges quid loquatur.
      EN: Look, I will bring a nation from far away against you, house of Israel, says the Lord: a mighty nation, an ancient nation, a nation whose language you will not know and whose speech you will not understand.

5:16  LA: Pharetra ejus quasi sepulchrum patens ; universi fortes.
      EN: Its quiver is like an open tomb; all of them are warriors.

5:17  LA: Et comedet segetes tuas et panem tuum ; devorabit filios tuos et filias tuas ; comedet gregem tuum et armenta tua ; comedet vineam tuam et ficum tuam : et conteret urbes munitas tuas, in quibus tu habes fiduciam, gladio.
      EN: It will eat your crops and your bread; it will devour your sons and your daughters; it will eat your flock and your herds; it will eat your vines and your fig trees. With the sword it will crush your fortified cities in which you trust.

5:18  LA: Verumtamen in diebus illis, ait Dominus, non faciam vos in consummationem.
      EN: Yet even in those days, says the Lord, I will not make a complete end of you.

5:19  LA: Quod si dixeritis : Quare fecit nobis Dominus Deus noster hæc omnia ? dices ad eos : Sicut dereliquistis me, et servistis deo alieno in terra vestra, sic servietis alienis in terra non vestra.
      EN: If you say, ‘Why has the Lord our God done all these things to us?’ you shall tell them: Just as you abandoned Me and served a foreign god in your own land, so you will serve foreigners in a land not your own.

5:20  LA: Annuntiate hoc domui Jacob, et auditum facite in Juda, dicentes :
      EN: Declare this to the house of Jacob and make it heard in Judah, saying:

5:21  LA: Audi, popule stulte, qui non habes cor : qui habentes oculos, non videtis ; et aures, et non auditis.
      EN: Hear this, foolish and senseless people, who have eyes but do not see, and ears but do not hear.

5:22  LA: Me ergo non timebitis, ait Dominus, et a facie mea non dolebitis ? qui posui arenam terminum mari, præceptum sempiternum quod non præteribit : et commovebuntur, et non poterunt ; et intumescent fluctus ejus, et non transibunt illud.
      EN: Will you not fear Me, then, says the Lord, and tremble before Me? I set the sand as the sea's boundary, an everlasting decree that it cannot cross. Its waves will churn but be powerless; they will swell but not cross it.

5:23  LA: Populo autem huic factum est cor incredulum et exasperans : recesserunt, et abierunt.
      EN: But this people has developed an unbelieving and rebellious heart; they have withdrawn and gone away.

5:24  LA: Et non dixerunt in corde suo : Metuamus Dominum Deum nostrum, qui dat nobis pluviam temporaneam et serotinam in tempore suo, plenitudinem annuæ messis custodientem nobis.
      EN: They have not said in their hearts, ‘Let us fear the Lord our God, who gives us early and late rain in their season and preserves the fullness of the yearly harvest for us.’

5:25  LA: Iniquitates vestræ declinaverunt hæc, et peccata vestra prohibuerunt bonum a vobis :
      EN: Your iniquities have diverted these things, and your sins have withheld good from you.

5:26  LA: quia inventi sunt in populo meo impii insidiantes quasi aucupes, laqueos ponentes et pedicas ad capiendos viros.
      EN: For among My people wicked men have been found lying in wait like bird catchers, setting snares and traps to catch men.

5:27  LA: Sicut decipula plena avibus, sic domus eorum plenæ dolo : ideo magnificati sunt et ditati.
      EN: Like a cage full of birds, their houses are full of deceit; therefore they have become great and rich.

5:28  LA: Incrassati sunt et impinguati, et præterierunt sermones meos pessime. Causam viduæ non judicaverunt, causam pupilli non direxerunt, et judicium pauperum non judicaverunt.
      EN: They have grown stout and fat and have wickedly disregarded My words. They have not judged the widow's case, upheld the orphan's case, or rendered judgment for the poor.

5:29  LA: Numquid super his non visitabo, dicit Dominus, aut super gentem hujuscemodi non ulciscetur anima mea ?
      EN: Shall I not punish these things, says the Lord, or shall My soul not take vengeance on a nation like this?

5:30  LA: Stupor et mirabilia facta sunt in terra :
      EN: Something appalling and astonishing has happened in the land:

5:31  LA: prophetæ prophetabant mendacium, et sacerdotes applaudebant manibus suis, et populus meus dilexit talia. Quid igitur fiet in novissimo ejus ?]
      EN: the prophets prophesied lies, the priests applauded with their hands, and My people loved such things. What, then, will happen at its end?”

JEREMIAH 6

6:1  LA: Confortamini , filii Benjamin, in medio Jerusalem : et in Thecua clangite buccina, et super Bethacarem levate vexillum, quia malum visum est ab aquilone, et contritio magna.
     EN: “Take courage, children of Benjamin, in the midst of Jerusalem. Sound the trumpet in Tekoa and raise a banner over Beth-haccherem, for disaster has appeared from the north, and great destruction.

6:2  LA: Speciosæ et delicatæ assimilavi filiam Sion.
     EN: I have likened daughter Zion to a beautiful and delicate woman.

6:3  LA: Ad eam venient pastores et greges eorum ; fixerunt in ea tentoria in circuitu : pascet unusquisque eos qui sub manu sua sunt.
     EN: Shepherds and their flocks will come to her; they have pitched their tents around her. Each will pasture those under his charge.

6:4  LA: Sanctificate super eam bellum : consurgite, et ascendamus in meridie : væ nobis, quia declinavit dies ; quia longiores factæ sunt umbræ vesperi !
     EN: Consecrate war against her! Rise, and let us go up at noon. Woe to us, for the day has declined and the evening shadows have grown longer!

6:5  LA: Surgite, et ascendamus in nocte, et dissipemus domus ejus.
     EN: Rise, and let us go up by night and destroy her houses.

6:6  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Cædite lignum ejus, et fundite circa Jerusalem aggerem. Hæc est civitas visitationis : omnis calumnia in medio ejus.
     EN: For thus says the Lord of hosts: Cut down her trees and heap up a ramp around Jerusalem. This is the city to be punished; oppression is everywhere within her.

6:7  LA: Sicut frigidam fecit cisterna aquam suam, sic frigidam fecit malitiam suam. Iniquitas et vastitas audietur in ea, coram me semper infirmitas et plaga.
     EN: As a cistern keeps its water cool, so she keeps her wickedness cool. Injustice and devastation will be heard within her; sickness and wounds are continually before Me.

6:8  LA: Erudire, Jerusalem, ne forte recedat anima mea a te ; ne forte ponam te desertam, terram inhabitabilem.
     EN: Accept instruction, Jerusalem, lest My soul withdraw from you, lest I make you desolate, an uninhabitable land.

6:9  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Usque ad racemum colligent quasi in vinea reliquias Israël. Converte manum tuam quasi vindemiator ad cartallum.
     EN: Thus says the Lord of hosts: They will gather Israel's remnant like a vine, down to its last cluster. Bring back your hand like a grape gatherer to the basket.

6:10  LA: Cui loquar, et quem contestabor ut audiat ? ecce incircumcisæ aures eorum, et audire non possunt : ecce verbum Domini factum est eis in opprobrium, et non suscipient illud.
      EN: To whom shall I speak, and whom shall I warn so that they listen? Look, their ears are uncircumcised and they cannot hear. Look, the word of the Lord has become an object of scorn to them, and they will not receive it.

6:11  LA: Idcirco furore Domini plenus sum ; laboravi sustinens. Effunde super parvulum foris, et super consilium juvenum simul : vir enim cum muliere capietur ; senex cum pleno dierum.
      EN: Therefore I am full of the Lord's fury; I am weary of holding it in. Pour it out on the little child outside and on the gathering of young men together. For husband and wife will be taken, the old man and the one full of days.

6:12  LA: Et transibunt domus eorum ad alteros, agri et uxores pariter, quia extendam manum meam super habitantes terram, dicit Dominus :
      EN: Their houses will pass to others, their fields and wives together, for I will stretch out My hand against the inhabitants of the land, says the Lord.

6:13  LA: a minore quippe usque ad majorem omnes avaritiæ student, et a propheta usque ad sacerdotem cuncti faciunt dolum.
      EN: For from the least to the greatest, all pursue greed; from prophet to priest, all practice deceit.

6:14  LA: Et curabant contritionem filiæ populi mei cum ignominia, dicentes : Pax, pax ! et non erat pax.
      EN: They treated the wound of the daughter of My people shamefully, saying, ‘Peace, peace!’ when there was no peace.

6:15  LA: Confusi sunt, quia abominationem fecerunt : quin potius confusione non sunt confusi, et erubescere nescierunt. Quam ob rem cadent inter ruentes : in tempore visitationis suæ corruent, dicit Dominus.
      EN: They were put to shame because they committed abomination; yet they felt no shame at all and did not know how to blush. Therefore they will fall among the fallen; at the time of their punishment they will collapse, says the Lord.

6:16  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : State super vias, et videte, et interrogate de semitis antiquis quæ sit via bona, et ambulate in ea : et invenietis refrigerium animabus vestris. Et dixerunt : Non ambulabimus.
      EN: Thus says the Lord: Stand at the roads and look; ask about the ancient paths, which is the good way, and walk in it, and you will find rest for your souls. But they said, ‘We will not walk in it.’

6:17  LA: Et constitui super vos speculatores : Audite vocem tubæ. Et dixerunt : Non audiemus.
      EN: I appointed watchmen over you: ‘Listen to the sound of the trumpet.’ But they said, ‘We will not listen.’

6:18  LA: Ideo audite, gentes, et cognosce, congregatio, quanta ego faciam eis.
      EN: Therefore hear, nations, and learn, assembly, how much I will do to them.

6:19  LA: Audi, terra : ecce ego adducam mala super populum istum, fructum cogitationum ejus : quia verba mea non audierunt, et legem meam projecerunt.
      EN: Hear, earth! Look, I will bring disasters upon this people, the fruit of their thoughts, because they have not listened to My words and have rejected My law.

6:20  LA: Ut quid mihi thus de Saba affertis, et calamum suave olentem de terra longinqua ? Holocautomata vestra non sunt accepta, et victimæ vestræ non placuerunt mihi.
      EN: Why do you bring Me frankincense from Sheba and sweet-smelling cane from a distant land? Your burnt offerings are not acceptable, and your sacrifices have not pleased Me.

6:21  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego dabo in populum istum ruinas : et ruent in eis patres et filii simul ; vicinus et proximus peribunt.
      EN: Therefore thus says the Lord: Look, I will set stumbling blocks before this people; fathers and sons together will stumble over them, and neighbor and friend will perish.

6:22  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce populus venit de terra aquilonis, et gens magna consurget a finibus terræ.
      EN: Thus says the Lord: Look, a people is coming from the land of the north, and a great nation will rise from the ends of the earth.

6:23  LA: Sagittam et scutum arripiet : crudelis est et non miserebitur. Vox ejus quasi mare sonabit : et super equos ascendent, præparati quasi vir ad prælium adversum te, filia Sion.
      EN: It will seize arrow and shield; it is cruel and will show no mercy. Its voice will roar like the sea. They will mount horses, prepared as one man for battle against you, daughter Zion.

6:24  LA: Audivimus famam ejus ; dissolutæ sunt manus nostræ : tribulatio apprehendit nos, dolores ut parturientem.
      EN: We have heard its report; our hands have gone limp. Distress has seized us, pains like those of a woman in labor.

6:25  LA: Nolite exire ad agros, et in via ne ambuletis, quoniam gladius inimici, pavor in circuitu.
      EN: Do not go out into the fields or walk on the road, for there is the enemy's sword, terror all around.

6:26  LA: Filia populi mei, accingere cilicio, et conspergere cinere : luctum unigeniti fac tibi, planctum amarum, quia repente veniet vastator super nos.
      EN: Daughter of My people, clothe yourself in sackcloth and sprinkle yourself with ashes. Mourn as for an only child, with bitter lamentation, for suddenly the destroyer will come upon us.

6:27  LA: Probatorem dedi te in populo meo robustum : et scies, et probabis viam eorum.
      EN: I have made you a strong assayer among My people; you will know and test their way.

6:28  LA: Omnes isti principes declinantes, ambulantes fraudulenter, æs et ferrum : universi corrupti sunt.
      EN: All these are rulers who turn aside and walk deceitfully; they are bronze and iron, all of them corrupt.

6:29  LA: Defecit sufflatorium ; in igne consumptum est plumbum : frustra conflavit conflator, malitiæ enim eorum non sunt consumptæ.
      EN: The bellows have failed; the lead has been consumed in the fire. The refiner has refined in vain, for their wickedness has not been consumed.

6:30  LA: Argentum reprobum vocate eos, quia Dominus projecit illos.]
      EN: Call them rejected silver, for the Lord has rejected them.”

JEREMIAH 7

7:2  LA: Sta in porta domus Domini, et prædica ibi verbum istud, et dic : [Audite verbum Domini, omnis Juda, qui ingredimini per portas has ut adoretis Dominum.
     EN: “Stand at the gate of the Lord's house, proclaim this word there, and say: Hear the word of the Lord, all Judah, you who enter through these gates to worship the Lord.

7:3  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Bonas facite vias vestras, et studia vestra, et habitabo vobiscum in loco isto.
     EN: Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Make your ways and your deeds good, and I will dwell with you in this place.

7:4  LA: Nolite confidere in verbis mendacii, dicentes : Templum Domini, templum Domini, templum Domini est !
     EN: Do not trust in lying words, saying, ‘The temple of the Lord, the temple of the Lord, the temple of the Lord is here!’

7:5  LA: Quoniam si bene direxeritis vias vestras, et studia vestra ; si feceritis judicium inter virum et proximum ejus ;
     EN: For if you truly set your ways and deeds right, if you render justice between a man and his neighbor,

7:6  LA: advenæ, et pupillo, et viduæ non feceritis calumniam, nec sanguinem innocentem effuderitis in loco hoc, et post deos alienos non ambulaveritis in malum vobismetipsis :
     EN: if you do not oppress the stranger, the orphan, and the widow, do not shed innocent blood in this place, and do not follow foreign gods to your own harm,

7:7  LA: habitabo vobiscum in loco isto, in terra quam dedi patribus vestris a sæculo et usque in sæculum.
     EN: I will dwell with you in this place, in the land that I gave your fathers from ages past and forever.

7:8  LA: Ecce vos confiditis vobis in sermonibus mendacii, qui non proderunt vobis :
     EN: Look, you trust in lying words that will not help you:

7:9  LA: furari, occidere, adulterari, jurare mendaciter, libare Baalim, et ire post deos alienos quos ignoratis :
     EN: stealing, killing, committing adultery, swearing falsely, pouring out offerings to the Baals, and following foreign gods whom you do not know.

7:10  LA: et venistis, et stetistis coram me in domo hac, in qua invocatum est nomen meum, et dixistis : Liberati sumus, eo quod fecerimus omnes abominationes istas.
      EN: Then you came and stood before Me in this house upon which My name has been invoked, and said, ‘We have been delivered because we have done all these abominations.’

7:11  LA: Numquid ergo spelunca latronum facta est domus ista, in qua invocatum est nomen meum in oculis vestris ? Ego, ego sum : ego vidi, dicit Dominus.
      EN: Has this house upon which My name has been invoked therefore become a den of robbers in your eyes? I, I am He; I have seen it, says the Lord.

7:12  LA: Ite ad locum meum in Silo, ubi habitavit nomen meum a principio, et videte quæ fecerim ei propter malitiam populi mei Israël.
      EN: Go to My place at Shiloh, where My name dwelt at first, and see what I did to it because of the wickedness of My people Israel.

7:13  LA: Et nunc, quia fecistis omnia opera hæc, dicit Dominus, et locutus sum ad vos mane consurgens, et loquens, et non audistis : et vocavi vos, et non respondistis :
      EN: And now, because you have done all these deeds, says the Lord, and I have spoken to you, rising early and speaking, but you have not listened; I have called you, but you have not answered:

7:14  LA: faciam domui huic, in qua invocatum est nomen meum, et in qua vos habetis fiduciam, et loco quem dedi vobis et patribus vestris, sicut feci Silo :
      EN: I will do to this house upon which My name has been invoked, in which you trust, and to the place that I gave you and your fathers, just as I did to Shiloh.

7:15  LA: et projiciam vos a facie mea sicut projeci omnes fratres vestros, universum semen Ephraim.
      EN: I will cast you out of My sight, as I cast out all your brothers, all the offspring of Ephraim.

7:16  LA: Tu ergo, noli orare pro populo hoc, nec assumas pro eis laudem et orationem : et non obsistas mihi, quia non exaudiam te.
      EN: So do not pray for this people or raise praise and prayer for them; do not stand against Me, for I will not hear you.

7:17  LA: Nonne vides quid isti faciunt in civitatibus Juda, et in plateis Jerusalem ?
      EN: Do you not see what they are doing in the cities of Judah and in Jerusalem's squares?

7:18  LA: Filii colligunt ligna, et patres succendunt ignem, et mulieres conspergunt adipem, ut faciant placentas reginæ cæli, et libent diis alienis, et me ad iracundiam provocent.
      EN: The children gather wood, the fathers kindle fire, and the women knead the rich dough to make cakes for the queen of heaven, to pour out offerings to foreign gods and provoke Me to anger.

7:19  LA: Numquid me ad iracundiam provocant ? dicit Dominus ; nonne semetipsos in confusionem vultus sui ?
      EN: Is it Me they provoke to anger, says the Lord, or rather themselves, to their own shame?

7:20  LA: Ideo hæc dicit Dominus Deus : Ecce furor meus et indignatio mea conflatur super locum istum, super viros, et super jumenta, et super lignum regionis, et super fruges terræ : et succendetur, et non extinguetur.
      EN: Therefore thus says the Lord God: Look, My fury and indignation are being kindled against this place, against men and animals, against the trees of the region and the crops of the land. It will burn and not be quenched.

7:21  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Holocautomata vestra addite victimis vestris, et comedite carnes :
      EN: Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Add your burnt offerings to your sacrifices, and eat the meat.

7:22  LA: quia non sum locutus cum patribus vestris, et non præcepi eis, in die qua eduxi eos de terra Ægypti, de verbo holocautomatum et victimarum :
      EN: For when I brought your fathers out of the land of Egypt, I did not speak to them or command them concerning burnt offerings and sacrifices.

7:23  LA: sed hoc verbum præcepi eis, dicens : Audite vocem meam, et ero vobis Deus, et vos eritis mihi populus : et ambulate in omni via quam mandavi vobis, ut bene sit vobis.
      EN: But this word I commanded them: Listen to My voice, and I will be your God and you will be My people. Walk in every way that I have commanded you, so that it may go well with you.

7:24  LA: Et non audierunt, nec inclinaverunt aurem suam : sed abierunt in voluntatibus et in pravitate cordis sui mali : factique sunt retrorsum, et non in ante,
      EN: But they did not listen or incline their ears; they followed their own desires and the perversity of their evil hearts. They went backward and not forward,

7:25  LA: a die qua egressi sunt patres eorum de terra Ægypti usque ad diem hanc. Et misi ad vos omnes servos meos prophetas per diem, consurgens diluculo, et mittens :
      EN: from the day their fathers left the land of Egypt until this day. I sent you all My servants the prophets day by day, rising at dawn and sending them.

7:26  LA: et non audierunt me, nec inclinaverunt aurem suam : sed induraverunt cervicem suam, et pejus operati sunt quam patres eorum.]
      EN: But they did not listen to Me or incline their ears; they stiffened their necks and acted worse than their fathers.”

7:27  LA: Et loqueris ad eos omnia verba hæc, et non audient te : et vocabis eos, et non respondebunt tibi.
      EN: You shall speak all these words to them, but they will not listen to you; you shall call them, but they will not answer you.

7:28  LA: Et loqueris ad eos omnia verba hæc, et non audient te : et vocabis eos, et non respondebunt tibi.
      EN: You shall speak all these words to them, but they will not listen to you; you shall call them, but they will not answer you.

7:29  LA: Tonde capillum tuum, et projice, et sume in directum planctum : quia projecit Dominus et reliquit generationem furoris sui ;
      EN: Cut off your hair and throw it away; raise a lament straight before you, for the Lord has rejected and abandoned the generation of His fury.

7:30  LA: quia fecerunt filii Juda malum in oculis meis, dicit Dominus. Posuerunt offendicula sua in domo in qua invocatum est nomen meum, ut polluerent eam :
      EN: For the children of Judah have done evil in My eyes, says the Lord. They have placed their abominations in the house upon which My name has been invoked, to defile it.

7:31  LA: et ædificaverunt excelsa Topheth, quæ est in valle filii Ennom, ut incenderent filios suos et filias suas igni, quæ non præcepi, nec cogitavi in corde meo.
      EN: They have built the high places of Topheth, which is in the Valley of the Son of Hinnom, to burn their sons and daughters in fire, things that I did not command or conceive in My heart.

7:32  LA: Ideo ecce dies venient, dicit Dominus, et non dicetur amplius Topheth, et vallis filii Ennom, sed vallis interfectionis : et sepelient in Topheth, eo quod non sit locus.
      EN: Therefore, look, days will come, says the Lord, when it will no longer be called Topheth or the Valley of the Son of Hinnom, but the Valley of Slaughter. They will bury in Topheth because there is no room elsewhere.

7:33  LA: Et erit morticinum populi hujus in cibos volucribus cæli et bestiis terræ, et non erit qui abigat.
      EN: The corpses of this people will be food for the birds of heaven and the beasts of the earth, with no one to drive them away.

7:34  LA: Et quiescere faciam de urbibus Juda, et de plateis Jerusalem, vocem gaudii et vocem lætitiæ, vocem sponsi et vocem sponsæ : in desolationem enim erit terra.]
      EN: I will silence in Judah's cities and Jerusalem's squares the voice of joy and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom and the voice of the bride, for the land will become desolate.”

JEREMIAH 8

8:1  LA: In illo tempore, ait Dominus, ejicient ossa regum Juda, et ossa principum ejus, et ossa sacerdotum, et ossa prophetarum, et ossa eorum qui habitaverunt Jerusalem, de sepulchris suis :
     EN: “At that time, says the Lord, they will cast out of their tombs the bones of Judah's kings, the bones of its rulers, the bones of its priests, the bones of its prophets, and the bones of those who lived in Jerusalem.

8:2  LA: et expandent ea ad solem, et lunam, et omnem militiam cæli, quæ dilexerunt, et quibus servierunt, et post quæ ambulaverunt, et quæ quæsierunt, et adoraverunt. Non colligentur, et non sepelientur : in sterquilinium super faciem terræ erunt.
     EN: They will spread them before the sun, the moon, and all the host of heaven, which they loved and served, followed, sought, and worshiped. They will not be gathered or buried; they will be dung upon the face of the earth.

8:3  LA: Et eligent magis mortem quam vitam, omnes qui residui fuerint de cognatione hac pessima, in universis locis quæ derelicta sunt, ad quæ ejeci eos, dicit Dominus exercituum.]
     EN: All who remain of this most wicked clan, in all the forsaken places to which I have driven them, will choose death rather than life, says the Lord of hosts.”

8:4  LA: Et dices ad eos : [Hæc dicit Dominus : Numquid qui cadit non resurget ? et qui aversus est non revertetur ?
     EN: You shall say to them, “Thus says the Lord: Does someone who falls not rise again? Does someone who turns away not return?

8:5  LA: Quare ergo aversus est populus iste in Jerusalem aversione contentiosa ? Apprehenderunt mendacium, et noluerunt reverti.
     EN: Why, then, has this people in Jerusalem turned away with such stubborn apostasy? They have grasped falsehood and refused to return.

8:6  LA: Attendi, et auscultavi : nemo quod bonum est loquitur ; nullus est qui agat pœnitentiam super peccato suo, dicens : Quid feci ? Omnes conversi sunt ad cursum suum, quasi equus impetu vadens ad prælium.
     EN: I have paid attention and listened: no one speaks what is good; no one repents of his sin, saying, ‘What have I done?’ All have turned to their own course, like a horse charging into battle.

8:7  LA: Milvus in cælo cognovit tempus suum : turtur, et hirundo, et ciconia custodierunt tempus adventus sui : populus autem meus non cognovit judicium Domini.
     EN: The kite in the sky knows its season; the turtledove, swallow, and stork keep the time of their arrival. But My people do not know the judgment of the Lord.

8:8  LA: Quomodo dicitis : Sapientes nos sumus, et lex Domini nobiscum est ? vere mendacium operatus est stylus mendax scribarum !
     EN: How can you say, ‘We are wise, and the law of the Lord is with us’? Truly the lying pen of the scribes has worked falsehood!

8:9  LA: Confusi sunt sapientes ; perterriti et capti sunt : verbum enim Domini projecerunt, et sapientia nulla est in eis.
     EN: The wise have been put to shame; they are terrified and caught. For they have rejected the word of the Lord, and there is no wisdom in them.

8:10  LA: Propterea dabo mulieres eorum exteris, agros eorum hæredibus, quia a minimo usque ad maximum omnes avaritiam sequuntur : a propheta usque ad sacerdotem cuncti faciunt mendacium.
      EN: Therefore I will give their wives to foreigners and their fields to new heirs, for from the least to the greatest all pursue greed; from prophet to priest, all practice falsehood.

8:13  LA: Congregans congregabo eos, ait Dominus. Non est uva in vitibus, et non sunt ficus in ficulnea : folium defluxit, et dedi eis quæ prætergressa sunt.
      EN: I will surely gather them, says the Lord. There are no grapes on the vines or figs on the fig tree; the leaf has fallen, and I have given them things that have passed away.

8:14  LA: Quare sedemus ? convenite, et ingrediamur civitatem munitam, et sileamus ibi : quia Dominus Deus noster silere nos fecit, et potum dedit nobis aquam fellis : peccavimus enim Domino.
      EN: Why are we sitting here? Assemble, and let us enter the fortified city and be silent there, for the Lord our God has silenced us and given us bitter water to drink, because we have sinned against the Lord.

8:15  LA: Exspectavimus pacem, et non erat bonum : tempus medelæ, et ecce formido.
      EN: We waited for peace, but there was no good; for a time of healing, and look, terror!

8:16  LA: A Dan auditus est fremitus equorum ejus ; a voce hinnituum pugnatorum ejus commota est omnis terra : et venerunt, et devoraverunt terram et plenitudinem ejus ; urbem et habitatores ejus.
      EN: From Dan the snorting of his horses has been heard. At the sound of his warhorses' neighing, the whole land has trembled. They have come and devoured the land and all that fills it, the city and its inhabitants.

8:17  LA: Quia ecce ego mittam vobis serpentes regulos, quibus non est incantatio : et mordebunt vos, ait Dominus.
      EN: For look, I will send you venomous snakes for which there is no charm, and they will bite you, says the Lord.

8:18  LA: Dolor meus super dolorem, in me cor meum mœrens.
      EN: My sorrow is upon sorrow; my heart mourns within me.

8:19  LA: Ecce vox clamoris filiæ populi mei de terra longinqua : Numquid Dominus non est in Sion ? aut rex ejus non est in ea ? Quare ergo me ad iracundiam concitaverunt in sculptilibus suis, et in vanitatibus alienis ?
      EN: Listen, the cry of the daughter of my people from a distant land: ‘Is the Lord not in Zion? Is her King not within her?’ Why, then, have they provoked Me to anger with their carved images and foreign vanities?

8:20  LA: Transiit messis, finita est æstas, et nos salvati non sumus.
      EN: The harvest has passed, the summer is ended, and we have not been saved.

8:21  LA: Super contritione filiæ populi mei contritus sum, et contristatus : stupor obtinuit me.
      EN: At the breaking of the daughter of my people I am broken and grieved; horror has seized me.

8:22  LA: Numquid resina non est in Galaad ? aut medicus non est ibi ? quare igitur non est obducta cicatrix filiæ populi mei ?]
      EN: Is there no balm in Gilead? Is there no physician there? Why, then, has the wound of the daughter of my people not closed over?”

8:23  LA: Quis dabit capiti meo aquam, et oculis meis fontem lacrimarum, et plorabo die ac nocte interfectos filiæ populi mei ?
      EN: Who will give my head water and my eyes a fountain of tears, that I may weep day and night for the slain of the daughter of my people?

JEREMIAH 9

9:1  LA: Quis dabit me in solitudine diversorium viatorum, et derelinquam populum meum, et recedam ab eis ? quia omnes adulteri sunt, cœtus prævaricatorum.
     EN: Who will give me a travelers' lodging in the wilderness, that I may abandon my people and go away from them? For they are all adulterers, a company of traitors.

9:2  LA: Et extenderunt linguam suam quasi arcum mendacii et non veritatis : confortati sunt in terra, quia de malo ad malum egressi sunt, et me non cognoverunt, dicit Dominus.
     EN: “They have drawn their tongue like a bow for falsehood, not for truth. They have grown strong in the land, for they have gone from evil to evil and have not known Me, says the Lord.

9:3  LA: Unusquisque se a proximo suo custodiat, et in omni fratre suo non habeat fiduciam : quia omnis frater supplantans supplantabit, et omnis amicus fraudulenter incedet.
     EN: Let everyone guard himself against his neighbor and trust no brother, for every brother will surely supplant, and every friend will act deceitfully.

9:4  LA: Et vir fratrem suum deridebit, et veritatem non loquentur : docuerunt enim linguam suam loqui mendacium ; ut inique agerent laboraverunt.
     EN: Each man will mock his brother, and they will not speak the truth. They have taught their tongues to tell lies; they have labored to act unjustly.

9:5  LA: Habitatio tua in medio doli : in dolo renuerunt scire me, dicit Dominus.
     EN: Your dwelling is in the midst of deceit; in deceit they have refused to know Me, says the Lord.

9:6  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Ecce ego conflabo, et probabo eos : quid enim aliud faciam a facie filiæ populi mei ?
     EN: Therefore thus says the Lord of hosts: Look, I will refine and test them; for what else can I do because of the daughter of My people?

9:7  LA: Sagitta vulnerans lingua eorum, dolum locuta est. In ore suo pacem cum amico suo loquitur, et occulte ponit ei insidias.
     EN: Their tongue is a wounding arrow; it has spoken deceit. With his mouth a man speaks peace to his friend, while secretly laying an ambush for him.

9:8  LA: Numquid super his non visitabo, dicit Dominus, aut in gente hujusmodi non ulciscetur anima mea ?
     EN: Shall I not punish these things, says the Lord, or shall My soul not take vengeance on a nation like this?

9:9  LA: Super montes assumam fletum ac lamentum, et super speciosa deserti planctum, quoniam incensa sunt, eo quod non sit vir pertransiens, et non audierunt vocem possidentis : a volucre cæli usque ad pecora transmigraverunt et recesserunt.
     EN: Over the mountains I will take up weeping and lamentation, and a dirge over the beautiful places of the wilderness, because they are burned, with no one passing through, and they have not heard the owner's voice. From the birds of heaven to the cattle, they have migrated and gone away.

9:10  LA: Et dabo Jerusalem in acervos arenæ, et cubilia draconum : et civitates Juda dabo in desolationem, eo quod non sit habitator.
      EN: I will make Jerusalem heaps of sand and lairs of dragons; I will make Judah's cities desolate, without an inhabitant.

9:11  LA: Quis est vir sapiens qui intelligat hoc, et ad quem verbum oris Domini fiat, ut annuntiet istud, quare perierit terra, et exusta sit quasi desertum, eo quod non sit qui pertranseat ?
      EN: Who is the wise man who understands this, to whom the word from the Lord's mouth comes, that he may declare why the land has perished and been burned like a wilderness, with no one passing through?”

9:12  LA: Et dixit Dominus : Quia dereliquerunt legem meam quam dedi eis, et non audierunt vocem meam, et non ambulaverunt in ea,
      EN: The Lord said, “Because they have abandoned My law which I gave them, have not listened to My voice, and have not walked in it,

9:13  LA: et abierunt post pravitatem cordis sui, et post Baalim, quod didicerunt a patribus suis :
      EN: but followed the perversity of their hearts and the Baals, as they learned from their fathers,

9:14  LA: idcirco hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Ecce ego cibabo populum istum absinthio, et potum dabo eis aquam fellis.
      EN: therefore thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Look, I will feed this people wormwood and give them bitter water to drink.

9:15  LA: Et dispergam eos in gentibus quas non noverunt ipsi et patres eorum, et mittam post eos gladium, donec consumantur.
      EN: I will scatter them among nations that neither they nor their fathers have known, and send the sword after them until they are consumed.

9:16  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Contemplamini, et vocate lamentatrices, et veniant : et ad eas quæ sapientes sunt mittite, et properent :
      EN: Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Consider, and summon the mourning women; let them come. Send for the skilled women, and let them hurry.

9:17  LA: festinent, et assumant super nos lamentum : deducant oculi nostri lacrimas, et palpebræ nostræ defluant aquis.
      EN: Let them hasten and raise a lament over us; let our eyes shed tears and our eyelids stream with water.

9:18  LA: Quia vox lamentationis audita est de Sion : Quomodo vastati sumus, et confusi vehementer ? quia dereliquimus terram ; quoniam dejecta sunt tabernacula nostra.
      EN: For a voice of lamentation is heard from Zion: ‘How we have been devastated and utterly put to shame! For we have left the land, because our dwellings have been thrown down.’

9:19  LA: Audite ergo, mulieres, verbum Domini, et assumant aures vestræ sermonem oris ejus, et docete filias vestras lamentum, et unaquæque proximam suam planctum :
      EN: So hear the word of the Lord, women, and let your ears receive the word of His mouth. Teach your daughters a lament, each her neighbor a dirge,

9:20  LA: quia ascendit mors per fenestras nostras ; ingressa est domos nostras, disperdere parvulos deforis, juvenes de plateis.]
      EN: for death has climbed through our windows and entered our houses, to destroy the little children outside and the young men in the squares.”

9:21  LA: Loquere : [Hæc dicit Dominus : Et cadet morticinum hominis quasi stercus super faciem regionis, et quasi fœnum post tergum metentis, et non est qui colligat.
      EN: Speak: “Thus says the Lord: Human corpses will fall like dung upon the face of the countryside, like cut grass behind the reaper, with no one to gather it.

9:22  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Non glorietur sapiens in sapientia sua, et non glorietur fortis in fortitudine sua, et non glorietur dives in divitiis suis :
      EN: Thus says the Lord: Let the wise man not boast of his wisdom, the strong man not boast of his strength, and the rich man not boast of his riches.

9:23  LA: sed in hoc glorietur, qui gloriatur, scire et nosse me, quia ego sum Dominus qui facio misericordiam, et judicium, et justitiam in terra : hæc enim placent mihi, ait Dominus.
      EN: Rather, let whoever boasts boast of this: understanding and knowing Me, that I am the Lord who practices mercy, judgment, and justice on earth; for these things please Me, says the Lord.

9:24  LA: Ecce dies veniunt, dicit Dominus, et visitabo super omnem qui circumcisum habet præputium,
      EN: Look, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will punish everyone whose foreskin is circumcised:

9:25  LA: super Ægyptum, et super Juda, et super Edom, et super filios Ammon, et super Moab ; et super omnes qui attonsi sunt in comam, habitantes in deserto : quia omnes gentes habent præputium, omnis autem domus Israël incircumcisi sunt corde.]
      EN: Egypt, Judah, Edom, the children of Ammon, Moab, and all who have their hair cropped and live in the wilderness. For all nations have the foreskin, but the whole house of Israel is uncircumcised in heart.”

JEREMIAH 10

10:1  LA: Audite verbum quod locutus est Dominus super vos, domus Israël.
      EN: Hear the word that the Lord has spoken concerning you, house of Israel.

10:2  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Juxta vias gentium nolite discere, et a signis cæli nolite metuere, quæ timent gentes,
      EN: Thus says the Lord: “Do not learn the ways of the nations, and do not fear the signs of heaven that the nations fear,

10:3  LA: quia leges populorum vanæ sunt. Quia lignum de saltu præcidit opus manus artificis in ascia :
      EN: for the customs of the peoples are empty. A tree is cut from the forest, the work of a craftsman's hands with an axe;

10:4  LA: argento et auro decoravit illud : clavis et malleis compegit, ut non dissolvatur :
      EN: he adorns it with silver and gold and fastens it with nails and hammers so that it will not fall apart.

10:5  LA: in similitudinem palmæ fabricata sunt, et non loquentur : portata tollentur, quia incedere non valent. Nolite ergo timere ea, quia nec male possunt facere, nec bene.
      EN: They are fashioned in the likeness of a palm tree and cannot speak. They have to be carried, because they cannot walk. So do not fear them, for they can do neither evil nor good.”

10:9  LA: Argentum involutum de Tharsis affertur, et aurum de Ophaz : opus artificis et manus ærarii. Hyacinthus et purpura indumentum eorum : opus artificum universa hæc.
      EN: Beaten silver is brought from Tarshish and gold from Uphaz, the work of a craftsman and a metalworker's hands. Blue and purple are their clothing; all these are the work of craftsmen.

10:11  LA: Sic ergo dicetis eis : Dii qui cælos et terram non fecerunt, pereant de terra et de his quæ sub cælo sunt !
       EN: Thus you shall say to them: “Let the gods who did not make heaven and earth perish from the earth and from beneath heaven!”

10:12  LA: Qui facit terram in fortitudine sua, præparat orbem in sapientia sua, et prudentia sua extendit cælos :
       EN: He makes the earth by His strength, establishes the world by His wisdom, and stretches out the heavens by His understanding.

10:13  LA: ad vocem suam dat multitudinem aquarum in cælo, et elevat nebulas ab extremitatibus terræ : fulgura in pluviam facit, et educit ventum de thesauris suis.
       EN: At His voice He gives a multitude of waters in heaven and raises clouds from the ends of the earth; He makes lightning for the rain and brings the wind out of His storehouses.

10:14  LA: Stultus factus est omnis homo a scientia : confusus est artifex omnis in sculptili, quoniam falsum est quod conflavit, et non est spiritus in eis.
       EN: Every man has become foolish in his knowledge; every craftsman is put to shame by his carved image, for what he has cast is false, and there is no breath in them.

10:15  LA: Vana sunt, et opus risu dignum : in tempore visitationis suæ peribunt.
       EN: They are empty, a work deserving laughter; at the time of their punishment they will perish.

10:16  LA: Non est his similis pars Jacob : qui enim formavit omnia, ipse est, et Israël virga hæreditatis ejus : Dominus exercituum nomen illi.
       EN: Jacob's portion is not like these, for He is the One who formed all things, and Israel is the tribe of His inheritance. The Lord of hosts is His name.

10:17  LA: Congrega de terra confusionem tuam, quæ habitas in obsidione :
       EN: “Gather your disgrace from the ground, you who dwell under siege,

10:18  LA: quia hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego longe projiciam habitatores terræ in hac vice, et tribulabo eos ita ut inveniantur.
       EN: for thus says the Lord: Look, this time I will fling the inhabitants of the land far away and distress them so that they are found.”

10:19  LA: Væ mihi super contritione mea : pessima plaga mea. Ego autem dixi : Plane hæc infirmitas mea est, et portabo illam.
       EN: Woe to me for my brokenness! My wound is grievous. But I said, “Surely this is my affliction, and I will bear it.”

10:20  LA: Tabernaculum meum vastatum est ; omnes funiculi mei dirupti sunt : filii mei exierunt a me, et non subsistunt. Non est qui extendat ultra tentorium meum, et erigat pelles meas.
       EN: My tent has been devastated; all my ropes have snapped. My children have gone away from me and are no more. There is no one left to stretch out my tent or erect my tent cloths.

10:21  LA: Quia stulte egerunt pastores, et Dominum non quæsierunt : propterea non intellexerunt, et omnis grex eorum dispersus est.
       EN: For the shepherds acted foolishly and did not seek the Lord; therefore they did not understand, and all their flock has been scattered.

10:22  LA: Vox auditionis ecce venit, et commotio magna de terra aquilonis : ut ponat civitates Juda solitudinem, et habitaculum draconum.
       EN: Listen, a report is coming, and a great upheaval from the northern land, to make Judah's cities a wilderness, a dwelling for dragons.

10:23  LA: Scio, Domine, quia non est hominis via ejus, nec viri est ut ambulet, et dirigat gressus suos.
       EN: I know, Lord, that a man's way is not his own, nor is it in a man to walk and direct his own steps.

10:24  LA: Corripe me, Domine, verumtamen in judicio, et non in furore tuo, ne forte ad nihilum redigas me.
       EN: Correct me, Lord, but with judgment, not in Your fury, lest You reduce me to nothing.
