10:25  LA: Effunde indignationem tuam super gentes quæ non cognoverunt te, et super provincias quæ nomen tuum non invocaverunt : quia comederunt Jacob, et devoraverunt eum, et consumpserunt illum, et decus ejus dissipaverunt.]
       EN: Pour out Your indignation on the nations that have not known You, and on the provinces that have not invoked Your name, for they have eaten Jacob, devoured him, consumed him, and laid waste his splendor.

JEREMIAH 11

11:1  LA: Verbum quod factum est a Domino ad Jeremiam, dicens :
      EN: The word that came from the Lord to Jeremiah, saying:

11:2  LA: [Audite verba pacti hujus, et loquimini ad viros Juda, et ad habitatores Jerusalem,
      EN: “Hear the words of this covenant and speak to the men of Judah and the inhabitants of Jerusalem.

11:3  LA: et dices ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus Israël : Maledictus vir qui non audierit verba pacti hujus
      EN: You shall say to them: Thus says the Lord, the God of Israel: Cursed is the man who does not hear the words of this covenant,

11:4  LA: quod præcepi patribus vestris, in die qua eduxi eos de terra Ægypti, de fornace ferrea, dicens : Audite vocem meam, et facite omnia quæ præcipio vobis, et eritis mihi in populum, et ego ero vobis in Deum :
      EN: which I commanded your fathers on the day I brought them out of the land of Egypt, out of the iron furnace, saying, ‘Listen to My voice and do everything I command you, and you will be My people and I will be your God,

11:5  LA: ut suscitem juramentum quod juravi patribus vestris, daturum me eis terram fluentem lacte et melle, sicut est dies hæc.] Et respondi, et dixi : Amen, Domine.
      EN: so that I may fulfill the oath I swore to your fathers, to give them a land flowing with milk and honey, as it is this day.’” And I answered, “Amen, Lord.”

11:6  LA: Et dixit Dominus ad me : [Vociferare omnia verba hæc in civitatibus Juda, et foris Jerusalem, dicens : Audite verba pacti hujus, et facite illa,
      EN: The Lord said to me, “Proclaim all these words in Judah's cities and outside Jerusalem, saying: Hear the words of this covenant and carry them out.

11:8  LA: Et non audierunt, nec inclinaverunt aurem suam, sed abierunt, unusquisque in pravitate cordis sui mali : et induxi super eos omnia verba pacti hujus quod præcepi ut facerent, et non fecerunt.]
      EN: But they did not listen or incline their ears; each followed the perversity of his evil heart. So I brought upon them all the words of this covenant, which I commanded them to keep, but they did not keep.”

11:9  LA: Et dixit Dominus ad me : [Inventa est conjuratio in viris Juda et in habitatoribus Jerusalem.
      EN: The Lord said to me, “A conspiracy has been found among the men of Judah and the inhabitants of Jerusalem.

11:10  LA: Reversi sunt ad iniquitates patrum suorum priores, qui noluerunt audire verba mea : et hi ergo abierunt post deos alienos, ut servirent eis : irritum fecerunt domus Israël et domus Juda pactum meum quod pepigi cum patribus eorum.
       EN: They have returned to the former sins of their fathers who would not hear My words; these too have followed foreign gods to serve them. The house of Israel and the house of Judah have broken My covenant that I made with their fathers.

11:11  LA: Quam ob rem hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego inducam super eos mala de quibus exire non poterunt : et clamabunt ad me, et non exaudiam eos.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord: Look, I will bring disasters upon them from which they cannot escape. They will cry to Me, but I will not hear them.

11:12  LA: Et ibunt civitates Juda et habitatores Jerusalem, et clamabunt ad deos quibus libant, et non salvabunt eos in tempore afflictionis eorum.
       EN: Then Judah's cities and Jerusalem's inhabitants will go and cry to the gods to whom they pour out offerings, but these will not save them in the time of their distress.

11:13  LA: Secundum numerum enim civitatum tuarum erant dii tui, Juda : et secundum numerum viarum Jerusalem, posuisti aras confusionis, aras ad libandum Baalim.
       EN: For your gods were as many as your cities, Judah; and as many as Jerusalem's streets were the altars of shame you set up, altars for pouring out offerings to the Baals.

11:14  LA: Tu ergo noli orare pro populo hoc, et ne assumas pro eis laudem et orationem, quia non exaudiam in tempore clamoris eorum ad me, in tempore afflictionis eorum.
       EN: So do not pray for this people or raise praise and prayer for them, for I will not hear when they cry to Me in the time of their distress.

11:15  LA: Quid est, quod dilectus meus in domo mea fecit scelera multa ? numquid carnes sanctæ auferent a te malitias tuas, in quibus gloriata es ?
       EN: What is this, that My beloved has committed many crimes in My house? Will consecrated meat take away the wickedness in which you have gloried?

11:16  LA: Olivam uberem, pulchram, fructiferam, speciosam, vocavit Dominus nomen tuum : ad vocem loquelæ, grandis exarsit ignis in ea, et combusta sunt fruteta ejus.
       EN: The Lord called your name ‘A flourishing olive tree, beautiful, fruitful, lovely.’ At the sound of a word, a great fire blazed in it, and its branches were burned.

11:17  LA: Et Dominus exercituum, qui plantavit te, locutus est super te malum, pro malis domus Israël, et domus Juda, quæ fecerunt sibi ad irritandum me, libantes Baalim.]
       EN: The Lord of hosts, who planted you, has pronounced disaster against you because of the evils that the house of Israel and the house of Judah have done to themselves, provoking Me by pouring out offerings to the Baals.”

11:18  LA: [Tu autem, Domine, demonstrasti mihi, et cognovi : tunc ostendisti mihi studia eorum.
       EN: But You, Lord, made it known to me, and I knew; then You showed me their designs.

11:19  LA: Et ego quasi agnus mansuetus, qui portatur ad victimam : et non cognovi quia cogitaverunt super me consilia, dicentes : Mittamus lignum in panem ejus, et eradamus eum de terra viventium, et nomen ejus non memoretur amplius.
       EN: I was like a gentle lamb being carried to sacrifice, and I did not know that they had devised schemes against me, saying, “Let us put wood into his bread and uproot him from the land of the living, so that his name is remembered no more.”

11:20  LA: Tu autem, Domine Sabaoth, qui judicas juste, et probas renes et corda, videam ultionem tuam ex eis : tibi enim revelavi causam meam.
       EN: But You, Lord of hosts, who judge justly and test the kidneys and hearts, let me see Your vengeance upon them, for I have disclosed my case to You.

11:21  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus ad viros Anathoth, qui quærunt animam tuam, et dicunt : Non prophetabis in nomine Domini, et non morieris in manibus nostris :
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord concerning the men of Anathoth who seek your life and say, “Do not prophesy in the name of the Lord, and you will not die by our hands”:

11:22  LA: propterea hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Ecce ego visitabo super eos : juvenes morientur in gladio ; filii eorum et filiæ eorum morientur in fame.
       EN: therefore thus says the Lord of hosts: “Look, I will punish them. Their young men will die by the sword; their sons and daughters will die of famine.

11:23  LA: Et reliquiæ non erunt ex eis : inducam enim malum super viros Anathoth, annum visitationis eorum.]
       EN: No remnant of them will remain, for I will bring disaster upon the men of Anathoth, the year of their punishment.”

JEREMIAH 12

12:1  LA: Justus quidem tu es, Domine, si disputem tecum : verumtamen justa loquar ad te : Quare via impiorum prosperatur ; bene est omnibus qui prævaricantur et inique agunt ?
      EN: You are indeed just, Lord, if I dispute with You; nevertheless, I will speak with You about justice. Why does the way of the wicked prosper? Why is it well with all who betray and act unjustly?

12:2  LA: Plantasti eos, et radicem miserunt : proficiunt, et faciunt fructum : prope es tu ori eorum, et longe a renibus eorum.
      EN: You planted them, and they took root; they grow and bear fruit. You are near their mouths but far from their kidneys.

12:3  LA: Et tu, Domine, nosti me, vidisti me, et probasti cor meum tecum. Congrega eos quasi gregem ad victimam, et sanctifica eos in die occisionis.
      EN: But You, Lord, know me; You have seen me and tested my heart toward You. Gather them like a flock for sacrifice, and consecrate them for the day of slaughter.

12:4  LA: Usquequo lugebit terra, et herba omnis regionis siccabitur, propter malitiam habitantium in ea ? Consumptum est animal, et volucre, quoniam dixerunt : Non videbit novissima nostra.
      EN: How long will the land mourn and the grass of every region wither because of the wickedness of those who live in it? Animal and bird have been consumed, because they said, “He will not see our final end.”

12:5  LA: Si cum peditibus currens laborasti, quomodo contendere poteris cum equis ? cum autem in terra pacis securus fueris, quid facies in superbia Jordanis ?
      EN: “If you grew weary running with footmen, how will you compete with horses? And if you have been secure in a peaceful land, what will you do in the swelling of the Jordan?

12:6  LA: Nam et fratres tui, et domus patris tui, etiam ipsi pugnaverunt adversum te, et clamaverunt post te plena voce : ne credas eis, cum locuti fuerint tibi bona.]
      EN: For even your brothers and your father's house have fought against you and cried after you with full voice. Do not believe them when they speak kindly to you.

12:7  LA: [Reliqui domum meam ; dimisi hæreditatem meam : dedi dilectam animam meam in manu inimicorum ejus.
      EN: I have abandoned My house and forsaken My inheritance; I have given My beloved soul into the hands of her enemies.

12:8  LA: Facta est mihi hæreditas mea quasi leo in silva : dedit contra me vocem, ideo odivi eam.
      EN: My inheritance has become to Me like a lion in the forest; she raised her voice against Me, and therefore I hated her.

12:9  LA: Numquid avis discolor hæreditas mea mihi ? numquid avis tincta per totum ? Venite, congregamini, omnes bestiæ terræ : properate ad devorandum.
      EN: Is My inheritance a many-colored bird to Me, a bird colored all over? Come, gather, all beasts of the earth; hurry to devour!

12:10  LA: Pastores multi demoliti sunt vineam meam, conculcaverunt partem meam, dederunt portionem meam desiderabilem in desertum solitudinis.
       EN: Many shepherds have destroyed My vineyard and trampled My portion; they have made My desirable portion a desolate wilderness.

12:11  LA: Posuerunt eam in dissipationem, luxitque super me : desolatione desolata est omnis terra, quia nullus est qui recogitet corde.
       EN: They have laid it waste, and it has mourned over Me. The whole land is utterly desolate, because no one takes it to heart.

12:12  LA: Super omnes vias deserti venerunt vastatores, quia gladius Domini devorabit : ab extremo terræ usque ad extremum ejus, non est pax universæ carni.
       EN: Destroyers have come upon all the paths of the wilderness, for the sword of the Lord will devour. From one end of the land to the other, no flesh has peace.

12:13  LA: Seminaverunt triticum, et spinas messuerunt : hæreditatem acceperunt, et non eis proderit. Confundemini a fructibus vestris propter iram furoris Domini.
       EN: They sowed wheat and reaped thorns; they received an inheritance, but it will not profit them. You will be ashamed of your produce because of the Lord's fierce anger.

12:14  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus adversum omnes vicinos meos pessimos, qui tangunt hæreditatem quam distribui populo meo Israël : Ecce ego evellam eos de terra sua, et domum Juda evellam de medio eorum.
       EN: Thus says the Lord against all My wicked neighbors who lay hands on the inheritance that I allotted to My people Israel: Look, I will uproot them from their land, and I will uproot the house of Judah from among them.

12:15  LA: Et cum evulsero eos, convertar, et miserebor eorum, et reducam eos : virum ad hæreditatem suam, et virum in terram suam.
       EN: After I have uprooted them, I will turn and have mercy on them, and bring them back, each to his inheritance and each to his land.

12:16  LA: Et erit : si eruditi didicerint vias populi mei, ut jurent in nomine meo : Vivit Dominus ! sicut docuerunt populum meum jurare in Baal, ædificabuntur in medio populi mei.
       EN: If they diligently learn the ways of My people, to swear by My name, ‘As the Lord lives,’ just as they taught My people to swear by Baal, they will be built up among My people.

12:17  LA: Quod si non audierint, evellam gentem illam evulsione et perditione, ait Dominus.]
       EN: But if they do not listen, I will utterly uproot that nation and destroy it, says the Lord.”

JEREMIAH 13

13:1  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus ad me : Vade, et posside tibi lumbare lineum, et pones illud super lumbos tuos, et in aquam non inferes illud.
      EN: Thus says the Lord to me: “Go and get yourself a linen loincloth, put it around your waist, and do not put it in water.”

13:2  LA: Et possedi lumbare juxta verbum Domini, et posui circa lumbos meos.
      EN: I obtained a loincloth according to the Lord's word and put it around my waist.

13:3  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad me secundo, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me a second time, saying:

13:4  LA: Tolle lumbare quod possedisti, quod est circa lumbos tuos : et surgens vade ad Euphraten, et absconde ibi illud in foramine petræ.
      EN: “Take the loincloth you obtained, which is around your waist, rise and go to the Euphrates, and hide it there in a cleft of the rock.”

13:5  LA: Et abii, et abscondi illud in Euphrate, sicut præceperat mihi Dominus.
      EN: So I went and hid it by the Euphrates, as the Lord had commanded me.

13:6  LA: Et factum est post dies plurimos, dixit Dominus ad me : Surge, vade ad Euphraten, et tolle inde lumbare quod præcepi tibi ut absconderes illud ibi.
      EN: After many days the Lord said to me, “Rise, go to the Euphrates, and take from there the loincloth that I commanded you to hide there.”

13:7  LA: Et abii ad Euphraten, et fodi, et tuli lumbare de loco ubi absconderam illud : et ecce computruerat lumbare, ita ut nulli usui aptum esset.
      EN: I went to the Euphrates, dug, and took the loincloth from the place where I had hidden it. And look, it had rotted and was fit for no use.

13:8  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

13:9  LA: [Hæc dicit Dominus : Sic putrescere faciam superbiam Juda, et superbiam Jerusalem multam :
      EN: “Thus says the Lord: In this way I will make Judah's pride and Jerusalem's great pride rot away.

13:10  LA: populum istum pessimum qui nolunt audire verba mea, et ambulant in pravitate cordis sui, abieruntque post deos alienos ut servirent eis et adorarent eos : et erunt sicut lumbare istud, quod nulli usui aptum est.
       EN: This most wicked people, who refuse to hear My words, walk in the perversity of their hearts, and have followed foreign gods to serve and worship them, will become like this loincloth, which is fit for no use.

13:11  LA: Sicut enim adhæret lumbare ad lumbos viri, sic agglutinavi mihi omnem domum Israël, et omnem domum Juda, dicit Dominus, ut essent mihi in populum, et in nomen, et in laudem, et in gloriam : et non audierunt.]
       EN: For as a loincloth clings to a man's waist, so I joined to Myself the whole house of Israel and the whole house of Judah, says the Lord, to be for Me a people, a name, a praise, and a glory; but they did not listen.”

13:12  LA: Dices ergo ad eos sermonem istum : [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus Israël : Omnis laguncula implebitur vino.] Et dicent ad te : [Numquid ignoramus quia omnis laguncula implebitur vino ?]
       EN: So you shall speak this word to them: “Thus says the Lord, the God of Israel: Every jar will be filled with wine.” And they will say to you, “Do we not know that every jar will be filled with wine?”

13:13  LA: Et dices ad eos : [Hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego implebo omnes habitatores terræ hujus, et reges qui sedent de stirpe David super thronum ejus, et sacerdotes, et prophetas, et omnes habitatores Jerusalem, ebrietate.
       EN: You shall tell them, “Thus says the Lord: Look, I will fill with drunkenness all the inhabitants of this land, the kings of David's line who sit on his throne, the priests, the prophets, and all Jerusalem's inhabitants.

13:14  LA: Et dispergam eos virum a fratre suo, et patres et filios pariter, ait Dominus. Non parcam, et non concedam : neque miserebor, ut non disperdam eos.
       EN: I will scatter them, one from his brother, fathers and sons alike, says the Lord. I will not spare, relent, or have pity to keep from destroying them.

13:15  LA: Audite, et auribus percipite : nolite elevari, quia Dominus locutus est.
       EN: Hear and give ear; do not be proud, for the Lord has spoken.

13:16  LA: Date Domino Deo vestro gloriam antequam contenebrescat, et antequam offendant pedes vestri ad montes caliginosos : exspectabitis lucem, et ponet eam in umbram mortis, et in caliginem.
       EN: Give glory to the Lord your God before it grows dark, before your feet stumble on dark mountains. You will wait for light, but He will turn it into death's shadow and thick darkness.

13:17  LA: Quod si hoc non audieritis, in abscondito plorabit anima mea a facie superbiæ : plorans plorabit, et deducet oculus meus lacrimam, quia captus est grex Domini.
       EN: If you do not listen to this, my soul will weep in secret because of pride; it will weep bitterly, and my eye will shed tears, because the Lord's flock has been taken captive.

13:18  LA: Dic regi et dominatrici : Humiliamini, sedete, quoniam descendit de capite vestro corona gloriæ vestræ.
       EN: Say to the king and the queen mother: Humble yourselves and sit down, for your glorious crown has fallen from your head.

13:19  LA: Civitates austri clausæ sunt, et non est qui aperiat : translata est omnis Juda transmigratione perfecta.
       EN: The cities of the south are shut, with no one to open them. All Judah has been carried away in a complete deportation.

13:20  LA: Levate oculos vestros et videte, qui venitis ab aquilone : ubi est grex qui datus est tibi, pecus inclytum tuum ?
       EN: Lift your eyes and look, you who come from the north. Where is the flock that was given you, your splendid sheep?

13:21  LA: Quid dices cum visitaverit te ? tu enim docuisti eos adversum te, et erudisti in caput tuum. Numquid non dolores apprehendent te, quasi mulierem parturientem ?
       EN: What will you say when He punishes you? For you yourself taught them to act against you and trained them against your own head. Will pains not seize you like a woman in labor?

13:22  LA: Quod si dixeris in corde tuo : Quare venerunt mihi hæc ? propter multitudinem iniquitatis tuæ revelata sunt verecundiora tua, pollutæ sunt plantæ tuæ.
       EN: If you say in your heart, ‘Why have these things come upon me?’ it is because of your many sins that your private parts have been exposed and your feet defiled.

13:23  LA: Si mutare potest Æthiops pellem suam, aut pardus varietates suas, et vos poteritis benefacere, cum didiceritis malum.
       EN: If an Ethiopian can change his skin, or a leopard its spots, then you too can do good, accustomed as you are to evil.

13:24  LA: Et disseminabo eos quasi stipulam quæ vento raptatur in deserto.
       EN: I will scatter them like stubble swept away by the wind in the wilderness.

13:25  LA: Hæc sors tua, parsque mensuræ tuæ a me, dicit Dominus, quia oblita es mei, et confisa es in mendacio.
       EN: This is your lot, the portion I have measured for you, says the Lord, because you have forgotten Me and trusted in falsehood.

13:26  LA: Unde et ego nudavi femora tua contra faciem tuam, et apparuit ignominia tua :
       EN: So I too have bared your thighs before your face, and your disgrace has appeared:

13:27  LA: adulteria tua, et hinnitus tuus, scelus fornicationis tuæ : super colles in agro vidi abominationes tuas. Væ tibi, Jerusalem ! non mundaberis post me : usquequo adhuc ?]
       EN: your adulteries, your neighing, your shameful prostitution. On the hills in the fields I have seen your abominations. Woe to you, Jerusalem! Will you not be cleansed after Me? How much longer?”

JEREMIAH 14

14:1  LA: Quod factum est verbum Domini ad Jeremiam, de sermonibus siccitatis.
      EN: The word of the Lord that came to Jeremiah concerning the drought.

14:2  LA: [Luxit Judæa, et portæ ejus corruerunt, et obscuratæ sunt in terra, et clamor Jerusalem ascendit.
      EN: Judah has mourned, and her gates have collapsed and grown dark upon the ground; Jerusalem's cry has gone up.

14:3  LA: Majores miserunt minores suos ad aquam : venerunt ad hauriendum. Non invenerunt aquam : reportaverunt vasa sua vacua. Confusi sunt, et afflicti, et operuerunt capita sua.
      EN: The great sent their servants for water; they came to draw it but found none. They brought their vessels back empty; ashamed and distressed, they covered their heads.

14:4  LA: Propter terræ vastitatem, quia non venit pluvia in terram, confusi sunt agricolæ : operuerunt capita sua.
      EN: Because the land lay waste, since no rain came upon it, the farmers were ashamed and covered their heads.

14:5  LA: Nam et cerva in agro peperit, et reliquit, quia non erat herba.
      EN: Even the doe gave birth in the field and abandoned her young, because there was no grass.

14:6  LA: Et onagri steterunt in rupibus ; traxerunt ventum quasi dracones : defecerunt oculi eorum, quia non erat herba.
      EN: Wild donkeys stood on the rocks and drew in the wind like dragons; their eyes failed because there was no grass.

14:7  LA: Si iniquitates nostræ responderint nobis, Domine, fac propter nomen tuum : quoniam multæ sunt aversiones nostræ : tibi peccavimus.
      EN: Though our iniquities testify against us, Lord, act for Your name's sake, for our apostasies are many; we have sinned against You.

14:8  LA: Exspectatio Israël, salvator ejus in tempore tribulationis, quare quasi colonus futurus es in terra, et quasi viator declinans ad manendum ?
      EN: Hope of Israel, its Savior in time of distress, why will You be like a sojourner in the land, like a traveler who turns aside to lodge?

14:9  LA: quare futurus es velut vir vagus, ut fortis qui non potest salvare ? Tu autem in nobis es, Domine, et nomen tuum invocatum est super nos : ne derelinquas nos.
      EN: Why will You be like a wandering man, like a warrior unable to save? Yet You are among us, Lord, and Your name has been invoked over us; do not abandon us.

14:10  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus populo huic, qui dilexit movere pedes suos, et non quievit, et Domino non placuit : Nunc recordabitur iniquitatum eorum, et visitabit peccata eorum.
       EN: Thus says the Lord concerning this people, who have loved to move their feet, have not rested, and have not pleased the Lord: “Now He will remember their iniquities and punish their sins.”

14:11  LA: Et dixit Dominus ad me : Noli orare pro populo isto in bonum.
       EN: The Lord said to me, “Do not pray for the welfare of this people.

14:12  LA: Cum jejunaverint, non exaudiam preces eorum, et si obtulerint holocautomata et victimas, non suscipiam ea : quoniam gladio, et fame, et peste consumam eos.]
       EN: When they fast, I will not hear their prayers; if they offer burnt offerings and sacrifices, I will not accept them, for I will consume them by sword, famine, and pestilence.”

14:13  LA: Et dixi : [A, a, a, Domine Deus : prophetæ dicunt eis : Non videbitis gladium, et fames non erit in vobis : sed pacem veram dabit vobis in loco isto.]
       EN: And I said, “Ah, ah, ah, Lord God! The prophets say to them, ‘You will not see the sword, and famine will not be among you; instead, He will give you true peace in this place.’”

14:14  LA: Et dicit Dominus ad me : [Falso prophetæ vaticinantur in nomine meo : non misi eos, et non præcepi eis, neque locutus sum ad eos. Visionem mendacem, et divinationem, et fraudulentiam, et seductionem cordis sui, prophetant vobis.
       EN: The Lord says to me, “The prophets prophesy falsely in My name. I did not send them, command them, or speak to them. They prophesy to you a lying vision, divination, fraud, and the deception of their own hearts.

14:15  LA: Idcirco hæc dicit Dominus de prophetis qui prophetant in nomine meo, quos ego non misi, dicentes : Gladius et fames non erit in terra hac : In gladio et fame consumentur prophetæ illi.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord concerning the prophets who prophesy in My name, whom I did not send, who say, ‘Sword and famine will not be in this land’: By sword and famine those prophets will be consumed.

14:16  LA: Et populi quibus prophetant erunt projecti in viis Jerusalem præ fame et gladio, et non erit qui sepeliat eos : ipsi et uxores eorum, filii et filiæ eorum : et effundam super eos malum suum.
       EN: The peoples to whom they prophesy will lie cast out in Jerusalem's streets because of famine and sword, with no one to bury them: they, their wives, their sons, and their daughters. I will pour their own evil upon them.

14:17  LA: Et dices ad eos verbum istud : Deducant oculi mei lacrimam per noctem et diem, et non taceant, quoniam contritione magna contrita est virgo filia populi mei, plaga pessima vehementer.
       EN: You shall speak this word to them: Let my eyes shed tears night and day without ceasing, for the virgin daughter of my people has been broken with a great blow, with an exceedingly grievous wound.

14:18  LA: Si egressus fuero ad agros, ecce occisi gladio : et si introiero in civitatem, ecce attenuati fame. Propheta quoque et sacerdos abierunt in terram quam ignorabant.
       EN: If I go out to the fields, there are those slain by the sword; if I enter the city, there are those wasting away with famine. Prophet and priest alike have gone to a land they did not know.”

14:19  LA: Numquid projiciens abjecisti Judam ? aut Sion abominata est anima tua ? quare ergo percussisti nos ita ut nulla sit sanitas ? Exspectavimus pacem, et non est bonum : et tempus curationis, et ecce turbatio.
       EN: Have You utterly rejected Judah, or has Your soul abhorred Zion? Why, then, have You struck us so that there is no healing? We waited for peace, but there is no good; for a time of healing, and look, turmoil!

14:20  LA: Cognovimus, Domine, impietates nostras, iniquitates patrum nostrorum, quia peccavimus tibi.
       EN: We acknowledge our wickedness, Lord, and the iniquities of our fathers, for we have sinned against You.

14:21  LA: Ne des nos in opprobrium, propter nomen tuum, neque facias nobis contumeliam solii gloriæ tuæ : recordare, ne irritum facias fœdus tuum nobiscum.
       EN: For Your name's sake, do not give us over to reproach, or dishonor for us the throne of Your glory. Remember; do not break Your covenant with us.

14:22  LA: Numquid sunt in sculptilibus gentium qui pluant ? aut cæli possunt dare imbres ? nonne tu es Dominus Deus noster, quem exspectavimus ? tu enim fecisti omnia hæc.]
       EN: Are there any among the nations' carved images who send rain? Can the heavens give showers? Is it not You, the Lord our God, for whom we have waited? For You have made all these things.

JEREMIAH 15

15:1  LA: Et dixit Dominus ad me : [Si steterit Moyses et Samuel coram me, non est anima mea ad populum istum : ejice illos a facie mea, et egrediantur.
      EN: The Lord said to me, “Even if Moses and Samuel stood before Me, My soul would not favor this people. Cast them out of My presence; let them go.

15:2  LA: Quod si dixerint ad te : Quo egrediemur ? dices ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus : Qui ad mortem, ad mortem, et qui ad gladium, ad gladium, et qui ad famem, ad famem, et qui ad captivitatem, ad captivitatem.
      EN: If they say to you, ‘Where shall we go?’ you shall tell them: Thus says the Lord: Those destined for death, to death; those for the sword, to the sword; those for famine, to famine; and those for captivity, to captivity.

15:3  LA: Et visitabo super eos quatuor species, dicit Dominus : gladium ad occisionem, et canes ad lacerandum, et volatilia cæli et bestias terræ ad devorandum et dissipandum.
      EN: I will appoint four kinds against them, says the Lord: the sword to kill, dogs to tear apart, and the birds of heaven and beasts of the earth to devour and scatter.

15:4  LA: Et dabo eos in fervorem universis regnis terræ, propter Manassen filium Ezechiæ regis Juda, super omnibus quæ fecit in Jerusalem.
      EN: I will make them an object of agitation to all the kingdoms of the earth because of Manasseh son of Hezekiah, king of Judah, for all that he did in Jerusalem.

15:5  LA: Quis enim miserebitur tui, Jerusalem, aut quis contristabitur pro te ? aut quis ibit ad rogandum pro pace tua ?
      EN: For who will pity you, Jerusalem, or grieve for you? Who will go to ask about your welfare?

15:6  LA: Tu reliquisti me, dicit Dominus ; retrorsum abiisti : et extendam manum meam super te, et interficiam te : laboravi rogans.
      EN: You have abandoned Me, says the Lord; you have gone backward. I will stretch out My hand against you and kill you; I have grown weary of pleading.

15:7  LA: Et dispergam eos ventilabro in portis terræ : interfeci et disperdidi populum meum, et tamen a viis suis non sunt reversi.
      EN: I will scatter them with a winnowing fork at the gates of the land. I have killed and destroyed My people, yet they have not turned from their ways.

15:8  LA: Multiplicatæ sunt mihi viduæ ejus super arenam maris : induxi eis super matrem adolescentis vastatorem meridie : misi super civitates repente terrorem.
      EN: Their widows have become more numerous to Me than the sand of the sea. Against the mother of a young man I have brought them a destroyer at noon; suddenly I have sent terror upon the cities.

15:9  LA: Infirmata est quæ peperit septem ; defecit anima ejus : occidit ei sol cum adhuc esset dies : confusa est, et erubuit : et residuos ejus in gladium dabo in conspectu inimicorum eorum, ait Dominus.]
      EN: She who bore seven has grown faint; her soul has failed. Her sun has set while it was still day; she is ashamed and humiliated. I will give those remaining of her to the sword before their enemies, says the Lord.”

15:10  LA: [Væ mihi, mater mea ! quare genuisti me, virum rixæ, virum discordiæ in universa terra ? Non fœneravi, nec fœneravit mihi quisquam : omnes maledicunt mihi.
       EN: Woe to me, my mother! Why did you bear me, a man of quarrel and discord throughout the land? I have not lent at interest, nor has anyone lent to me, yet everyone curses me.

15:11  LA: Dicit Dominus : Si non reliquiæ tuæ in bonum, si non occurri tibi in tempore afflictionis, et in tempore tribulationis adversus inimicum.
       EN: The Lord says, “Surely your remnant will turn out well; surely I have come to meet you against the enemy in the time of affliction and distress.

15:12  LA: Numquid fœderabitur ferrum ferro ab aquilone, et æs ?
       EN: Can iron be joined with iron from the north, and with bronze?

15:13  LA: Divitias tuas et thesauros tuos in direptionem dabo gratis, in omnibus peccatis tuis, et in omnibus terminis tuis.
       EN: Your wealth and treasures I will give away as plunder, without price, for all your sins throughout all your borders.

15:14  LA: Et adducam inimicos tuos de terra quam nescis, quia ignis succensus est in furore meo : super vos ardebit.
       EN: I will bring your enemies from a land you do not know, for a fire has been kindled in My fury; it will burn against you.”

15:15  LA: Tu scis, Domine : recordare mei, et visita me, et tuere me ab his qui persequuntur me. Noli in patientia tua suscipere me : scito quoniam sustinui propter te opprobrium.
       EN: You know, Lord; remember me, visit me, and protect me from those who persecute me. Do not take me away in Your patience; know that for Your sake I have endured reproach.

15:16  LA: Inventi sunt sermones tui, et comedi eos : et factum est mihi verbum tuum in gaudium et in lætitiam cordis mei, quoniam invocatum est nomen tuum super me, Domine Deus exercituum.
       EN: Your words were found, and I ate them. Your word became the joy and gladness of my heart, for Your name has been invoked over me, Lord God of hosts.

15:17  LA: Non sedi in concilio ludentium, et gloriatus sum a facie manus tuæ : solus sedebam, quoniam comminatione replesti me.
       EN: I did not sit in the company of those making merry, and I gloried in the presence of Your hand. I sat alone, for You filled me with threatening.

15:18  LA: Quare factus est dolor meus perpetuus, et plaga mea desperabilis renuit curari ? facta est mihi quasi mendacium aquarum infidelium.
       EN: Why has my pain become unending, and my desperate wound refused healing? It has become to me like the deception of unreliable waters.

15:19  LA: Propter hoc hæc dicit Dominus : Si converteris, convertam te, et ante faciem meam stabis : et si separaveris pretiosum a vili, quasi os meum eris : convertentur ipsi ad te, et tu non converteris ad eos.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord: “If you return, I will restore you, and you will stand before Me. If you separate the precious from the worthless, you will be as My mouth. They will turn to you, but you shall not turn to them.

15:20  LA: Et dabo te populo huic in murum æreum fortem : et bellabunt adversum te, et non prævalebunt, quia ego tecum sum ut salvem te, et eruam te, dicit Dominus :
       EN: I will make you a strong bronze wall against this people. They will fight against you but will not prevail, for I am with you to save and rescue you, says the Lord.

15:21  LA: et liberabo te de manu pessimorum, et redimam te de manu fortium.]
       EN: I will deliver you from the hands of the most wicked and redeem you from the hands of the mighty.”

JEREMIAH 16

16:1  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

16:2  LA: [Non accipies uxorem, et non erunt tibi filii et filiæ in loco isto.
      EN: “You shall not take a wife, and you shall have no sons or daughters in this place.

16:3  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus super filios et filias qui generantur in loco isto, et super matres eorum, quæ genuerunt eos, et super patres eorum, de quorum stirpe sunt nati in terra hac :
      EN: For thus says the Lord concerning the sons and daughters born in this place, the mothers who bore them, and the fathers from whose stock they were born in this land:

16:4  LA: Mortibus ægrotationum morientur : non plangentur, et non sepelientur : in sterquilinium super faciem terræ erunt, et gladio et fame consumentur : et erit cadaver eorum in escam volatilibus cæli et bestiis terræ.
      EN: They will die of deadly diseases; they will not be mourned or buried. They will be dung upon the face of the earth and will be consumed by sword and famine; their bodies will be food for the birds of heaven and the beasts of the earth.

16:5  LA: Hæc enim dicit Dominus : Ne ingrediaris domum convivii, neque vadas ad plangendum, neque consoleris eos, quia abstuli pacem meam a populo isto, dicit Dominus, misericordiam et miserationes.
      EN: For thus says the Lord: Do not enter a house of feasting, go to mourn, or comfort them, for I have taken My peace from this people, says the Lord, My mercy and compassion.

16:6  LA: Et morientur grandes et parvi in terra ista : non sepelientur, neque plangentur, et non se incident, neque calvitium fiet pro eis.
      EN: Great and small will die in this land; they will not be buried or mourned. No one will cut himself or shave his head for them.

16:7  LA: Et non frangent inter eos lugenti panem ad consolandum super mortuo, et non dabunt eis potum calicis ad consolandum super patre suo et matre.
      EN: They will not break bread among them for a mourner to comfort him for the dead, or give them the cup to drink in consolation for father or mother.

16:8  LA: Et domum convivii non ingrediaris, ut sedeas cum eis, et comedas, et bibas.
      EN: Nor shall you enter a house of feasting to sit with them, eat, and drink.

16:9  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Ecce ego auferam de loco isto, in oculis vestris, et in diebus vestris, vocem gaudii et vocem lætitiæ, vocem sponsi et vocem sponsæ.
      EN: For thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Look, in your sight and in your days I will take from this place the voice of joy and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom and the voice of the bride.

16:10  LA: Et cum annuntiaveris populo huic omnia verba hæc, et dixerint tibi : Quare locutus est Dominus super nos omne malum grande istud ? quæ iniquitas nostra, et quod peccatum nostrum, quod peccavimus Domino Deo nostro ?
       EN: When you announce all these words to this people, and they say to you, ‘Why has the Lord pronounced all this great disaster against us? What is our iniquity? What sin have we committed against the Lord our God?’

16:11  LA: dices ad eos : Quia dereliquerunt me patres vestri, ait Dominus, et abierunt post deos alienos, et servierunt eis, et adoraverunt eos, et me dereliquerunt, et legem meam non custodierunt.
       EN: you shall tell them: Because your fathers abandoned Me, says the Lord, followed foreign gods, served and worshiped them, abandoned Me, and did not keep My law.

16:12  LA: Sed et vos pejus operati estis quam patres vestri : ecce enim ambulat unusquisque post pravitatem cordis sui mali, ut me non audiat.
       EN: But you have acted worse than your fathers. Look, each of you follows the perversity of his evil heart instead of listening to Me.

16:13  LA: Et ejiciam vos de terra hac in terram quam ignoratis, vos et patres vestri : et servietis ibi diis alienis, die ac nocte, qui non dabunt vobis requiem.
       EN: I will cast you out of this land into a land that neither you nor your fathers know. There, day and night, you will serve foreign gods who will give you no rest.

16:14  LA: Propterea ecce dies veniunt, dicit Dominus, et non dicetur ultra : Vivit Dominus qui eduxit filios Israël de terra Ægypti,
       EN: Therefore, look, days are coming, says the Lord, when it will no longer be said, ‘As the Lord lives, who brought the children of Israel out of the land of Egypt,’

16:15  LA: sed : Vivit Dominus qui eduxit filios Israël de terra aquilonis, et de universis terris ad quas ejeci eos : et reducam eos in terram suam, quam dedi patribus eorum.
       EN: but, ‘As the Lord lives, who brought the children of Israel out of the northern land and all the lands to which I drove them.’ I will bring them back to their land, which I gave their fathers.

16:16  LA: Ecce ego mittam piscatores multos, dicit Dominus, et piscabuntur eos : et post hæc mittam eis multos venatores, et venabuntur eos de omni monte, et de omni colle, et de cavernis petrarum.
       EN: Look, I will send many fishermen, says the Lord, and they will fish for them; afterward I will send them many hunters, and they will hunt them from every mountain, every hill, and the clefts of the rocks.

16:17  LA: Quia oculi mei super omnes vias eorum : non sunt absconditæ a facie mea, et non fuit occultata iniquitas eorum ab oculis meis.
       EN: For My eyes are on all their ways; they are not hidden from My face, and their iniquity has not been concealed from My eyes.

16:18  LA: Et reddam primum duplices iniquitates et peccata eorum, quia contaminaverunt terram meam in morticinis idolorum suorum, et abominationibus suis impleverunt hæreditatem meam.
       EN: First I will repay their iniquities and sins twice over, because they have defiled My land with the dead bodies of their idols and filled My inheritance with their abominations.”

16:19  LA: Domine, fortitudo mea, et robur meum, et refugium meum in die tribulationis, ad te gentes venient ab extremis terræ, et dicent : Vere mendacium possederunt patres nostri, vanitatem quæ eis non profuit.
       EN: Lord, my strength, my might, and my refuge on the day of distress, nations will come to You from the ends of the earth and say, “Truly our fathers inherited falsehood, emptiness that did not help them.”

16:20  LA: Numquid faciet sibi homo deos, et ipsi non sunt dii ?
       EN: Can a man make gods for himself, when they are not gods?

16:21  LA: Idcirco ecce ego ostendam eis per vicem hanc, ostendam eis manum meam, et virtutem meam, et scient quia nomen mihi Dominus.]
       EN: “Therefore, look, this time I will make them know; I will show them My hand and My might, and they will know that My name is the Lord.”

JEREMIAH 17

17:5  LA: [Hæc dicit Dominus : Maledictus homo qui confidit in homine, et ponit carnem brachium suum, et a Domino recedit cor ejus.
      EN: Thus says the Lord: Cursed is the man who trusts in man, makes flesh his arm, and turns his heart away from the Lord.

17:6  LA: Erit enim quasi myricæ in deserto, et non videbit cum venerit bonum : sed habitabit in siccitate in deserto, in terra salsuginis et inhabitabili.
      EN: He will be like a tamarisk in the wilderness and will not see when good comes, but will dwell in the parched wilderness, in a salt land unfit for habitation.

17:7  LA: Benedictus vir qui confidit in Domino, et erit Dominus fiducia ejus.
      EN: Blessed is the man who trusts in the Lord, whose confidence is the Lord.

17:8  LA: Et erit quasi lignum quod transplantatur super aquas, quod ad humorem mittit radices suas, et non timebit cum venerit æstus : et erit folium ejus viride, et in tempore siccitatis non erit sollicitum, nec aliquando desinet facere fructum.
      EN: He will be like a tree transplanted beside waters, sending its roots toward moisture. It will not fear when heat comes; its leaves will stay green. In time of drought it will not be troubled, and it will never cease bearing fruit.

17:9  LA: Pravum est cor omnium, et inscrutabile : quis cognoscet illud ?
      EN: The heart of all is perverse and unsearchable; who can know it?

17:10  LA: Ego Dominus scrutans cor, et probans renes : qui do unicuique juxta viam suam, et juxta fructum adinventionum suarum.
       EN: I, the Lord, search the heart and test the kidneys, giving each according to his way and the fruit of his deeds.

17:11  LA: Perdix fovit quæ non peperit : fecit divitias, et non in judicio : in dimidio dierum suorum derelinquet eas, et in novissimo suo erit insipiens.
       EN: A partridge broods over what it did not hatch; a man acquires riches unjustly. In the midst of his days he will leave them, and at his end he will be a fool.”

17:12  LA: Solium gloriæ altitudinis a principio, locus sanctificationis nostræ.
       EN: A throne of glory, exalted from the beginning, is the place of our sanctification.

17:13  LA: Exspectatio Israël, Domine, omnes qui te derelinquunt confundentur : recedentes a te, in terra scribentur, quoniam dereliquerunt venam aquarum viventium Dominum.
       EN: Lord, hope of Israel, all who abandon You will be put to shame. Those who withdraw from You will be written in the earth, because they have abandoned the Lord, the spring of living waters.

17:14  LA: Sana me, Domine, et sanabor : salvum me fac, et salvus ero : quoniam laus mea tu es.
       EN: Heal me, Lord, and I will be healed; save me, and I will be saved, for You are my praise.

17:15  LA: Ecce ipsi dicunt ad me : Ubi est verbum Domini ? veniat :
       EN: Look, they say to me, “Where is the word of the Lord? Let it come!”

17:16  LA: et ego non sum turbatus, te pastorem sequens : et diem hominis non desideravi, tu scis : quod egressum est de labiis meis, rectum in conspectu tuo fuit.
       EN: But I have not been troubled as I followed You, my Shepherd, and I have not desired the day of man. You know; what came from my lips was right in Your sight.

17:17  LA: Non sis tu mihi formidini : spes mea tu in die afflictionis.
       EN: Do not be a terror to me; You are my hope on the day of affliction.

17:18  LA: Confundantur qui me persequuntur, et non confundar ego : paveant illi, et non paveam ego : induc super eos diem afflictionis, et duplici contritione contere eos.]
       EN: Let those who persecute me be ashamed, but let me not be ashamed; let them be terrified, but let me not be terrified. Bring the day of affliction upon them, and crush them with double destruction.

17:19  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus ad me : Vade, et sta in porta filiorum populi, per quam ingrediuntur reges Juda, et egrediuntur, et in cunctis portis Jerusalem :
       EN: Thus says the Lord to me: “Go and stand at the gate of the children of the people, through which Judah's kings enter and leave, and at all Jerusalem's gates.

17:20  LA: et dices ad eos : [Audite verbum Domini, reges Juda, et omnis Juda, cunctique habitatores Jerusalem, qui ingredimini per portas istas.
       EN: Say to them: Hear the word of the Lord, kings of Judah, all Judah, and all inhabitants of Jerusalem who enter through these gates.

17:21  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Custodite animas vestras, et nolite portare pondera in die sabbati, nec inferatis per portas Jerusalem :
       EN: Thus says the Lord: Guard your lives, and do not carry burdens on the sabbath day or bring them through Jerusalem's gates.

17:22  LA: et nolite ejicere onera de domibus vestris in die sabbati, et omne opus non facietis : sanctificate diem sabbati, sicut præcepi patribus vestris.
       EN: Do not carry loads out of your houses on the sabbath day, and do no work. Keep the sabbath day holy, as I commanded your fathers.

17:23  LA: Et non audierunt, nec inclinaverunt aurem suam : sed induraverunt cervicem suam, ne audirent me, et ne acciperent disciplinam.
       EN: But they did not listen or incline their ears; they stiffened their necks, refusing to hear Me or accept correction.

17:24  LA: Et erit : si audieritis me, dicit Dominus, ut non inferatis onera per portas civitatis hujus in die sabbati : et si sanctificaveritis diem sabbati, ne faciatis in eo omne opus :
       EN: If you listen to Me, says the Lord, bringing no loads through this city's gates on the sabbath day, and if you keep the sabbath day holy, doing no work on it,

17:25  LA: ingredientur per portas civitatis hujus reges et principes, sedentes super solium David, et ascendentes in curribus et equis, ipsi et principes eorum, viri Juda, et habitatores Jerusalem : et habitabitur civitas hæc in sempiternum.
       EN: then kings and rulers sitting on David's throne will enter this city's gates, riding in chariots and on horses: they and their rulers, the men of Judah, and Jerusalem's inhabitants. This city will be inhabited forever.

17:26  LA: Et venient de civitatibus Juda, et de circuitu Jerusalem, et de terra Benjamin, et de campestribus, et de montuosis, et ab austro, portantes holocaustum, et victimam, et sacrificium, et thus, et inferent oblationem in domum Domini.
       EN: They will come from Judah's cities, from around Jerusalem, from the land of Benjamin, from the plains, from the hill country, and from the south, bringing burnt offerings, victims, sacrifices, and frankincense; they will bring offerings into the Lord's house.

17:27  LA: Si autem non audieritis me ut sanctificetis diem sabbati, et ne portetis onus, et ne inferatis per portas Jerusalem in die sabbati, succendam ignem in portis ejus, et devorabit domos Jerusalem, et non extinguetur.]
       EN: But if you do not listen to Me, keeping the sabbath day holy, carrying no load and bringing none through Jerusalem's gates on the sabbath day, I will kindle a fire in her gates; it will devour Jerusalem's houses and will not be quenched.”

JEREMIAH 18

18:1  LA: Verbum quod factum est ad Jeremiam a Domino, dicens :
      EN: The word that came to Jeremiah from the Lord, saying:

18:2  LA: Surge, et descende in domum figuli, et ibi audies verba mea.
      EN: “Rise and go down to the potter's house, and there you will hear My words.”

18:3  LA: Et descendi in domum figuli, et ecce ipse faciebat opus super rotam.
      EN: I went down to the potter's house, and there he was working at the wheel.

18:4  LA: Et dissipatum est vas quod ipse faciebat e luto manibus suis : conversusque fecit illud vas alterum, sicut placuerat in oculis ejus ut faceret.
      EN: The vessel that he was making from clay with his hands was spoiled, so he made it again into another vessel, as seemed good to him to make.

18:5  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

18:6  LA: [Numquid sicut figulus iste, non potero vobis facere, domus Israël ? ait Dominus : ecce sicut lutum in manu figuli, sic vos in manu mea, domus Israël.
      EN: “Can I not do to you as this potter does, house of Israel? says the Lord. Look, like clay in the potter's hand, so are you in My hand, house of Israel.

18:7  LA: Repente loquar adversum gentem et adversus regnum, ut eradicem, et destruam, et disperdam illud :
      EN: At one moment I may speak against a nation and a kingdom, to uproot, destroy, and ruin it.

18:8  LA: si pœnitentiam egerit gens illa a malo suo quod locutus sum adversus eam, agam et ego pœnitentiam super malo quod cogitavi ut facerem ei.
      EN: If that nation turns in repentance from the evil against which I have spoken, I too will relent concerning the disaster I intended to bring upon it.

18:9  LA: Et subito loquar de gente et de regno, ut ædificem et plantem illud.
      EN: At another moment I may speak concerning a nation and a kingdom, to build and plant it.

18:10  LA: Si fecerit malum in oculis meis, ut non audiat vocem meam, pœnitentiam agam super bono quod locutus sum ut facerem ei.
       EN: If it does evil in My eyes by not listening to My voice, I will relent concerning the good I said I would do for it.

18:11  LA: Nunc ergo dic viro Juda, et habitatoribus Jerusalem, dicens : Hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego fingo contra vos malum, et cogito contra vos cogitationem : revertatur unusquisque a via sua mala, et dirigite vias vestras et studia vestra.
       EN: So now speak to the men of Judah and Jerusalem's inhabitants: Thus says the Lord: Look, I am shaping disaster against you and devising a plan against you. Let each turn from his evil way; set your ways and deeds right.

18:12  LA: Qui dixerunt : Desperavimus : post cogitationes enim nostras ibimus, et unusquisque pravitatem cordis sui mali faciemus.
       EN: But they said, ‘We have given up hope; we will follow our own thoughts, and each of us will act according to the perversity of his evil heart.’

18:13  LA: Ideo hæc dicit Dominus : Interrogate gentes : Quis audivit talia horribilia, quæ fecit nimis virgo Israël ?
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord: Ask among the nations: Who has heard such exceedingly horrible things as virgin Israel has done?

18:14  LA: Numquid deficiet de petra agri nix Libani ? aut evelli possunt aquæ erumpentes frigidæ, et defluentes ?
       EN: Will the snow of Lebanon fail from the rock of the field? Can the cold, flowing waters that gush forth be uprooted?

18:15  LA: Quia oblitus est mei populus meus, frustra libantes, et impingentes in viis suis, in semitis sæculi, ut ambularent per eas in itinere non trito,
       EN: For My people have forgotten Me, pouring out offerings in vain and stumbling in their ways, in the ancient paths, to walk along them by an untrodden road,

18:16  LA: ut fieret terra eorum in desolationem, et in sibilum sempiternum : omnis qui præterierit per eam obstupescet, et movebit caput suum.
       EN: making their land a desolation and an everlasting object of hissing. Everyone who passes through it will be astounded and shake his head.

18:17  LA: Sicut ventus urens dispergam eos coram inimico : dorsum, et non faciem, ostendam eis in die perditionis eorum.]
       EN: Like a scorching wind I will scatter them before the enemy. I will show them My back, not My face, on the day of their destruction.”

18:18  LA: Et dixerunt : [Venite, et cogitemus contra Jeremiam cogitationes : non enim peribit lex a sacerdote, neque consilium a sapiente, nec sermo a propheta : venite, et percutiamus eum lingua, et non attendamus ad universos sermones ejus.]
       EN: They said, “Come, let us devise plans against Jeremiah, for the law will not perish from the priest, counsel from the wise, or the word from the prophet. Come, let us strike him with the tongue and pay no attention to any of his words.”

18:19  LA: [Attende, Domine, ad me, et audi vocem adversariorum meorum.
       EN: Pay attention to me, Lord, and hear the voice of my adversaries.

18:20  LA: Numquid redditur pro bono malum, quia foderunt foveam animæ meæ ? Recordare quod steterim in conspectu tuo ut loquerer pro eis bonum, et averterem indignationem tuam ab eis.
       EN: Is evil repaid for good, that they have dug a pit for my life? Remember how I stood before You to speak good on their behalf and turn Your indignation away from them.

18:21  LA: Propterea da filios eorum in famem, et deduc eos in manus gladii : fiant uxores eorum absque liberis, et viduæ : et viri earum interficiantur morte : juvenes eorum confodiantur gladio in prælio :
       EN: Therefore give their children over to famine and hand them over to the sword. Let their wives be childless and widowed; let their husbands be put to death and their young men pierced by the sword in battle.

18:22  LA: audiatur clamor de domibus eorum : adduces enim super eos latronem repente, quia foderunt foveam ut caperent me, et laqueos absconderunt pedibus meis.
       EN: Let a cry be heard from their houses, for You will suddenly bring a raider upon them, because they dug a pit to catch me and hid snares for my feet.

18:23  LA: Tu autem, Domine, scis omne consilium eorum adversum me in mortem : ne propitieris iniquitati eorum, et peccatum eorum a facie tua non deleatur : fiant corruentes in conspectu tuo ; in tempore furoris tui abutere eis.]
       EN: But You, Lord, know all their plans to put me to death. Do not forgive their iniquity or wipe their sin from Your sight. Let them fall before You; deal with them in the time of Your fury.

JEREMIAH 19

19:1  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Vade, et accipe lagunculam figuli testeam a senioribus populi et a senioribus sacerdotum,
      EN: Thus says the Lord: “Go and obtain a potter's earthenware jar from the elders of the people and the elders of the priests.

19:2  LA: et egredere ad vallem filii Ennom, quæ est juxta introitum portæ fictilis : et prædicabis ibi verba quæ ego loquar ad te.
      EN: Go out to the Valley of the Son of Hinnom, which is beside the entrance of the Potsherd Gate, and proclaim there the words that I speak to you.

19:3  LA: Et dices : [Audite verbum Domini, reges Juda, et habitatores Jerusalem. Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Ecce ego inducam afflictionem super locum istum, ita ut omnis qui audierit illam, tinniant aures ejus,
      EN: Say: Hear the word of the Lord, kings of Judah and inhabitants of Jerusalem. Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Look, I will bring distress upon this place such that the ears of everyone who hears it will ring,

19:4  LA: eo quod dereliquerint me, et alienum fecerint locum istum, et libaverunt in eo diis alienis quos nescierunt, ipsi et patres eorum, et reges Juda : et repleverunt locum istum sanguine innocentum,
      EN: because they have abandoned Me, made this place foreign to Me, and poured out offerings here to foreign gods unknown to them, their fathers, and Judah's kings. They have filled this place with innocent blood

19:5  LA: et ædificaverunt excelsa Baalim, ad comburendos filios suos igni in holocaustum Baalim : quæ non præcepi, nec locutus sum, nec ascenderunt in cor meum.
      EN: and built high places for the Baals, to burn their sons in fire as burnt offerings to the Baals, things that I did not command or speak and that never entered My heart.

19:6  LA: Propterea ecce dies veniunt, dicit Dominus, et non vocabitur amplius locus iste Topheth, et vallis filii Ennom, sed vallis occisionis.
      EN: Therefore, look, days are coming, says the Lord, when this place will no longer be called Topheth or the Valley of the Son of Hinnom, but the Valley of Slaughter.

19:7  LA: Et dissipabo consilium Juda et Jerusalem in loco isto, et subvertam eos gladio in conspectu inimicorum suorum, et in manu quærentium animas eorum : et dabo cadavera eorum escam volatilibus cæli et bestiis terræ.
      EN: I will frustrate the plans of Judah and Jerusalem in this place and overthrow them by the sword before their enemies, by the hands of those seeking their lives. I will give their corpses as food to the birds of heaven and the beasts of the earth.

19:8  LA: Et ponam civitatem hanc in stuporem, et in sibilum : omnis qui præterierit per eam obstupescet, et sibilabit super universa plaga ejus.
      EN: I will make this city an object of horror and hissing; everyone who passes through it will be astounded and hiss at all its wounds.

19:9  LA: Et cibabo eos carnibus filiorum suorum et carnibus filiarum suarum : et unusquisque carnem amici sui comedet in obsidione, et in angustia in qua concludent eos inimici eorum, et qui quærunt animas eorum.]
      EN: I will feed them the flesh of their sons and daughters; each will eat his friend's flesh during the siege and distress in which their enemies and those seeking their lives will confine them.”

19:10  LA: Et conteres lagunculam in oculis virorum qui ibunt tecum,
       EN: You shall smash the jar in the sight of the men who go with you,

19:11  LA: et dices ad eos : [Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Sic conteram populum istum, et civitatem istam, sicut conteritur vas figuli, quod non potest ultra instaurari : et in Topheth sepelientur, eo quod non sit alius locus ad sepeliendum.
       EN: and say to them, “Thus says the Lord of hosts: So will I smash this people and this city, as a potter's vessel is smashed and cannot be restored again. They will be buried in Topheth because there is no other place to bury them.

19:12  LA: Sic faciam loco huic, ait Dominus, et habitatoribus ejus, et ponam civitatem istam sicut Topheth.
       EN: So will I do to this place, says the Lord, and to its inhabitants; I will make this city like Topheth.

19:13  LA: Et erunt domus Jerusalem, et domus regum Juda, sicut locus Topheth, immundæ, omnes domus in quarum domatibus sacrificaverunt omni militiæ cæli, et libaverunt libamina diis alienis.]
       EN: The houses of Jerusalem and the houses of Judah's kings will be unclean like the place of Topheth: all the houses on whose roofs they sacrificed to all the host of heaven and poured out drink offerings to foreign gods.”

19:14  LA: Venit autem Jeremias de Topheth, quo miserat eum Dominus ad prophetandum, et stetit in atrio domus Domini, et dixit ad omnem populum :
       EN: Jeremiah came from Topheth, where the Lord had sent him to prophesy, stood in the court of the Lord's house, and said to all the people:

19:15  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Ecce ego inducam super civitatem hanc, et super omnes urbes ejus, universa mala quæ locutus sum adversum eam, quoniam induraverunt cervicem suam ut non audirent sermones meos.
       EN: “Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Look, I will bring upon this city and all its towns every disaster that I have pronounced against it, because they have stiffened their necks so as not to hear My words.”

JEREMIAH 20

20:1  LA: Et audivit Phassur filius Emmer, sacerdos, qui constitutus erat princeps in domo Domini, Jeremiam prophetantem sermones istos.
      EN: Pashhur son of Immer, the priest appointed chief officer in the Lord's house, heard Jeremiah prophesying these words.

20:2  LA: Et percussit Phassur Jeremiam prophetam, et misit eum in nervum quod erat in porta Benjamin superiori, in domo Domini.
      EN: Pashhur struck Jeremiah the prophet and put him in the stocks that were at the upper Benjamin Gate in the Lord's house.

20:3  LA: Cumque illuxisset in crastinum, eduxit Phassur Jeremiam de nervo, et dixit ad eum Jeremias : Non Phassur vocavit Dominus nomen tuum, sed Pavorem undique.
      EN: When the next day dawned, Pashhur released Jeremiah from the stocks. Jeremiah said to him, “The Lord has not called your name Pashhur, but Terror on Every Side.

20:4  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus : [Ecce ego dabo te in pavorem, te et omnes amicos tuos : et corruent gladio inimicorum suorum, et oculi tui videbunt : et omnem Judam dabo in manum regis Babylonis, et traducet eos in Babylonem, et percutiet eos gladio.
      EN: For thus says the Lord: Look, I will make you a terror, you and all your friends. They will fall by their enemies' sword, and your eyes will see it. I will give all Judah into the hand of the king of Babylon; he will carry them to Babylon and strike them with the sword.

20:5  LA: Et dabo universam substantiam civitatis hujus, et omnem laborem ejus, omneque pretium, et cunctos thesauros regum Juda dabo in manu inimicorum eorum : et diripient eos, et tollent, et ducent in Babylonem.
      EN: I will give all this city's substance, all its labor and valuables, and all the treasures of Judah's kings into their enemies' hands. They will plunder them, take them away, and carry them to Babylon.

20:6  LA: Tu autem, Phassur, et omnes habitatores domus tuæ, ibitis in captivitatem : et in Babylonem venies, et ibi morieris, ibique sepelieris tu, et omnes amici tui, quibus prophetasti mendacium.]
      EN: And you, Pashhur, and all who live in your house will go into captivity. You will come to Babylon, die there, and be buried there, you and all your friends to whom you prophesied lies.”

20:7  LA: [Seduxisti me, Domine, et seductus sum : fortior me fuisti, et invaluisti : factus sum in derisum tota die ; omnes subsannant me.
      EN: You enticed me, Lord, and I was enticed; You were stronger than I and prevailed. I have become a laughingstock all day long; everyone mocks me.

20:8  LA: Quia jam olim loquor, vociferans iniquitatem, et vastitatem clamito : et factus est mihi sermo Domini in opprobrium, et in derisum tota die.
      EN: For now I have long spoken, crying out against injustice and proclaiming devastation. The word of the Lord has brought me reproach and mockery all day long.

20:9  LA: Et dixi : Non recordabor ejus, neque loquar ultra in nomine illius : et factus est in corde meo quasi ignis exæstuans, claususque in ossibus meis, et defeci, ferre non sustinens.
      EN: I said, “I will not remember Him or speak in His name anymore.” But it became in my heart like a blazing fire shut up in my bones, and I grew faint, unable to bear it.

20:10  LA: Audivi enim contumelias multorum, et terrorem in circuitu : Persequimini, et persequamur eum, ab omnibus viris qui erant pacifici mei, et custodientes latus meum : si quomodo decipiatur, et prævaleamus adversus eum, et consequamur ultionem ex eo.
       EN: For I heard the insults of many and terror all around: “Pursue him! Let us pursue him!” All the men who were at peace with me and watched at my side said, “Perhaps he may be deceived, and we may prevail against him and take our revenge on him.”

20:11  LA: Dominus autem mecum est, quasi bellator fortis : idcirco qui persequuntur me cadent, et infirmi erunt : confundentur vehementer, quia non intellexerunt opprobrium sempiternum, quod numquam delebitur.
       EN: But the Lord is with me like a mighty warrior. Therefore those who persecute me will fall and be powerless; they will be utterly ashamed, because they did not understand the everlasting reproach that will never be erased.

20:12  LA: Et tu, Domine exercituum, probator justi, qui vides renes et cor, videam, quæso, ultionem tuam ex eis : tibi enim revelavi causam meam.
       EN: And You, Lord of hosts, who test the just and see kidneys and heart, let me, I pray, see Your vengeance upon them, for I have disclosed my case to You.

20:13  LA: Cantate Domino, laudate Dominum, quia liberavit animam pauperis de manu malorum.
       EN: Sing to the Lord; praise the Lord, for He has delivered the poor man's life from the hands of the wicked.

20:14  LA: Maledicta dies in qua natus sum ! dies in qua peperit me mater mea non sit benedicta !
       EN: Cursed be the day on which I was born! Let the day on which my mother bore me not be blessed!

20:15  LA: Maledictus vir qui annuntiavit patri meo, dicens : Natus est tibi puer masculus, et quasi gaudio lætificavit eum !
       EN: Cursed be the man who told my father, “A male child has been born to you,” making him so glad!

20:16  LA: Sit homo ille ut sunt civitates quæ subvertit Dominus, et non pœnituit eum : audiat clamorem mane, et ululatum in tempore meridiano,
       EN: Let that man be like the cities that the Lord overthrew without relenting. Let him hear a cry in the morning and wailing at noon,

20:17  LA: qui non me interfecit a vulva, ut fieret mihi mater mea sepulchrum, et vulva ejus conceptus æternus !
       EN: because he did not kill me in the womb, so that my mother might have become my tomb and her womb remained pregnant forever!

20:18  LA: Quare de vulva egressus sum, ut viderem laborem et dolorem, et consumerentur in confusione dies mei ?]
       EN: Why did I come out of the womb to see toil and sorrow and have my days consumed in shame?

JEREMIAH 21

21:1  LA: Verbum quod factum est ad Jeremiam a Domino, quando misit ad eum rex Sedecias Phassur filium Melchiæ, et Sophoniam filium Maasiæ sacerdotem, dicens :
      EN: The word that came to Jeremiah from the Lord when King Zedekiah sent Pashhur son of Malchiah and the priest Zephaniah son of Maaseiah to him, saying:

21:2  LA: Interroga pro nobis Dominum, quia Nabuchodonosor, rex Babylonis, præliatur adversum nos : si forte faciat Dominus nobiscum secundum omnia mirabilia sua, et recedat a nobis.
      EN: “Inquire of the Lord for us, because Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon is waging war against us. Perhaps the Lord will act for us according to all His wonders, and he will withdraw from us.”

21:3  LA: Et dixit Jeremias ad eos : Sic dicetis Sedeciæ :
      EN: Jeremiah said to them, “This is what you shall tell Zedekiah:

21:4  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus Israël : Ecce ego convertam vasa belli quæ in manibus vestris sunt, et quibus vos pugnatis adversum regem Babylonis et Chaldæos qui obsident vos in circuitu murorum : et congregabo ea in medio civitatis hujus.
      EN: Thus says the Lord, the God of Israel: Look, I will turn back the weapons of war in your hands, with which you fight against the king of Babylon and the Chaldeans besieging you around the walls, and gather them into the midst of this city.

21:5  LA: Et debellabo ego vos in manu extenta, et in brachio forti, et in furore, et in indignatione, et in ira grandi.
      EN: I Myself will fight against you with outstretched hand and mighty arm, in fury, indignation, and great anger.

21:6  LA: Et percutiam habitatores civitatis hujus : homines et bestiæ pestilentia magna morientur.
      EN: I will strike the inhabitants of this city; men and beasts will die of a great pestilence.

21:7  LA: Et post hæc, ait Dominus, dabo Sedeciam regem Juda, et servos ejus, et populum ejus, et qui derelicti sunt in civitate hac a peste, et gladio, et fame, in manu Nabuchodonosor regis Babylonis, et in manu inimicorum eorum, et in manu quærentium animam eorum : et percutiet eos in ore gladii, et non flectetur, neque parcet, nec miserebitur.
      EN: After this, says the Lord, I will give Zedekiah king of Judah, his servants, his people, and those in this city who survive pestilence, sword, and famine into the hand of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon, into the hands of their enemies and those seeking their lives. He will strike them with the edge of the sword; he will not relent, spare, or have mercy.

21:8  LA: Et ad populum hunc dices : Hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego do coram vobis viam vitæ, et viam mortis.
      EN: To this people you shall say: Thus says the Lord: Look, I set before you the way of life and the way of death.

21:9  LA: Qui habitaverit in urbe hac morietur gladio, et fame, et peste : qui autem egressus fuerit, et transfugerit ad Chaldæos qui obsident vos, vivet, et erit ei anima sua quasi spolium.
      EN: Anyone who stays in this city will die by sword, famine, and pestilence; but anyone who goes out and defects to the Chaldeans besieging you will live and have his life as plunder.

21:10  LA: Posui enim faciem meam super civitatem hanc in malum, et non in bonum, ait Dominus : in manu regis Babylonis dabitur, et exuret eam igni.
       EN: For I have set My face against this city for evil and not for good, says the Lord. It will be given into the hand of the king of Babylon, and he will burn it with fire.

21:11  LA: Et domui regis Juda : [Audite verba Domini,
       EN: And to the house of the king of Judah: Hear the words of the Lord,

21:12  LA: domus David. Hæc dicit Dominus : Judicate mane judicium, et eruite vi oppressum de manu calumniantis, ne forte egrediatur ut ignis indignatio mea, et succendatur, et non sit qui extinguat, propter malitiam studiorum vestrorum.
       EN: house of David. Thus says the Lord: Render judgment in the morning and rescue the violently oppressed from the oppressor's hand, lest My indignation come forth like fire and burn, with no one to quench it, because of the wickedness of your deeds.

21:13  LA: Ecce ego ad te, habitatricem vallis solidæ atque campestris, ait Dominus : qui dicitis : Quis percutiet nos ? et quis ingredietur domos nostras ?
       EN: Look, I am against you, inhabitant of the firm, level valley, says the Lord, you who say, ‘Who will strike us? Who will enter our houses?’

21:14  LA: Et visitabo super vos juxta fructum studiorum vestrorum, dicit Dominus : et succendam ignem in saltu ejus, et devorabit omnia in circuitu ejus.]
       EN: I will punish you according to the fruit of your deeds, says the Lord. I will kindle a fire in its forest, and it will devour everything around it.”

JEREMIAH 22

22:1  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Descende in domum regis Juda, et loqueris ibi verbum hoc,
      EN: Thus says the Lord: “Go down to the house of the king of Judah and speak this word there.

22:2  LA: et dices : [Audi verbum Domini, rex Juda, qui sedes super solium David : tu et servi tui, et populus tuus, qui ingredimini per portas istas.
      EN: Say: Hear the word of the Lord, king of Judah who sit on David's throne, you, your servants, and your people who enter through these gates.

22:3  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Facite judicium et justitiam, et liberate vi oppressum de manu calumniatoris : et advenam, et pupillum, et viduam nolite contristare, neque opprimatis inique, et sanguinem innocentem ne effundatis in loco isto.
      EN: Thus says the Lord: Practice judgment and justice, and rescue the violently oppressed from the oppressor's hand. Do not distress the stranger, the orphan, or the widow, or oppress them unjustly, and do not shed innocent blood in this place.

22:4  LA: Si enim facientes feceritis verbum istud, ingredientur per portas domus hujus reges sedentes de genere David super thronum ejus, et ascendentes currus et equos, ipsi, et servi, et populus eorum.
      EN: For if you truly carry out this word, kings of David's line sitting on his throne will enter through this house's gates, riding in chariots and on horses, they, their servants, and their people.

22:5  LA: Quod si non audieritis verba hæc : in memetipso juravi, dicit Dominus, quia in solitudinem erit domus hæc.
      EN: But if you do not hear these words, I have sworn by Myself, says the Lord, that this house will become a wilderness.

22:6  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus super domum regis Juda : Galaad, tu mihi caput Libani, si non posuero te solitudinem, urbes inhabitabiles !
      EN: For thus says the Lord concerning the house of Judah's king: You are Gilead to Me, the summit of Lebanon; surely I will make you a wilderness, uninhabitable cities!

22:7  LA: Et sanctificabo super te, interficientem virum et arma ejus : et succident electas cedros tuas, et præcipitabunt in ignem.
      EN: I will consecrate destroyers against you, each man with his weapons. They will cut down your choice cedars and throw them into the fire.

22:8  LA: Et pertransibunt gentes multæ per civitatem hanc, et dicet unusquisque proximo suo : Quare fecit Dominus sic civitati huic grandi ?
      EN: Many nations will pass by this city, and each will say to his neighbor, ‘Why has the Lord done this to this great city?’

22:9  LA: Et respondebunt : Eo quod dereliquerint pactum Domini Dei sui, et adoraverint deos alienos, et servierint eis.
      EN: They will answer, ‘Because they abandoned the covenant of the Lord their God and worshiped and served foreign gods.’

22:10  LA: Nolite flere mortuum, neque lugeatis super eum fletu : plangite eum qui egreditur, quia non revertetur ultra, nec videbit terram nativitatis suæ.
       EN: Do not weep for the dead or mourn over him with tears. Lament for the one who goes away, for he will never return or see his native land.

22:11  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus ad Sellum, filium Josiæ, regem Juda, qui regnavit pro Josia patre suo, qui egressus est de loco isto : Non revertetur huc amplius,
       EN: For thus says the Lord concerning Shallum son of Josiah, king of Judah, who reigned in place of his father Josiah and left this place: He will not return here again.

22:12  LA: sed in loco ad quem transtuli eum, ibi morietur, et terram istam non videbit amplius.
       EN: Instead, he will die in the place to which I have deported him and will never see this land again.

22:13  LA: Væ qui ædificat domum suam in injustitia, et cœnacula sua non in judicio : amicum suum opprimet frustra, et mercedem ejus non reddet ei :
       EN: Woe to the one who builds his house in injustice and his upper rooms without justice, who forces his friend to work for nothing and does not pay his wages;

22:14  LA: qui dicit : Ædificabo mihi domum latam, et cœnacula spatiosa : qui aperit sibi fenestras et facit laquearia cedrina, pingitque sinopide.
       EN: who says, ‘I will build myself a spacious house with roomy upper chambers’; who opens windows for himself, makes cedar ceilings, and paints them with red ochre.

22:15  LA: Numquid regnabis quoniam confers te cedro ? pater tuus numquid non comedit et bibit, et fecit judicium et justitiam tunc cum bene erat ei ?
       EN: Will you reign because you compare yourself with cedar? Did your father not eat and drink and practice judgment and justice when it went well with him?

22:16  LA: Judicavit causam pauperis et egeni in bonum suum : numquid non ideo quia cognovit me ? dicit Dominus.
       EN: He judged the cause of the poor and needy to his own good. Was that not because he knew Me? says the Lord.

22:17  LA: Tui vero oculi et cor ad avaritiam, et ad sanguinem innocentem fundendum, et ad calumniam, et ad cursum mali operis.
       EN: But your eyes and your heart are set on greed, on shedding innocent blood, on oppression, and on pursuing evil deeds.

22:18  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus ad Joakim, filium Josiæ, regem Juda : Non plangent eum : Væ frater ! et væ soror ! non concrepabunt ei : Væ domine ! et væ inclyte !
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord concerning Jehoiakim son of Josiah, king of Judah: They will not lament him, ‘Alas, brother! Alas, sister!’ They will not cry aloud for him, ‘Alas, lord! Alas, glorious one!’

22:19  LA: Sepultura asini sepelietur, putrefactus et projectus extra portas Jerusalem.]
       EN: He will be buried with a donkey's burial, rotting and thrown outside Jerusalem's gates.

22:20  LA: [Ascende Libanum, et clama, et in Basan da vocem tuam : et clama ad transeuntes, quia contriti sunt omnes amatores tui.
       EN: Go up to Lebanon and cry; raise your voice in Bashan. Cry to those passing by, for all your lovers have been crushed.

22:21  LA: Locutus sum ad te in abundantia tua, et dixisti : Non audiam : hæc est via tua ab adolescentia tua, quia non audisti vocem meam.
       EN: I spoke to you in your prosperity, and you said, ‘I will not listen.’ This has been your way from your youth, for you have not listened to My voice.

22:22  LA: Omnes pastores tuos pascet ventus, et amatores tui in captivitatem ibunt : et tunc confunderis, et erubesces ab omni malitia tua.
       EN: The wind will pasture all your shepherds, and your lovers will go into captivity. Then you will be ashamed and humiliated because of all your wickedness.

22:23  LA: Quæ sedes in Libano, et nidificas in cedris, quomodo congemuisti cum venissent tibi dolores, quasi dolores parturientis ?
       EN: You who sit in Lebanon and nest in the cedars, how you groaned when pains came upon you, like the pains of a woman in labor!

22:24  LA: Vivo ego, dicit Dominus, quia si fuerit Jechonias filius Joakim regis Juda annulus in manu dextera mea, inde evellam eum,
       EN: As I live, says the Lord, even if Jeconiah son of Jehoiakim, king of Judah, were a signet ring on My right hand, I would tear him from there.

22:25  LA: et dabo te in manu quærentium animam tuam, et in manu quorum tu formidas faciem, et in manu Nabuchodonosor regis Babylonis, et in manu Chaldæorum :
       EN: I will give you into the hands of those seeking your life, into the hands of those whose faces you fear, into the hand of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon and the hands of the Chaldeans.

22:26  LA: et mittam te, et matrem tuam quæ genuit te, in terram alienam, in qua nati non estis, ibique moriemini.
       EN: I will cast you and the mother who bore you into a foreign land where you were not born, and there you will die.

22:27  LA: Et in terram ad quam ipsi levant animam suam ut revertantur illuc, non revertentur.
       EN: They will not return to the land to which they lift their souls in longing to return.

22:28  LA: Numquid vas fictile atque contritum vir iste Jechonias ? numquid vas absque omni voluptate ? quare abjecti sunt ipse et semen ejus, et projecti in terram quam ignoraverunt ?
       EN: Is this man Jeconiah an earthen vessel, broken, a vessel that gives no pleasure at all? Why have he and his offspring been cast away, thrown into a land they did not know?

22:29  LA: Terra, terra, terra, audi sermonem Domini.
       EN: Earth, earth, earth, hear the word of the Lord!

22:30  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Scribe virum istum sterilem, virum qui in diebus suis non prosperabitur : nec enim erit de semine ejus vir qui sedeat super solium David, et potestatem habeat ultra in Juda.]
       EN: Thus says the Lord: Record this man as childless, a man who will not prosper in his days, for no man from his offspring will sit on David's throne and rule again in Judah.”

JEREMIAH 23

23:1  LA: Væ pastoribus qui disperdunt et dilacerant gregem pascuæ meæ ! dicit Dominus.
      EN: “Woe to the shepherds who destroy and tear apart the flock of My pasture! says the Lord.

23:2  LA: Ideo hæc dicit Dominus Deus Israël ad pastores qui pascunt populum meum : Vos dispersistis gregem meum, et ejecistis eos, et non visitastis eos : ecce ego visitabo super vos malitiam studiorum vestrorum, ait Dominus.
      EN: Therefore thus says the Lord, the God of Israel, to the shepherds who pasture My people: You have scattered My flock and driven them out, and have not visited them. Look, I will visit upon you the wickedness of your deeds, says the Lord.

23:3  LA: Et ego congregabo reliquias gregis mei de omnibus terris ad quas ejecero eos illuc : et convertam eos ad rura sua, et crescent et multiplicabuntur.
      EN: I Myself will gather the remnant of My flock from all the lands to which I have driven them, bring them back to their pastures, and they will grow and multiply.
