23:4  LA: Et suscitabo super eos pastores, et pascent eos : non formidabunt ultra, et non pavebunt, et nullus quæretur ex numero, dicit Dominus.]
      EN: I will raise up shepherds over them, and they will pasture them. They will fear and tremble no more, and none will be missing from their number, says the Lord.

23:5  LA: [Ecce dies veniunt, dicit Dominus, et suscitabo David germen justum : et regnabit rex, et sapiens erit, et faciet judicium et justitiam in terra.
      EN: Look, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will raise up a righteous shoot for David. A king will reign and be wise; he will practice judgment and justice in the land.

23:6  LA: In diebus illis salvabitur Juda, et Israël habitabit confidenter : et hoc est nomen quod vocabunt eum : Dominus justus noster.
      EN: In those days Judah will be saved and Israel will dwell in confidence. This is the name they will call him: ‘The Lord, Our Righteous One.’

23:7  LA: Propter hoc ecce dies veniunt, dicit Dominus, et non dicent ultra : Vivit Dominus, qui eduxit filios Israël de terra Ægypti,
      EN: Therefore, look, days are coming, says the Lord, when they will no longer say, ‘As the Lord lives, who brought the children of Israel out of the land of Egypt,’

23:8  LA: sed : Vivit Dominus, qui eduxit et adduxit semen domus Israël de terra aquilonis, et de cunctis terris ad quas ejeceram eos illuc, et habitabunt in terra sua.]
      EN: but, ‘As the Lord lives, who brought out and led back the offspring of the house of Israel from the northern land and from all the lands to which I had driven them.’ And they will dwell in their own land.”

23:9  LA: Ad prophetas : [Contritum est cor meum in medio mei ; contremuerunt omnia ossa mea : factus sum quasi vir ebrius, et quasi homo madidus a vino, a facie Domini, et a facie verborum sanctorum ejus.
      EN: Concerning the prophets: My heart is broken within me; all my bones have trembled. I have become like a drunken man, like a man soaked with wine, before the Lord and His holy words.

23:10  LA: Quia adulteris repleta est terra, quia a facie maledictionis luxit terra, arefacta sunt arva deserti : factus est cursus eorum malus, et fortitudo eorum dissimilis.
       EN: For the land is full of adulterers; because of the curse the land has mourned, and the fields of the wilderness have dried up. Their course has become evil, and their strength is unlike what it should be.

23:11  LA: Propheta namque et sacerdos polluti sunt, et in domo mea inveni malum eorum, ait Dominus.
       EN: “For prophet and priest alike are defiled; even in My house I have found their evil, says the Lord.

23:12  LA: Idcirco via eorum erit quasi lubricum in tenebris : impellentur enim, et corruent in ea : afferam enim super eos mala, annum visitationis eorum, ait Dominus.
       EN: Therefore their way will be like slippery ground in darkness; they will be driven on and fall there. For I will bring disasters upon them, the year of their punishment, says the Lord.

23:13  LA: Et in prophetis Samariæ vidi fatuitatem : prophetabant in Baal, et decipiebant populum meum Israël.
       EN: Among Samaria's prophets I saw folly: they prophesied by Baal and deceived My people Israel.

23:14  LA: Et in prophetis Jerusalem vidi similitudinem adulterantium, et iter mendacii : et confortaverunt manus pessimorum, ut non converteretur unusquisque a malitia sua : facti sunt mihi omnes ut Sodoma, et habitatores ejus quasi Gomorrha.
       EN: Among Jerusalem's prophets I saw the likeness of adulterers and a path of falsehood. They strengthened the hands of the most wicked, so that no one turned from his wickedness. They have all become like Sodom to Me, and its inhabitants like Gomorrah.

23:15  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus exercituum ad prophetas : Ecce ego cibabo eos absinthio, et potabo eos felle : a prophetis enim Jerusalem egressa est pollutio super omnem terram.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord of hosts concerning the prophets: Look, I will feed them wormwood and give them gall to drink, for from Jerusalem's prophets defilement has gone out over the whole land.

23:16  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Nolite audire verba prophetarum qui prophetant vobis, et decipiunt vos : visionem cordis sui loquuntur, non de ore Domini.
       EN: Thus says the Lord of hosts: Do not listen to the words of the prophets who prophesy to you and deceive you. They speak a vision of their own hearts, not from the Lord's mouth.

23:17  LA: Dicunt his qui blasphemant me : Locutus est Dominus : Pax erit vobis : et omni qui ambulat in pravitate cordis sui dixerunt : Non veniet super vos malum.
       EN: They say to those who blaspheme Me, ‘The Lord has spoken: You will have peace.’ To everyone who walks in the perversity of his heart they have said, ‘No disaster will come upon you.’

23:18  LA: Quis enim affuit in consilio Domini, et vidit, et audivit sermonem ejus ? quis consideravit verbum illius, et audivit ?
       EN: For who has been present in the Lord's council and seen and heard His word? Who has considered His word and heard it?

23:19  LA: Ecce turbo Dominicæ indignationis egredietur, et tempestas erumpens super caput impiorum veniet.
       EN: Look, the whirlwind of the Lord's indignation will go forth; a bursting storm will come upon the heads of the wicked.

23:20  LA: Non revertetur furor Domini, usque dum faciat et usque dum compleat cogitationem cordis sui : in novissimis diebus intelligetis consilium ejus.
       EN: The Lord's fury will not turn back until He has accomplished and fulfilled the purpose of His heart. In the last days you will understand His counsel.

23:21  LA: Non mittebam prophetas, et ipsi currebant : non loquebar ad eos, et ipsi prophetabant.
       EN: I did not send the prophets, yet they ran; I did not speak to them, yet they prophesied.

23:22  LA: Si stetissent in consilio meo, et nota fecissent verba mea populo meo, avertissem utique eos a via sua mala, et a cogitationibus suis pessimis.
       EN: If they had stood in My council and made My words known to My people, I would surely have turned them from their evil way and their most wicked thoughts.

23:23  LA: Putasne Deus e vicino ego sum, dicit Dominus, et non Deus de longe ?
       EN: Do you think I am a God nearby, says the Lord, and not a God far away?

23:24  LA: Si occultabitur vir in absconditis, et ego non videbo eum ? dicit Dominus. Numquid non cælum et terram ego impleo ? dicit Dominus.
       EN: If a man hides in secret places, shall I not see him? says the Lord. Do I not fill heaven and earth? says the Lord.

23:25  LA: Audivi quæ dixerunt prophetæ prophetantes in nomine meo mendacium, atque dicentes : Somniavi, somniavi.
       EN: I have heard what the prophets say who prophesy lies in My name, saying, ‘I have dreamed, I have dreamed!’

23:26  LA: Usquequo istud est in corde prophetarum vaticinantium mendacium, et prophetantium seductiones cordis sui ?
       EN: How long will this be in the hearts of the prophets who prophesy falsehood and the deceptions of their own hearts?

23:27  LA: Qui volunt facere ut obliviscatur populus meus nominis mei, propter somnia eorum quæ narrat unusquisque ad proximum suum, sicut obliti sunt patres eorum nominis mei propter Baal ?
       EN: They want to make My people forget My name through the dreams that each tells his neighbor, just as their fathers forgot My name because of Baal.

23:28  LA: Propheta qui habet somnium, narret somnium : et qui habet sermonem meum, loquatur sermonem meum vere. Quid paleis ad triticum ? dicit Dominus.
       EN: Let the prophet who has a dream tell his dream, and let the one who has My word speak My word truthfully. What has chaff to do with wheat? says the Lord.

23:29  LA: Numquid non verba mea sunt quasi ignis, dicit Dominus, et quasi malleus conterens petram ?
       EN: Are My words not like fire, says the Lord, and like a hammer shattering rock?

23:30  LA: Propterea ecce ego ad prophetas, ait Dominus, qui furantur verba mea unusquisque a proximo suo.
       EN: Therefore, look, I am against the prophets, says the Lord, who steal My words, each from his neighbor.

23:31  LA: Ecce ego ad prophetas, ait Dominus, qui assumunt linguas suas, et aiunt : Dicit Dominus.
       EN: Look, I am against the prophets, says the Lord, who wield their tongues and say, ‘The Lord says.’

23:32  LA: Ecce ego ad prophetas somniantes mendacium, ait Dominus, qui narraverunt ea, et seduxerunt populum meum in mendacio suo et in miraculis suis, cum ego non misissem eos, nec mandassem eis : qui nihil profuerunt populo huic, dicit Dominus.
       EN: Look, I am against the prophets who dream falsehood, says the Lord, who recount these dreams and mislead My people by their falsehood and their wonders, though I did not send or command them. They have done this people no good at all, says the Lord.

23:33  LA: Si igitur interrogaverit te populus iste, vel propheta, aut sacerdos, dicens : Quod est onus Domini ? dices ad eos : Vos estis onus : projiciam quippe vos, dicit Dominus.
       EN: So if this people, a prophet, or a priest asks you, ‘What is the Lord's burden?’ you shall tell them, ‘You are the burden, and I will cast you off, says the Lord.’

23:34  LA: Et propheta, et sacerdos, et populus qui dicit : Onus Domini : visitabo super virum illum et super domum ejus.
       EN: As for the prophet, priest, or person who says, ‘The Lord's burden,’ I will punish that man and his household.

23:35  LA: Hæc dicetis unusquisque ad proximum, et ad fratrem suum : Quid respondit Dominus ? et quid locutus est Dominus ?
       EN: This is what each of you shall say to his neighbor and his brother: ‘What has the Lord answered? What has the Lord spoken?’

23:36  LA: Et onus Domini ultra non memorabitur : quia onus erit unicuique sermo suus, et pervertistis verba Dei viventis, Domini exercituum, Dei nostri.
       EN: The Lord's burden shall no longer be mentioned, for each one's own word will be his burden, and you have perverted the words of the living God, the Lord of hosts, our God.

23:37  LA: Hæc dices ad prophetam : Quid respondit tibi Dominus ? et quid locutus est Dominus ?
       EN: This is what you shall say to the prophet: ‘What has the Lord answered you? What has the Lord spoken?’

23:38  LA: Si autem onus Domini dixeritis, propter hoc hæc dicit Dominus : Quia dixistis sermonem istum : Onus Domini, et misi ad vos dicens : Nolite dicere : Onus Domini :
       EN: But if you say, ‘The Lord's burden,’ therefore thus says the Lord: Because you have said these words, ‘The Lord's burden,’ though I sent you the message, ‘Do not say, “The Lord's burden,”’

23:39  LA: propterea ecce ego tollam vos portans, et derelinquam vos, et civitatem quam dedi vobis et patribus vestris, a facie mea :
       EN: therefore, look, I will lift you up and carry you away, casting you from My presence, you and the city that I gave you and your fathers.

23:40  LA: et dabo vos in opprobrium sempiternum, et in ignominiam æternam, quæ numquam oblivione delebitur.]
       EN: I will give you everlasting reproach and eternal disgrace that will never be erased by forgetfulness.”

JEREMIAH 24

24:1  LA: Ostendit mihi Dominus : et ecce duo calathi pleni ficis, positi ante templum Domini, postquam transtulit Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis Jechoniam filium Joakim, regem Juda, et principes ejus, et fabrum, et inclusorem, de Jerusalem, et adduxit eos in Babylonem.
      EN: The Lord showed me two baskets full of figs set before the Lord's temple, after Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon had deported Jeconiah son of Jehoiakim, king of Judah, his rulers, the craftsmen, and the metalworkers from Jerusalem and brought them to Babylon.

24:2  LA: Calathus unus ficus bonas habebat nimis, ut solent ficus esse primi temporis : et calathus unus ficus habebat malas nimis, quæ comedi non poterant eo quod essent malæ.
      EN: One basket had very good figs, like the first figs of the season; the other basket had very bad figs, too bad to eat.

24:3  LA: Et dixit Dominus ad me : Quid tu vides, Jeremia ? Et dixi : Ficus, ficus bonas, bonas valde : et malas, malas valde, quæ comedi non possunt eo quod sint malæ.
      EN: The Lord said to me, “What do you see, Jeremiah?” I said, “Figs: good figs, very good, and bad figs, very bad, too bad to eat.”

24:4  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to me, saying:

24:5  LA: [Hæc dicit Dominus Deus Israël : Sicut ficus hæ bonæ, sic cognoscam transmigrationem Juda, quam emisi de loco isto in terram Chaldæorum, in bonum.
      EN: “Thus says the Lord, the God of Israel: Like these good figs, so will I regard favorably the exiles of Judah whom I sent from this place into the land of the Chaldeans.

24:6  LA: Et ponam oculos meos super eos ad placandum, et reducam eos in terram hanc : et ædificabo eos, et non destruam : et plantabo eos, et non evellam.
      EN: I will set My eyes upon them with favor and bring them back to this land. I will build them up and not destroy them; I will plant them and not uproot them.

24:7  LA: Et dabo eis cor ut sciant me, quia ego sum Dominus : et erunt mihi in populum, et ego ero eis in Deum, quia revertentur ad me in toto corde suo.
      EN: I will give them a heart to know Me, that I am the Lord. They will be My people and I will be their God, for they will return to Me with their whole heart.

24:8  LA: Et sicut ficus pessimæ quæ comedi non possunt eo quod sint malæ, hæc dicit Dominus : Sic dabo Sedeciam regem Juda, et principes ejus, et reliquos de Jerusalem, qui remanserunt in urbe hac, et qui habitant in terra Ægypti.
      EN: But like the very bad figs, too bad to eat, thus says the Lord: So will I make Zedekiah king of Judah, his rulers, and the remnant of Jerusalem who remain in this city and those who live in the land of Egypt.

24:9  LA: Et dabo eos in vexationem, afflictionemque omnibus regnis terræ, in opprobrium, et in parabolam, et in proverbium, et in maledictionem in universis locis ad quæ ejeci eos.
      EN: I will make them an object of harassment and affliction to all the kingdoms of the earth, a reproach, a tale, a proverb, and a curse in all the places to which I have driven them.

24:10  LA: Et mittam in eis gladium, et famem, et pestem, donec consumantur de terra quam dedi eis et patribus eorum.]
       EN: I will send sword, famine, and pestilence among them until they are consumed from the land that I gave them and their fathers.”

JEREMIAH 25

25:1  LA: Verbum quod factum est ad Jeremiam, de omni populo Juda, in anno quarto Joakim filii Josiæ regis Juda (ipse est annus primus Nabuchodonosor regis Babylonis),
      EN: The word that came to Jeremiah concerning all the people of Judah in the fourth year of Jehoiakim son of Josiah, king of Judah, which was the first year of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon,

25:2  LA: quod locutus est Jeremias propheta ad omnem populum Juda, et ad universos habitatores Jerusalem, dicens :
      EN: which Jeremiah the prophet spoke to all the people of Judah and all Jerusalem's inhabitants, saying:

25:3  LA: A tertiodecimo anno Josiæ filii Amon regis Juda, usque ad diem hanc, iste tertius et vigesimus annus, factum est verbum Domini ad me, et locutus sum ad vos, de nocte consurgens et loquens, et non audistis.
      EN: “From the thirteenth year of Josiah son of Amon, king of Judah, until this day, these twenty-three years, the word of the Lord has come to me. I have spoken to you, rising in the night and speaking, but you have not listened.

25:4  LA: Et misit Dominus ad vos omnes servos suos prophetas, consurgens diluculo, mittensque : et non audistis, neque inclinastis aures vestras ut audiretis,
      EN: The Lord sent you all His servants the prophets, rising at dawn and sending them, but you did not listen or incline your ears to hear,

25:5  LA: cum diceret : Revertimini unusquisque a via sua mala, et a pessimis cogitationibus vestris, et habitabitis in terra quam dedit Dominus vobis et patribus vestris, a sæculo et usque in sæculum :
      EN: when He said: Turn back, each of you, from your evil way and your most wicked thoughts, and dwell in the land that the Lord gave you and your fathers from ages past and forever.

25:6  LA: et nolite ire post deos alienos, ut serviatis eis, adoretisque eos : neque me ad iracundiam provocetis in operibus manuum vestrarum, et non affligam vos.
      EN: Do not follow foreign gods to serve and worship them; do not provoke Me to anger with the works of your hands, and I will not afflict you.

25:7  LA: Et non audistis me, dicit Dominus, ut me ad iracundiam provocaretis in operibus manuum vestrarum, in malum vestrum.
      EN: But you have not listened to Me, says the Lord, provoking Me to anger with the works of your hands, to your own harm.

25:8  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Pro eo quod non audistis verba mea,
      EN: Therefore thus says the Lord of hosts: Because you have not listened to My words,

25:9  LA: ecce ego mittam et assumam universas cognationes aquilonis, ait Dominus, et Nabuchodonosor regem Babylonis servum meum, et adducam eos super terram istam, et super habitatores ejus, et super omnes nationes quæ in circuitu illius sunt : et interficiam eos, et ponam eos in stuporem et in sibilum, et in solitudines sempiternas.
      EN: look, I will send and bring all the clans of the north, says the Lord, and Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon, My servant. I will bring them against this land, its inhabitants, and all the surrounding nations; I will kill them and make them an object of horror and hissing, everlasting ruins.

25:10  LA: Perdamque ex eis vocem gaudii et vocem lætitiæ, vocem sponsi et vocem sponsæ, vocem molæ et lumen lucernæ.
       EN: I will remove from them the voice of joy and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom and the voice of the bride, the sound of the millstone and the light of the lamp.

25:11  LA: Et erit universa terra hæc in solitudinem, et in stuporem : et servient omnes gentes istæ regi Babylonis septuaginta annis.
       EN: This whole land will become a desolation and an object of horror, and all these nations will serve the king of Babylon for seventy years.

25:12  LA: Cumque impleti fuerint septuaginta anni, visitabo super regem Babylonis et super gentem illam, dicit Dominus, iniquitatem eorum, et super terram Chaldæorum, et ponam illam in solitudines sempiternas.
       EN: When seventy years have been completed, I will punish the king of Babylon and that nation for their iniquity, says the Lord, and the land of the Chaldeans; I will make it everlasting ruins.

25:13  LA: Et adducam super terram illam omnia verba mea, quæ locutus sum contra eam, omne quod scriptum est in libro isto, quæcumque prophetavit Jeremias adversum omnes gentes :
       EN: I will bring upon that land all My words that I spoke against it, everything written in this book, whatever Jeremiah prophesied against all the nations,

25:14  LA: Quod facum est verbum Domini ad Jeremiam prophetam adversus Ælam, in principio regni Sedeciæ regis Juda, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord that came to Jeremiah the prophet against Elam, at the beginning of the reign of Zedekiah king of Judah:

25:15  LA: [Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Ecce ego confringam arcum Ælam, et summam fortitudinem eorum :
       EN: “Thus says the Lord of hosts: Behold, I will break the bow of Elam, the chief strength of their might.

25:16  LA: et inducam super Ælam quatuor ventos a quatuor plagis cæli, et ventilabo eos in omnes ventos istos, et non erit gens ad quam non perveniant profugi Ælam.
       EN: I will bring upon Elam four winds from the four quarters of heaven and scatter them to all these winds. There will be no nation to which the fugitives of Elam do not come.

25:17  LA: Et pavere faciam Ælam coram inimicis suis, et in conspectu quærentium animam eorum : et adducam super eos malum, iram furoris mei, dicit Dominus, et mittam post eos gladium donec consumam eos.
       EN: I will make Elam tremble before their enemies and before those who seek their lives. I will bring calamity upon them, My fierce anger, says the Lord, and send the sword after them until I consume them.

25:18  LA: Et ponam solium meum in Ælam, et perdam inde reges et principes, ait Dominus.
       EN: I will set My throne in Elam and destroy her kings and rulers, says the Lord.

25:19  LA: In novissimis autem diebus reverti faciam captivos Ælam, dicit Dominus.]
       EN: But in the last days I will bring back the captives of Elam, says the Lord.”

25:20  LA: 
       EN: 

JEREMIAH 26

26:2  LA: Ad Ægyptum, adversum exercitum Pharaonis Nechao regis Ægypti, qui erat juxta fluvium Euphraten in Charcamis, quem percussit Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis, in quarto anno Joakim filii Josiæ regis Juda.
      EN: Concerning Egypt, against the army of Pharaoh Neco king of Egypt, which was by the river Euphrates at Carchemish and which Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon defeated in the fourth year of Jehoiakim son of Josiah, king of Judah:

26:3  LA: [Præparate scutum et clypeum, et procedite ad bellum.
      EN: “Prepare shield and buckler, and advance to battle!

26:4  LA: Jungite equos, et ascendite, equites : state in galeis, polite lanceas, induite vos loricis.
      EN: Harness the horses and mount, horsemen! Take your positions in helmets, polish your spears, put on your coats of armor!

26:5  LA: Quid igitur ? vidi ipsos pavidos, et terga vertentes, fortes eorum cæsos : fugerunt conciti, nec respexerunt : terror undique, ait Dominus.
      EN: What is this? I have seen them terrified and turning their backs, their mighty men struck down. They fled in haste without looking back. Terror is on every side, says the Lord.

26:6  LA: Non fugiat velox, nec salvari se putet fortis : ad aquilonem juxta flumen Euphraten victi sunt, et ruerunt.
      EN: Let not the swift flee, nor the mighty think to save himself. In the north, beside the river Euphrates, they were defeated and fell.

26:7  LA: Quis est iste, qui quasi flumen ascendit, et veluti fluviorum intumescunt gurgites ejus ?
      EN: Who is this rising like a river, whose floods swell like streams?

26:8  LA: Ægyptus fluminis instar ascendit, et velut flumina movebuntur fluctus ejus, et dicet : Ascendens operiam terram : perdam civitatem, et habitatores ejus.
      EN: Egypt rises like a river, and its waves will surge like streams. It will say, ‘I will rise and cover the earth; I will destroy the city and its inhabitants.’

26:9  LA: Ascendite equos, et exsultate in curribus, et procedant fortes, Æthiopia et Libyes tenentes scutum, et Lydii arripientes et jacientes sagittas.
      EN: Mount the horses and exult in the chariots! Let the mighty advance: Ethiopia and the Libyans who hold the shield, and the Lydians who take up and shoot arrows.

26:10  LA: Dies autem ille Domini Dei exercituum dies ultionis, ut sumat vindictam de inimicis suis : devorabit gladius, et saturabitur, et inebriabitur sanguine eorum : victima enim Domini Dei exercituum in terra aquilonis juxta flumen Euphraten.
       EN: But that day belongs to the Lord God of hosts, a day of vengeance, when He takes vengeance upon His enemies. The sword will devour, be sated, and be drunk with their blood, for the Lord God of hosts has a sacrifice in the land of the north, beside the river Euphrates.

26:11  LA: Ascende in Galaad, et tolle resinam, virgo filia Ægypti : frustra multiplicas medicamina : sanitas non erit tibi.
       EN: Go up to Gilead and take balm, virgin daughter of Egypt. In vain you multiply remedies; there will be no healing for you.

26:12  LA: Audierunt gentes ignominiam tuam, et ululatus tuus replevit terram : quia fortis impegit in fortem, et ambo pariter conciderunt.]
       EN: The nations have heard of your disgrace, and your wailing has filled the earth, for one mighty man has stumbled against another, and both have fallen together.”

26:13  LA: Verbum quod locutus est Dominus ad Jeremiam prophetam, super eo quod venturus esset Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis, et percussurus terram Ægypti :
       EN: The word the Lord spoke to Jeremiah the prophet concerning the coming of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon to strike the land of Egypt:

26:14  LA: [Annuntiate Ægypto, et auditum facite in Magdalo, et resonet in Memphis, et in Taphnis : dicite : Sta, et præpara te, quia devorabit gladius ea quæ per circuitum tuum sunt.
       EN: “Proclaim it in Egypt, make it heard in Migdol, let it resound in Memphis and Tahpanhes. Say, ‘Stand ready and prepare yourself, for the sword will devour everything around you.’

26:15  LA: Quare computruit fortis tuus ? non stetit, quoniam Dominus subvertit eum.
       EN: Why has your mighty one wasted away? He did not stand, because the Lord overthrew him.

26:16  LA: Multiplicavit ruentes, ceciditque vir ad proximum suum, et dicent : Surge, et revertamur ad populum nostrum, et ad terram nativitatis nostræ, a facie gladii columbæ.
       EN: He multiplied the fallen; one man fell against another, and they will say, ‘Rise, let us return to our people and to the land of our birth, away from the sword of the dove.’

26:17  LA: Vocate nomen Pharaonis regis Ægypti : tumultum adduxit tempus.
       EN: Call Pharaoh king of Egypt by this name: ‘Time has brought tumult.’

26:18  LA: Vivo ego, inquit Rex (Dominus exercituum nomen ejus), quoniam sicut Thabor in montibus, et sicut Carmelus in mari, veniet.
       EN: As I live, declares the King, whose name is the Lord of hosts, he will come like Tabor among the mountains and like Carmel beside the sea.

26:19  LA: Vasa transmigrationis fac tibi, habitatrix filia Ægypti : quia Memphis in solitudinem erit, et deseretur, et inhabitabilis erit.
       EN: Prepare baggage for exile, daughter dwelling in Egypt, for Memphis will become a wilderness, deserted and uninhabitable.

26:20  LA: Vitula elegans atque formosa Ægyptus, stimulator ab aquilone veniet ei.
       EN: Egypt is an elegant and beautiful heifer; a goader will come against her from the north.

26:21  LA: Mercenarii quoque ejus, qui versabantur in medio ejus quasi vituli saginati, versi sunt, et fugerunt simul, nec stare potuerunt : quia dies interfectionis eorum venit super eos, tempus visitationis eorum.
       EN: Her mercenaries who lived in her midst like fattened calves have also turned and fled together. They could not stand, for the day of their slaughter has come upon them, the time of their visitation.

26:22  LA: Vox ejus quasi æris sonabit : quoniam cum exercitu properabunt, et cum securibus venient ei quasi cædentes ligna.
       EN: Her voice will ring like bronze, for they will hurry with an army and come against her with axes like woodcutters.

26:23  LA: Succiderunt saltum ejus, ait Dominus, qui supputari non potest : multiplicati sunt super locustas, et non est eis numerus.
       EN: They have cut down her forest, says the Lord, which cannot be counted, for they are more numerous than locusts and are beyond number.

26:24  LA: Confusa est filia Ægypti, et tradita in manu populi aquilonis.
       EN: The daughter of Egypt has been put to shame and delivered into the hand of the people of the north.

26:25  LA: Dixit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Ecce ego visitabo super tumultum Alexandriæ, et super Pharaonem, et super Ægyptum, et super deos ejus, et super reges ejus, et super Pharaonem, et super eos qui confidunt in eo :
       EN: The Lord of hosts, the God of Israel, has said: Behold, I will call to account the tumult of Alexandria, Pharaoh, Egypt, her gods, her kings, Pharaoh, and those who trust in him.

26:27  LA: Et tu ne timeas, serve meus Jacob, et ne paveas, Israël : quia ecce ego salvum te faciam de longinquo, et semen tuum de terra captivitatis tuæ : et revertetur Jacob, et requiescet, et prosperabitur, et non erit qui exterreat eum.
       EN: But you, do not fear, My servant Jacob, and do not be dismayed, Israel. For behold, I will save you from far away and your descendants from the land of your captivity. Jacob will return, rest, and prosper, with no one to terrify him.

26:28  LA: Et tu noli timere, serve meus Jacob, ait Dominus, quia tecum ego sum : quia ego consumam cunctas gentes ad quas ejeci te, te vero non consumam : sed castigabo te in judicio, nec quasi innocenti parcam tibi.]
       EN: Do not fear, My servant Jacob, says the Lord, for I am with you. I will consume all the nations to which I drove you, but I will not consume you. I will discipline you with justice and will not spare you as though you were innocent.”

JEREMIAH 27

27:1  LA: Verbum quod locutus est Dominus de Babylone et de terra Chaldæorum, in manu Jeremiæ prophetæ.
      EN: The word the Lord spoke concerning Babylon and the land of the Chaldeans through Jeremiah the prophet:

27:2  LA: [Annuntiate in gentibus, et auditum facite : levate signum, prædicate, et nolite celare : dicite : Capta est Babylon, confusus est Bel, victus est Merodach, confusa sunt sculptilia ejus, superata sunt idola eorum.
      EN: “Proclaim it among the nations and make it heard. Raise a banner, announce it, and do not conceal it. Say, ‘Babylon has been captured, Bel put to shame, Merodach defeated. Her carved images are ashamed, and their idols overcome.’

27:3  LA: Quoniam ascendit contra eam gens ab aquilone, quæ ponet terram ejus in solitudinem, et non erit qui habitet in ea ab homine usque ad pecus : et moti sunt, et abierunt.
      EN: For a nation has come up against her from the north, which will make her land a wilderness. No one will dwell in it, neither human nor animal; they have moved away and departed.

27:4  LA: In diebus illis, et in tempore illo, ait Dominus, venient filii Israël ipsi et filii Juda simul : ambulantes et flentes properabunt, et Dominum Deum suum quærent :
      EN: In those days and at that time, says the Lord, the children of Israel and the children of Judah will come together. They will hasten along weeping and seek the Lord their God.

27:5  LA: in Sion interrogabunt viam, huc facies eorum : venient, et apponentur ad Dominum fœdere sempiterno, quod nulla oblivione delebitur.
      EN: They will ask the way to Zion, their faces turned toward it. They will come and join themselves to the Lord in an everlasting covenant that no forgetfulness will erase.

27:6  LA: Grex perditus factus est populus meus : pastores eorum seduxerunt eos, feceruntque vagari in montibus : de monte in collem transierunt ; obliti sunt cubilis sui.
      EN: My people have become a lost flock. Their shepherds led them astray and made them wander on the mountains. They passed from mountain to hill and forgot their resting place.

27:7  LA: Omnes qui invenerunt comederunt eos, et hostes eorum dixerunt : Non peccavimus : pro eo quod peccaverunt Domino decori justitiæ, et exspectationi patrum eorum Domino.
      EN: All who found them devoured them, and their enemies said, ‘We have not sinned, because they sinned against the Lord, the beauty of justice, the Lord, the hope of their fathers.’

27:8  LA: Recedite de medio Babylonis, et de terra Chaldæorum egredimini, et estote quasi hædi ante gregem.
      EN: Depart from the midst of Babylon and go out from the land of the Chaldeans. Be like young goats before the flock.

27:9  LA: Quoniam ecce ego suscito, et adducam in Babylonem congregationem gentium magnarum de terra aquilonis, et præparabuntur adversus eam, et inde capietur : sagitta ejus quasi viri fortis interfectoris : non revertetur vacua.
      EN: For behold, I am stirring up and will bring against Babylon an assembly of great nations from the land of the north. They will take their positions against her, and from there she will be captured. Their arrow is like that of a mighty killer; it will not return empty.

27:10  LA: Et erit Chaldæa in prædam : omnes vastantes eam replebuntur, ait Dominus.
       EN: Chaldea will become plunder; all who ravage her will be filled, says the Lord.

27:11  LA: Quoniam exsultatis, et magna loquimini, diripientes hæreditatem meam : quoniam effusi estis sicut vituli super herbam, et mugistis sicut tauri :
       EN: Because you exult and speak boastfully, plundering My inheritance, because you have run free like calves over the grass and bellowed like bulls,

27:12  LA: confusa est mater vestra nimis, et adæquata pulveri, quæ genuit vos : ecce novissima erit in gentibus, deserta, invia, et arens.
       EN: your mother is deeply ashamed, and she who bore you is brought down to the dust. Behold, she will be last among the nations, a deserted, trackless, parched land.

27:13  LA: Ab ira Domini non habitabitur, sed redigetur tota in solitudinem : omnis qui transibit per Babylonem stupebit, et sibilabit super universis plagis ejus.
       EN: Because of the Lord’s anger she will not be inhabited, but will be reduced entirely to a wilderness. Everyone who passes through Babylon will be astonished and hiss at all her wounds.

27:14  LA: Præparamini contra Babylonem per circuitum, omnes qui tenditis arcum : debellate eam, non parcatis jaculis, quia Domino peccavit.
       EN: Take your positions against Babylon on every side, all you who bend the bow. Make war upon her; spare no arrows, for she has sinned against the Lord.

27:15  LA: Clamate adversus eam, ubique dedit manum : ceciderunt fundamenta ejus, destructi sunt muri ejus, quoniam ultio Domini est : ultionem accipite de ea : sicut fecit, facite ei.
       EN: Shout against her all around! She has surrendered; her foundations have fallen and her walls been destroyed, for this is the Lord’s vengeance. Take vengeance upon her; as she has done, do to her.

27:16  LA: Disperdite satorem de Babylone, et tenentem falcem in tempore messis : a facie gladii columbæ unusquisque ad populum suum convertetur, et singuli ad terram suam fugient.
       EN: Destroy the sower from Babylon and the one who holds the sickle at harvesttime. Before the sword of the dove, each will turn toward his people and each flee to his own land.

27:17  LA: Grex dispersus Israël : leones ejecerunt eum. Primus comedit eum rex Assur : iste novissimus exossavit eum Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis.
       EN: Israel is a scattered flock; lions have driven him away. First the king of Assyria devoured him; last, this Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon has stripped his bones.

27:18  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Ecce ego visitabo regem Babylonis et terram ejus, sicut visitavi regem Assur :
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Behold, I will call the king of Babylon and his land to account, as I called the king of Assyria to account.

27:19  LA: et reducam Israël ad habitaculum suum : et pascetur Carmelum et Basan, et in monte Ephraim et Galaad saturabitur anima ejus.
       EN: I will bring Israel back to his dwelling. He will graze on Carmel and Bashan, and his soul will be satisfied on the mountains of Ephraim and Gilead.

27:20  LA: In diebus illis, et in tempore illo, ait Dominus, quæretur iniquitas Israël, et non erit, et peccatum Juda, et non invenietur : quoniam propitius ero eis quos reliquero.
       EN: In those days and at that time, says the Lord, the iniquity of Israel will be sought and will not exist, and the sin of Judah will not be found, for I will forgive those whom I leave as a remnant.

27:21  LA: Super terram dominantium ascende, et super habitatores ejus visita : dissipa, et interfice quæ post eos sunt, ait Dominus, et fac juxta omnia quæ præcepi tibi.
       EN: Go up against the land of the rulers, and call its inhabitants to account. Lay waste and kill what remains after them, says the Lord, and do all that I have commanded you.

27:22  LA: Vox belli in terra, et contritio magna.
       EN: The sound of war is in the land, and great destruction.

27:23  LA: Quomodo confractus est et contritus malleus universæ terræ ? quomodo versa est in desertum Babylon in gentibus ?
       EN: How the hammer of the whole earth has been broken and shattered! How Babylon has become a wilderness among the nations!

27:24  LA: Illaqueavi te, et capta es, Babylon, et nesciebas : inventa es et apprehensa, quoniam Dominum provocasti.
       EN: I have ensnared you, and you have been captured, Babylon, without knowing it. You have been found and seized, because you provoked the Lord.

27:25  LA: Aperuit Dominus thesaurum suum, et protulit vasa iræ suæ, quoniam opus est Domino Deo exercituum, in terra Chaldæorum.
       EN: The Lord has opened His storehouse and brought out the weapons of His wrath, for the Lord God of hosts has work to do in the land of the Chaldeans.

27:26  LA: Venite ad eam ab extremis finibus ; aperite ut exeant qui conculcent eam : tollite de via lapides, et redigite in acervos : et interficite eam, nec sit quidquam reliquum.
       EN: Come against her from the farthest borders. Open the way for those who trample her to come out. Remove stones from the road and pile them in heaps. Destroy her, and let nothing remain.

27:27  LA: Dissipate universos fortes ejus : descendant in occisionem : væ eis, quia venit dies eorum, tempus visitationis eorum !
       EN: Scatter all her mighty men; let them go down to slaughter. Woe to them, for their day has come, the time of their visitation!

27:28  LA: Vox fugientium, et eorum qui evaserunt de terra Babylonis, ut annuntient in Sion ultionem Domini Dei nostri, ultionem templi ejus.
       EN: Hear the voice of those fleeing and those who have escaped from the land of Babylon, to proclaim in Zion the vengeance of the Lord our God, vengeance for His temple.

27:29  LA: Annuntiate in Babylonem plurimis, omnibus qui tendunt arcum : consistite adversus eam per gyrum, et nullus evadat : reddite ei secundum opus suum : juxta omnia quæ fecit, facite illi, quia contra Dominum erecta est, adversum Sanctum Israël.
       EN: Summon many against Babylon, all who bend the bow. Take your stand against her all around, and let no one escape. Repay her according to her deeds; do to her all that she has done, for she has exalted herself against the Lord, against the Holy One of Israel.

27:30  LA: Idcirco cadent juvenes ejus in plateis ejus, et omnes viri bellatores ejus conticescent in die illa, ait Dominus.
       EN: Therefore her young men will fall in her streets, and all her warriors will fall silent on that day, says the Lord.

27:31  LA: Ecce ego ad te, superbe ! dicit Dominus Deus exercituum : quia venit dies tuus, tempus visitationis tuæ.
       EN: Behold, I am against you, proud one, says the Lord God of hosts, for your day has come, the time of your visitation.

27:32  LA: Et cadet superbus, et corruet, et non erit qui suscitet eum : et succendam ignem in urbibus ejus, et devorabit omnia in circuitu ejus.
       EN: The proud one will stumble and fall, with no one to raise him. I will kindle a fire in his cities, and it will devour everything around him.

27:33  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Calumniam sustinent filii Israël, et filii Juda simul : omnes qui ceperunt eos, tenent : nolunt dimittere eos.
       EN: Thus says the Lord of hosts: The children of Israel and the children of Judah suffer oppression together. All who captured them hold them fast and refuse to let them go.

27:34  LA: Redemptor eorum fortis, Dominus exercituum nomen ejus : judicio defendet causam eorum, ut exterreat terram, et commoveat habitatores Babylonis.
       EN: Their Redeemer is strong; the Lord of hosts is His name. He will defend their cause in judgment, to terrify the earth and shake the inhabitants of Babylon.

27:35  LA: Gladius ad Chaldæos, ait Dominus, et ad habitatores Babylonis, et ad principes, et ad sapientes ejus.
       EN: A sword against the Chaldeans, says the Lord, against the inhabitants of Babylon, against her rulers and her wise men!

27:36  LA: Gladius ad divinos ejus, qui stulti erunt : gladius ad fortes illius, qui timebunt.
       EN: A sword against her diviners, who will become fools! A sword against her mighty men, who will be afraid!

27:37  LA: Gladius ad equos ejus, et ad currus ejus, et ad omne vulgus quod est in medio ejus : et erunt quasi mulieres : gladius ad thesauros ejus, qui diripientur.
       EN: A sword against her horses and chariots, and against all the people in her midst, who will become like women! A sword against her treasures, which will be plundered!

27:38  LA: Siccitas super aquas ejus erit, et arescent, quia terra sculptilium est, et in portentis gloriantur.
       EN: Drought will come upon her waters, and they will dry up, for she is a land of carved images, and they boast in monstrous idols.

27:39  LA: Propterea habitabunt dracones cum faunis ficariis, et habitabunt in ea struthiones : et non inhabitabitur ultra usque in sempiternum, nec exstruetur usque ad generationem et generationem.
       EN: Therefore dragons will dwell there with wild fauns, and ostriches will inhabit her. She will never again be inhabited or rebuilt, from generation to generation.

27:40  LA: Sicut subvertit Dominus Sodomam et Gomorrham, et vicinas ejus, ait Dominus, non habitabit ibi vir, et non incolet eam filius hominis.
       EN: As the Lord overthrew Sodom, Gomorrah, and their neighboring towns, says the Lord, no man will dwell there and no human being will inhabit her.

27:41  LA: Ecce populus venit ab aquilone, et gens magna, et reges multi consurgent a finibus terræ.
       EN: Behold, a people comes from the north, a great nation, and many kings will rise from the ends of the earth.

27:42  LA: Arcum et scutum apprehendent : crudeles sunt, et immisericordes : vox eorum quasi mare sonabit, et super equos ascendent, sicut vir paratus ad prælium contra te, filia Babylon.
       EN: They will take up bow and shield. They are cruel and merciless; their voice will roar like the sea, and they will ride horses, like a man prepared for battle against you, daughter Babylon.

27:43  LA: Audivit rex Babylonis famam eorum, et dissolutæ sunt manus ejus : angustia apprehendit eum, dolor quasi parturientem.
       EN: The king of Babylon has heard the report of them, and his hands have grown weak. Distress has seized him, pain like that of a woman in labor.

27:44  LA: Ecce quasi leo ascendet, de superbia Jordanis ad pulchritudinem robustam, quia subito currere faciam eum ad illam. Et quis erit electus, quem præponam ei ? quis est enim similis mei ? et quis sustinebit me ? et quis est iste pastor, qui resistat vultui meo ?
       EN: Behold, like a lion he will come up from the luxuriant growth of the Jordan against the strong and beautiful place, for I will suddenly make him run against it. Who is the chosen one I will appoint over it? For who is like Me? Who can withstand Me? And who is the shepherd who can resist Me to My face?

27:45  LA: Propterea audite consilium Domini quod mente concepit adversum Babylonem, et cogitationes ejus quas cogitavit super terram Chaldæorum : nisi detraxerint eos parvuli gregum, nisi dissipatum fuerit cum ipsis habitaculum eorum.
       EN: Therefore hear the counsel the Lord has conceived against Babylon and the plans He has formed against the land of the Chaldeans: Surely the little ones of the flocks will drag them down; surely their dwelling will be destroyed with them.

27:46  LA: A voce captivitatis Babylonis commota est terra, et clamor inter gentes auditus est.]
       EN: At the sound of Babylon’s capture the earth has shaken, and a cry has been heard among the nations.”

JEREMIAH 28

28:1  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego suscitabo super Babylonem et super habitatores ejus, qui cor suum levaverunt contra me, quasi ventum pestilentem :
      EN: “Thus says the Lord: Behold, I will stir up something like a deadly wind against Babylon and against her inhabitants who have lifted their hearts against Me.

28:2  LA: et mittam in Babylonem ventilatores, et ventilabunt eam et demolientur terram ejus, quoniam venerunt super eam undique in die afflictionis ejus.
      EN: I will send winnowers into Babylon, and they will winnow her and devastate her land, for they have come against her from every side on the day of her affliction.

28:3  LA: Non tendat qui tendit arcum suum, et non ascendat loricatus : nolite parcere juvenibus ejus ; interficite omnem militiam ejus.
      EN: Let the archer not bend his bow, and let the armored man not advance. Do not spare her young men; destroy her whole army.

28:4  LA: Et cadent interfecti in terra Chaldæorum, et vulnerati in regionibus ejus.
      EN: They will fall slain in the land of the Chaldeans, and wounded in her regions.

28:5  LA: Quoniam non fuit viduatus Israël et Juda a Deo suo, Domino exercituum, terra autem eorum repleta est delicto a Sancto Israël.
      EN: For Israel and Judah have not been left widowed by their God, the Lord of hosts, though their land is filled with guilt against the Holy One of Israel.

28:6  LA: Fugite de medio Babylonis, et salvet unusquisque animam suam : nolite tacere super iniquitatem ejus, quoniam tempus ultionis est a Domino : vicissitudinem ipse retribuet ei.
      EN: Flee from the midst of Babylon, and let each save his life. Do not remain silent over her iniquity, for this is the time of vengeance from the Lord; He Himself will repay her in kind.

28:7  LA: Calix aureus Babylon in manu Domini, inebrians omnem terram : de vino ejus biberunt gentes, et ideo commotæ sunt.
      EN: Babylon is a golden cup in the Lord’s hand, making the whole earth drunk. The nations have drunk her wine and therefore been thrown into confusion.

28:8  LA: Subito cecidit Babylon, et contrita est. Ululate super eam : tollite resinam ad dolorem ejus, si forte sanetur.
      EN: Suddenly Babylon has fallen and been shattered. Wail over her! Take balm for her pain; perhaps she may be healed.

28:9  LA: Curavimus Babylonem, et non est sanata : derelinquamus eam, et eamus unusquisque in terram suam : quoniam pervenit usque ad cælos judicium ejus, et elevatum est usque ad nubes.
      EN: We have treated Babylon, but she has not been healed. Let us abandon her and go, each to his own land, for her judgment has reached the heavens and risen to the clouds.

28:10  LA: Protulit Dominus justitias nostras : venite, et narremus in Sion opus Domini Dei nostri.
       EN: The Lord has brought forth our vindication. Come, let us recount in Zion the work of the Lord our God.

28:11  LA: Acuite sagittas, implete pharetras : suscitavit Dominus spiritum regum Medorum : et contra Babylonem mens ejus est ut perdat eam, quoniam ultio Domini est, ultio templi sui.
       EN: Sharpen the arrows, fill the quivers! The Lord has stirred up the spirit of the kings of the Medes. His purpose against Babylon is to destroy her, for this is the Lord’s vengeance, vengeance for His temple.

28:12  LA: Super muros Babylonis levate signum, augete custodiam, levate custodes, præparate insidias : quia cogitavit Dominus, et fecit quæcumque locutus est contra habitatores Babylonis.
       EN: Raise a banner over the walls of Babylon, strengthen the watch, post guards, prepare ambushes. For the Lord has planned and carried out everything He spoke against the inhabitants of Babylon.

28:13  LA: Quæ habitas super aquas multas, locuples in thesauris : venit finis tuus, pedalis præcisionis tuæ.
       EN: You who dwell beside many waters, rich in treasures, your end has come, the measured span of your cutting off.

28:14  LA: Juravit Dominus exercituum per animam suam : Quoniam replebo te hominibus quasi brucho, et super te celeuma cantabitur.
       EN: The Lord of hosts has sworn by His own life: I will fill you with men like locusts, and a shout will be sung over you.

28:15  LA: Qui fecit terram in fortitudine sua, præparavit orbem in sapientia sua, et prudentia sua extendit cælos.
       EN: He made the earth by His power, established the world by His wisdom, and stretched out the heavens by His understanding.

28:16  LA: Dante eo vocem, multiplicantur aquæ in cælo : qui levat nubes ab extremo terræ, fulgura in pluviam fecit, et produxit ventum de thesauris suis.
       EN: When He gives His voice, waters multiply in heaven. He raises clouds from the ends of the earth, makes lightning for the rain, and brings forth the wind from His storehouses.

28:17  LA: Stultus factus est omnis homo a scientia ; confusus est omnis conflator in sculptili : quia mendax est conflatio eorum, nec est spiritus in eis.
       EN: Every human being has become foolish in knowledge; every metalworker is put to shame by the carved image. For what they cast is false, and there is no breath in them.

28:18  LA: Vana sunt opera, et risu digna : in tempore visitationis suæ peribunt.
       EN: They are empty works, worthy of ridicule; at the time of their visitation they will perish.

28:19  LA: Non sicut hæc, pars Jacob, quia qui fecit omnia ipse est : et Israël sceptrum hæreditatis ejus : Dominus exercituum nomen ejus.
       EN: The portion of Jacob is not like these, for He is the Maker of all things, and Israel is the scepter of His inheritance. The Lord of hosts is His name.

28:20  LA: Collidis tu mihi vasa belli : et ego collidam in te gentes, et disperdam in te regna :
       EN: You shatter weapons of war for Me, and with you I will shatter nations; with you I will destroy kingdoms.

28:21  LA: et collidam in te equum et equitem ejus : et collidam in te currum et ascensorem ejus :
       EN: With you I will shatter horse and rider; with you I will shatter chariot and its rider.

28:22  LA: et collidam in te virum et mulierem : et collidam in te senem et puerum : et collidam in te juvenem et virginem :
       EN: With you I will shatter man and woman; with you I will shatter old man and boy; with you I will shatter young man and virgin.

28:23  LA: et collidam in te pastorem et gregem ejus : et collidam in te agricolam et jugales ejus : et collidam in te duces et magistratus :
       EN: With you I will shatter shepherd and flock; with you I will shatter farmer and his team; with you I will shatter commanders and magistrates.

28:24  LA: et reddam Babyloni, et cunctis habitatoribus Chaldææ, omne malum suum quod fecerunt in Sion, in oculis vestris, ait Dominus.
       EN: Before your eyes I will repay Babylon and all the inhabitants of Chaldea for all the evil they have done in Zion, says the Lord.

28:25  LA: Ecce ego ad te, mons pestifer, ait Dominus, qui corrumpis universam terram : et extendam manum meam super te, et evolvam te de petris, et dabo te in montem combustionis :
       EN: Behold, I am against you, deadly mountain, says the Lord, you who ruin the whole earth. I will stretch My hand over you, roll you down from the rocks, and make you a burnt mountain.

28:26  LA: et non tollent de te lapidem in angulum, et lapidem in fundamenta : sed perditus in æternum eris, ait Dominus.
       EN: They will take from you no stone for a corner and no stone for foundations. You will be ruined forever, says the Lord.

28:27  LA: Levate signum in terra, clangite buccina in gentibus, sanctificate super eam gentes, annuntiate contra illam regibus Ararat, Menni, et Ascenez : numerate contra eam Taphsar, adducite equum quasi bruchum aculeatum.
       EN: Raise a banner in the land, sound the trumpet among the nations, consecrate nations against her. Summon against her the kings of Ararat, Minni, and Ashkenaz. Muster Taphsar against her; bring up horses like bristling locusts.

28:28  LA: Sanctificate contra eam gentes, reges Mediæ, duces ejus, et universos magistratus ejus, cunctamque terram potestatis ejus.
       EN: Consecrate nations against her: the kings of Media, her commanders, all her magistrates, and the whole land under her rule.

28:29  LA: Et commovebitur terra et conturbabitur, quia evigilabit contra Babylonem cogitatio Domini, ut ponat terram Babylonis desertam et inhabitabilem.
       EN: The earth will shake and be troubled, for the Lord’s purpose against Babylon will awaken, to make the land of Babylon deserted and uninhabitable.

28:30  LA: Cessaverunt fortes Babylonis a prælio ; habitaverunt in præsidiis : devoratum est robur eorum, et facti sunt quasi mulieres : incensa sunt tabernacula ejus, contriti sunt vectes ejus.
       EN: Babylon’s mighty men have ceased fighting and remain in their strongholds. Their strength has been devoured, and they have become like women. Her dwellings have been burned and her bars broken.

28:31  LA: Currens obviam currenti veniet, et nuntius obvius nuntianti, ut annuntiet regi Babylonis quia capta est civitas ejus a summo usque ad summum.
       EN: Runner will meet runner, and messenger meet messenger, to tell the king of Babylon that his city has been captured from end to end.

28:32  LA: Et vada præoccupata sunt, et paludes incensæ sunt igni, et viri bellatores conturbati sunt.
       EN: The fords have been seized, the marshes burned with fire, and the warriors thrown into confusion.

28:33  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Filia Babylonis quasi area, tempus trituræ ejus : adhuc modicum, et veniet tempus messionis ejus.
       EN: For thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: The daughter of Babylon is like a threshing floor at the time of her threshing. A little while yet, and the time of her harvest will come.

28:34  LA: Comedit me, devoravit me Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis : reddidit me quasi vas inane, absorbuit me quasi draco, replevit ventrem suum teneritudine mea, et ejecit me.
       EN: Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon has eaten me and devoured me. He has made me like an empty vessel, swallowed me like a dragon, filled his belly with my delicacies, and cast me out.

28:35  LA: Iniquitas adversum me et caro mea super Babylonem, dicit habitatio Sion : et sanguis meus super habitatores Chaldææ, dicit Jerusalem.
       EN: ‘Let the wrong done to me and my flesh fall upon Babylon,’ says the dwelling of Zion; ‘and my blood upon the inhabitants of Chaldea,’ says Jerusalem.

28:36  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego judicabo causam tuam, et ulciscar ultionem tuam : et desertum faciam mare ejus, et siccabo venam ejus.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord: Behold, I will judge your cause and avenge your wrong. I will make her sea a desert and dry up her spring.

28:37  LA: Et erit Babylon in tumulos, habitatio draconum, stupor et sibilus, eo quod non sit habitator.
       EN: Babylon will become heaps of ruins, a dwelling of dragons, an object of horror and hissing, without an inhabitant.

28:38  LA: Simul ut leones rugient ; excutient comas veluti catuli leonum.
       EN: Together they will roar like lions and shake their manes like young lions.

28:39  LA: In calore eorum ponam potus eorum, et inebriabo eos ut sopiantur, et dormiant somnum sempiternum, et non consurgant, dicit Dominus.
       EN: In their heat I will set out their drinks and make them drunk, so that they grow drowsy and sleep an everlasting sleep, never to rise, says the Lord.

28:40  LA: Deducam eos quasi agnos ad victimam, et quasi arietes cum hædis.
       EN: I will bring them down like lambs for sacrifice, like rams together with young goats.

28:41  LA: Quomodo capta est Sesach, et comprehensa est inclyta universæ terræ ! quomodo facta est in stuporem Babylon inter gentes !
       EN: How Sheshach has been captured, the renowned one of the whole earth seized! How Babylon has become an object of horror among the nations!

28:42  LA: Ascendit super Babylonem mare : multitudine fluctuum ejus operta est.
       EN: The sea has risen over Babylon; she has been covered by the multitude of its waves.

28:43  LA: Factæ sunt civitates ejus in stuporem, terra inhabitabilis et deserta, terra in qua nullus habitet, nec transeat per eam filius hominis.
       EN: Her cities have become an object of horror, a land uninhabitable and deserted, a land where no one dwells and no human being passes through.

28:44  LA: Et visitabo super Bel in Babylone, et ejiciam quod absorbuerat de ore ejus : et non confluent ad eum ultra gentes, siquidem et murus Babylonis corruet.
       EN: I will call Bel in Babylon to account and bring out of his mouth what he swallowed. The nations will no longer flow to him, for even the wall of Babylon will fall.

28:49  LA: Et quomodo fecit Babylon, ut caderent occisi in Israël, sic de Babylone cadent occisi in universa terra.
       EN: As Babylon caused the slain to fall in Israel, so from Babylon the slain will fall throughout the earth.

28:50  LA: Qui fugistis gladium, venite, nolite stare : recordamini procul Domini, et Jerusalem ascendat super cor vestrum.
       EN: You who have escaped the sword, come, do not stand still. Remember the Lord from afar, and let Jerusalem come into your heart.

28:51  LA: Confusi sumus, quoniam audivimus opprobrium : operuit ignominia facies nostras, quia venerunt alieni super sanctificationem domus Domini.
       EN: We are ashamed because we have heard reproach; disgrace has covered our faces, because foreigners have entered the holy precincts of the house of the Lord.

28:52  LA: Propterea ecce dies veniunt, ait Dominus, et visitabo super sculptilia ejus, et in omni terra ejus mugiet vulneratus.
       EN: Therefore behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will call her carved images to account, and the wounded will groan throughout her land.

28:53  LA: Si ascenderit Babylon in cælum, et firmaverit in excelso robur suum, a me venient vastatores ejus, ait Dominus.
       EN: Though Babylon climb to heaven and establish her strength on high, her destroyers will come from Me, says the Lord.

28:54  LA: Vox clamoris de Babylone, et contritio magna de terra Chaldæorum :
       EN: A cry is heard from Babylon, and great destruction from the land of the Chaldeans,

28:55  LA: quoniam vastavit Dominus Babylonem, et perdidit ex ea vocem magnam : et sonabunt fluctus eorum quasi aquæ multæ ; dedit sonitum vox eorum :
       EN: for the Lord has devastated Babylon and silenced her mighty voice. Their waves will roar like many waters; their voice has resounded,

28:56  LA: quia venit super eam, id est super Babylonem, prædo, et apprehensi sunt fortes ejus, et emarcuit arcus eorum, quia fortis ultor Dominus reddens retribuet.
       EN: because a destroyer has come against her, against Babylon. Her mighty men have been captured, and their bow has lost its strength, for the Lord is a mighty avenger who will surely repay.

28:57  LA: Et inebriabo principes ejus, et sapientes ejus, et duces ejus, et magistratus ejus, et fortes ejus : et dormient somnum sempiternum, et non expergiscentur, ait Rex (Dominus exercituum nomen ejus).
       EN: I will make her rulers, wise men, commanders, magistrates, and mighty men drunk. They will sleep an everlasting sleep and never awaken, declares the King, whose name is the Lord of hosts.

28:58  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Murus Babylonis ille latissimus suffossione suffodietur, et portæ ejus excelsæ igni comburentur, et labores populorum ad nihilum, et gentium in ignem erunt, et disperibunt.]
       EN: Thus says the Lord of hosts: Babylon’s broad wall will be utterly undermined, and her lofty gates burned with fire. The labors of peoples will come to nothing, the toil of nations to the fire, and they will perish.”

28:59  LA: Verbum quod præcepit Jeremias propheta Saraiæ filio Neriæ filii Maasiæ, cum pergeret cum Sedecia rege in Babylonem, in anno quarto regni ejus : Saraias autem erat princeps prophetiæ.
       EN: The charge Jeremiah the prophet gave to Seraiah son of Neriah, son of Maaseiah, when he went with King Zedekiah to Babylon in the fourth year of his reign. Seraiah was chief of prophecy.

28:60  LA: Et scripsit Jeremias omne malum quod venturum erat super Babylonem, in libro uno : omnia verba hæc quæ scripta sunt contra Babylonem.
       EN: Jeremiah wrote in one book all the calamity that would come upon Babylon, all these words written against Babylon.

28:61  LA: Et dixit Jeremias ad Saraiam : Cum veneris in Babylonem, et videris, et legeris omnia verba hæc,
       EN: Jeremiah said to Seraiah, “When you come to Babylon and see it and read all these words,

28:62  LA: dices : Domine, tu locutus es contra locum istum, ut disperderes eum, ne sit qui in eo habitet, ab homine usque ad pecus, et ut sit perpetua solitudo.
       EN: you shall say, ‘Lord, You have spoken against this place, to destroy it so that no one dwells in it, neither human nor animal, and so that it becomes a perpetual wilderness.’

28:63  LA: Cumque compleveris legere librum istum, ligabis ad eum lapidem, et projicies illum in medium Euphraten,
       EN: When you have finished reading this book, tie a stone to it and throw it into the midst of the Euphrates.

28:64  LA: et dices : Sic submergetur Babylon, et non consurget a facie afflictionis quam ego adduco super eam, et dissolvetur. Hucusque verba Jeremiæ.
       EN: Then say, ‘So will Babylon sink and not rise from the affliction I am bringing upon her, and she will collapse.’” Thus far the words of Jeremiah.

JEREMIAH 29

29:1  LA: Quod factum est verbum Domini ad Jeremiam prophetam contra Palæstinos, antequam percuteret Pharao Gazam.
      EN: The word of the Lord that came to Jeremiah the prophet against the Philistines, before Pharaoh struck Gaza.

29:2  LA: [Hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce aquæ ascendunt ab aquilone, et erunt quasi torrens inundans, et operient terram et plenitudinem ejus, urbem et habitatores ejus. Clamabunt homines, et ululabunt omnes habitatores terræ,
      EN: “Thus says the Lord: Behold, waters are rising from the north. They will become an overflowing torrent and cover the land and all that fills it, the city and its inhabitants. People will cry out, and all the inhabitants of the land will wail

29:3  LA: a strepitu pompæ armorum, et bellatorum ejus, a commotione quadrigarum ejus, et multitudine rotarum illius. Non respexerunt patres filios manibus dissolutis,
      EN: at the clamor of the array of his weapons and warriors, the shaking of his chariots, and the multitude of his wheels. Fathers have not looked back for their children, their hands hanging helpless,

29:4  LA: pro adventu diei in quo vastabuntur omnes Philisthiim, et dissipabitur Tyrus et Sidon cum omnibus reliquis auxiliis suis : depopulatus est enim Dominus Palæstinos, reliquias insulæ Cappadociæ.
      EN: because of the coming day when all the Philistines will be laid waste and Tyre and Sidon destroyed with all their remaining helpers. For the Lord has ravaged the Philistines, the remnant of the island of Cappadocia.

29:5  LA: Venit calvitium super Gazam ; conticuit Ascalon, et reliquiæ vallis earum : usquequo concideris ?
      EN: Baldness has come upon Gaza; Ashkelon has fallen silent, with the remnant of their valley. How long will you cut yourself?

29:6  LA: O mucro Domini, usquequo non quiesces ? ingredere in vaginam tuam, refrigerare, et sile.
      EN: O sword of the Lord, how long before you rest? Return to your sheath, grow cool, and be still!

29:7  LA: Quomodo quiescet, cum Dominus præceperit ei adversus Ascalonem, et adversus maritimas ejus regiones, ibique condixerit illi ?]
      EN: How can it rest when the Lord has commanded it against Ashkelon and the regions along her coast, and assigned it there?”

JEREMIAH 30

30:1  LA: Ad Idumæam. [Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Numquid non ultra est sapientia in Theman ? periit consilium a filiis ; inutilis facta est sapientia eorum.
      EN: Concerning Edom. “Thus says the Lord of hosts: Is there no longer wisdom in Teman? Counsel has perished from her children; their wisdom has become useless.

30:2  LA: Fugite, et terga vertite ; descendite in voraginem, habitatores Dedan : quoniam perditionem Esau adduxi super eum, tempus visitationis ejus.
      EN: Flee, turn your backs, descend into the depths, inhabitants of Dedan. For I have brought Esau’s destruction upon him, the time of his visitation.

30:3  LA: Si vindemiatores venissent super te, non reliquissent racemum : si fures in nocte rapuissent quod sufficeret sibi.
      EN: If grape gatherers came upon you, they would not leave a cluster; if thieves came by night, they would steal what was enough for them.

30:4  LA: Ego vero discooperui Esau : revelavi abscondita ejus, et celari non poterit : vastatum est semen ejus, et fratres ejus, et vicini ejus, et non erit.
      EN: But I have stripped Esau bare; I have revealed his hiding places, and he cannot hide. His offspring, brothers, and neighbors have been laid waste, and he will be no more.

30:5  LA: Relinque pupillos tuos : ego faciam eos vivere : et viduæ tuæ in me sperabunt.
      EN: Leave your orphans; I will keep them alive, and let your widows trust in Me.

30:6  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce quibus non erat judicium ut biberent calicem, bibentes bibent : et tu, quasi innocens relinqueris ? non eris innocens, sed bibens bibes.
      EN: For thus says the Lord: Behold, those who were not sentenced to drink the cup will surely drink it. And will you be left as though innocent? You will not be innocent, but will surely drink.

30:7  LA: Quia per memetipsum juravi, dicit Dominus, quod in solitudinem, et in opprobrium, et in desertum, et in maledictionem erit Bosra, et omnes civitates ejus erunt in solitudines sempiternas.
      EN: For I have sworn by Myself, says the Lord, that Bozrah will become a desolation, a reproach, a wilderness, and a curse, and all her cities will be everlasting ruins.

30:8  LA: Auditum audivi a Domino, et legatus ad gentes missus est : Congregamini, et venite contra eam, et consurgamus in prælium.
      EN: I have heard a report from the Lord, and an envoy has been sent among the nations: ‘Gather and come against her, and let us rise for battle.’

30:9  LA: Ecce enim parvulum dedi te in gentibus, contemptibilem inter homines.
      EN: For behold, I have made you small among the nations, despised among humankind.

30:10  LA: Arrogantia tua decepit te, et superbia cordis tui, qui habitas in cavernis petræ, et apprehendere niteris altitudinem collis : cum exaltaveris quasi aquila nidum tuum, inde detraham te, dicit Dominus.
       EN: Your arrogance has deceived you, and the pride of your heart, you who dwell in the clefts of the rock and strive to seize the height of the hill. Though you raise your nest like an eagle, I will bring you down from there, says the Lord.

30:11  LA: Et erit Idumæa deserta : omnis qui transibit per eam stupebit, et sibilabit super omnes plagas ejus.
       EN: Edom will be desolate. Everyone who passes through her will be astonished and hiss at all her wounds.

30:12  LA: Sicut subversa est Sodoma et Gomorrha, et vicinæ ejus, ait Dominus : non habitabit ibi vir, et non incolet eam filius hominis.
       EN: As Sodom, Gomorrah, and their neighboring towns were overthrown, says the Lord, no man will dwell there and no human being will inhabit her.

30:13  LA: Ecce quasi leo ascendet de superbia Jordanis ad pulchritudinem robustam, quia subito currere faciam eum ad illam. Et quis erit electus, quem præponam ei ? quis enim similis mei ? et quis sustinebit me ? et quis est iste pastor, qui resistat vultui meo ?
       EN: Behold, like a lion he will come up from the luxuriant growth of the Jordan against the strong and beautiful place, for I will suddenly make him run against it. Who is the chosen one I will appoint over it? For who is like Me? Who can withstand Me? And who is the shepherd who can resist Me to My face?

30:14  LA: Propterea audite consilium Domini quod iniit de Edom, et cogitationes ejus quas cogitavit de habitatoribus Theman : si non dejecerint eos parvuli gregis, nisi dissipaverint cum eis habitaculum eorum.
       EN: Therefore hear the counsel the Lord has taken concerning Edom and the plans He has formed concerning the inhabitants of Teman: Surely the little ones of the flock will cast them down; surely they will destroy their dwelling with them.

30:15  LA: A voce ruinæ eorum commota est terra ; clamor in mari Rubro auditus est vocis ejus.
       EN: At the sound of their fall the earth has shaken; the cry of her voice has been heard at the Red Sea.

30:16  LA: Ecce quasi aquila ascendet, et avolabit, et expandet alas suas super Bosran : et erit cor fortium Idumææ in die illa quasi cor mulieris parturientis.]
       EN: Behold, like an eagle he will rise and fly, spreading his wings over Bozrah. On that day the heart of Edom’s mighty men will be like the heart of a woman in labor.”

30:17  LA: Ad filios Ammon. [Hæc dicit Dominus : Numquid non filii sunt Israël, aut hæres non est ei ? cur igitur hæreditate possedit Melchom Gad, et populus ejus in urbibus ejus habitavit ?
       EN: Concerning the Ammonites. “Thus says the Lord: Has Israel no children? Has he no heir? Why, then, has Milcom taken Gad as an inheritance, and why have his people dwelt in Gad’s cities?

30:18  LA: Ideo ecce dies veniunt, dicit Dominus, et auditum faciam super Rabbath filiorum Ammon fremitum prælii, et erit in tumultum dissipata, filiæque ejus igni succendentur, et possidebit Israël possessores suos, ait Dominus.
       EN: Therefore behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will make the roar of battle heard against Rabbah of the Ammonites. She will be scattered in tumult, and her daughters will be burned with fire. Israel will possess those who possessed him, says the Lord.

30:19  LA: Ulula, Hesebon, quoniam vastata est Hai ; clamate, filiæ Rabbath : accingite vos ciliciis, plangite et circuite per sepes, quoniam Melchom in transmigrationem ducetur, sacerdotes ejus et principes ejus simul.
       EN: Wail, Heshbon, for Ai has been laid waste! Cry out, daughters of Rabbah; gird yourselves with sackcloth, mourn, and wander among the hedges. For Milcom will be led into exile, his priests and rulers together.

30:20  LA: Quid gloriaris in vallibus ? defluxit vallis tua, filia delicata, quæ confidebas in thesauris tuis, et dicebas : Quis veniet ad me ?
       EN: Why do you boast in the valleys? Your valley has flowed away, pampered daughter, who trusted in your treasures and said, ‘Who will come against me?’

30:21  LA: Ecce ego inducam super te terrorem, ait Dominus Deus exercituum, ab omnibus qui sunt in circuitu tuo : et dispergemini singuli a conspectu vestro, nec erit qui congreget fugientes.
       EN: Behold, I am bringing terror upon you, says the Lord God of hosts, from all those around you. You will be scattered, each from the sight of the others, and no one will gather the fugitives.

30:23  LA: Ad Cedar, et ad regna Asor, quæ percussit Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis. [Hæc dicit Dominus : Surgite, et ascendite ad Cedar, et vastate filios orientis.
       EN: Concerning Kedar and the kingdoms of Hazor, which Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon struck. “Thus says the Lord: Rise and go up against Kedar, and lay waste the children of the east.

30:24  LA: Tabernacula eorum, et greges eorum capient : pelles eorum, et omnia vasa eorum, et camelos eorum tollent sibi, et vocabunt super eos formidinem in circuitu.
       EN: They will seize their tents and flocks. They will take for themselves their tent coverings, all their vessels, and their camels, and cry over them, ‘Terror on every side!’

30:25  LA: Fugite, abite vehementer, in voraginibus sedete, qui habitatis Asor, ait Dominus : iniit enim contra vos Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis consilium, et cogitavit adversum vos cogitationes.
       EN: Flee, go far away, dwell in the depths, inhabitants of Hazor, says the Lord. For Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon has taken counsel against you and formed plans against you.

30:26  LA: Consurgite, et ascendite ad gentem quietam, et habitantem confidenter, ait Dominus : non ostia, nec vectes eis : soli habitant.
       EN: Rise and go up against a nation at rest, dwelling securely, says the Lord. They have neither gates nor bars; they dwell alone.

30:27  LA: Et erunt cameli eorum in direptionem, et multitudo jumentorum in prædam : et dispergam eos in omnem ventum, qui sunt attonsi in comam, et ex omni confinio eorum adducam interitum super eos, ait Dominus.
       EN: Their camels will become booty and their many livestock plunder. I will scatter to every wind those whose hair is clipped, and from every border I will bring destruction upon them, says the Lord.

30:28  LA: Et erit Asor in habitaculum draconum, deserta usque in æternum : non manebit ibi vir, nec incolet eam filius hominis.]
       EN: Hazor will become a dwelling of dragons, a wilderness forever. No man will remain there and no human being will inhabit her.”

30:29  LA: Ad Damascum. [Confusa est Emath et Arphad, quia auditum pessimum audierunt : turbati sunt in mari ; præ sollicitudine quiescere non potuit.
       EN: Concerning Damascus. “Hamath and Arpad are ashamed, for they have heard a grievous report. They are troubled upon the sea; in its anxiety it could not be still.

30:30  LA: Dissoluta est Damascus, versa est in fugam : tremor apprehendit eam, angustia et dolores tenuerunt eam quasi parturientem.
       EN: Damascus has grown feeble and turned to flee. Trembling has seized her; distress and pain have gripped her like a woman in labor.

30:31  LA: Quomodo dereliquerunt civitatem laudabilem, urbem lætitiæ ?
       EN: How have they abandoned the city of praise, the town of joy?

30:32  LA: Ideo cadent juvenes ejus in plateis ejus, et omnes viri prælii conticescent in die illa, ait Dominus exercituum.
       EN: Therefore her young men will fall in her streets, and all her warriors will fall silent on that day, says the Lord of hosts.

30:33  LA: Et succendam ignem in muro Damasci, et devorabit mœnia Benadad.]
       EN: I will kindle a fire in the wall of Damascus, and it will devour the fortifications of Ben-hadad.”

JEREMIAH 31

31:1  LA: Ad Moab. [Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Væ super Nabo, quoniam vastata est, et confusa ! capta est Cariathaim, confusa est fortis, et tremuit.
      EN: Concerning Moab. “Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Woe to Nebo, for it has been laid waste and put to shame! Kiriathaim has been captured; the stronghold is ashamed and trembling.

31:2  LA: Non est ultra exsultatio in Moab contra Hesebon : cogitaverunt malum : Venite, et disperdamus eam de gente. Ergo silens conticesces, sequeturque te gladius.
      EN: There is no longer exultation in Moab against Heshbon. They have plotted evil: ‘Come, let us destroy her as a nation.’ Therefore you will be silenced and still, and the sword will pursue you.

31:3  LA: Vox clamoris de Oronaim, vastitas et contritio magna.
      EN: A cry is heard from Horonaim: devastation and great destruction!

31:4  LA: Contrita est Moab : annuntiate clamorem parvulis ejus.
      EN: Moab has been shattered; proclaim a cry to her little ones.

31:5  LA: Per ascensum enim Luith plorans ascendet in fletu, quoniam in descensu Oronaim hostes ululatum contritionis audierunt.
      EN: Up the ascent of Luhith they will climb, weeping in tears, for on the descent of Horonaim the enemies have heard the wail of destruction.

31:6  LA: Fugite, salvate animas vestras, et eritis quasi myricæ in deserto :
      EN: Flee, save your lives, and be like tamarisks in the wilderness.

31:7  LA: pro eo enim quod habuisti fiduciam in munitionibus tuis et in thesauris tuis, tu quoque capieris : et ibit Chamos in transmigrationem, sacerdotes ejus et principes ejus simul.
      EN: Because you trusted in your fortifications and treasures, you too will be captured. Chemosh will go into exile, his priests and rulers together.

31:8  LA: Et veniet prædo ad omnem urbem, et urbs nulla salvabitur : et peribunt valles, et dissipabuntur campestria, quoniam dixit Dominus :
      EN: The destroyer will come to every city, and no city will be saved. The valleys will perish and the plains be laid waste, for the Lord has spoken.

31:9  LA: Date florem Moab, quia florens egredietur : et civitates ejus desertæ erunt, et inhabitabiles.
      EN: Give Moab a flower, for she will go forth blooming. Her cities will be deserted and uninhabitable.

31:10  LA: Maledictus qui facit opus Domini fraudulenter, et maledictus qui prohibet gladium suum a sanguine.
       EN: Cursed is the one who does the Lord’s work deceitfully, and cursed is the one who keeps his sword from blood.

31:11  LA: Fertilis fuit Moab ab adolescentia sua, et requievit in fæcibus suis : nec transfusus est de vase in vas, et in transmigrationem non abiit : idcirco permansit gustus ejus in eo, et odor ejus non est immutatus.
       EN: Moab has been fruitful from his youth and has rested upon his lees. He has not been poured from vessel to vessel or gone into exile. Therefore his taste has remained in him, and his aroma has not changed.

31:12  LA: Propterea ecce dies veniunt, dicit Dominus, et mittam ei ordinatores et stratores laguncularum : et sternent eum, et vasa ejus exhaurient, et lagunculas eorum collident.
       EN: Therefore behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will send him those who arrange and lay down jars. They will lay him low, empty his vessels, and smash their jars.

31:13  LA: Et confundetur Moab a Chamos sicut confusa est domus Israël a Bethel, in qua habebat fiduciam.
       EN: Moab will be ashamed of Chemosh, as the house of Israel was ashamed of Bethel, in which it trusted.

31:14  LA: Quomodo dicitis : Fortes sumus, et viri robusti ad præliandum ?
       EN: How can you say, ‘We are mighty, men strong for battle’?

31:15  LA: Vastata est Moab, et civitates illius succiderunt, et electi juvenes ejus descenderunt in occisionem, ait Rex : Dominus exercituum nomen ejus.
       EN: Moab has been laid waste, her cities have fallen, and her chosen young men have gone down to slaughter, declares the King, whose name is the Lord of hosts.

31:16  LA: Prope est interitus Moab ut veniat, et malum ejus velociter accurret nimis.
       EN: Moab’s destruction is near at hand, and her calamity is hastening swiftly.

31:17  LA: Consolamini eum, omnes qui estis in circuitu ejus : et universi qui scitis nomen ejus, dicite : Quomodo confracta est virga fortis, baculus gloriosus ?
       EN: Comfort him, all you around him. All you who know his name, say, ‘How the strong rod is broken, the glorious staff!’

31:18  LA: Descende de gloria, et sede in siti, habitatio filiæ Dibon, quoniam vastator Moab ascendit ad te : dissipavit munitiones tuas.
       EN: Come down from glory and sit in thirst, dwelling of the daughter of Dibon, for the destroyer of Moab has come up against you and shattered your fortifications.

31:19  LA: In via sta, et prospice, habitatio Aroër : interroga fugientem, et ei qui evasit dic : Quid accidit ?
       EN: Stand by the road and watch, dwelling of Aroer. Ask the fugitive, and say to the one who escaped, ‘What has happened?’

31:20  LA: Confusus est Moab, quoniam victus est. Ululate, et clamate : annuntiate in Arnon, quoniam vastata est Moab,
       EN: Moab is ashamed, for he has been defeated. Wail and cry out! Proclaim in Arnon that Moab has been laid waste.

31:21  LA: et judicium venit ad terram campestrem, super Helon, et super Jasa, et super Mephaath,
       EN: Judgment has come upon the land of the plain, upon Holon, Jahzah, and Mephaath,

31:22  LA: et super Dibon, et super Nabo, et super domum Deblathaim,
       EN: upon Dibon, Nebo, and Beth-diblathaim,

31:23  LA: et super Cariathaim, et super Bethgamul, et super Bethmaon,
       EN: upon Kiriathaim, Beth-gamul, and Beth-meon,

31:24  LA: et super Carioth, et super Bosra, et super omnes civitates terræ Moab, quæ longe et quæ prope sunt.
       EN: upon Kerioth, Bozrah, and all the cities of the land of Moab, far and near.

31:25  LA: Abscissum est cornu Moab, et brachium ejus contritum est, ait Dominus.
       EN: Moab’s horn has been cut off and his arm broken, says the Lord.
