31:26  LA: Inebriate eum, quoniam contra Dominum erectus est : et allidet manum Moab in vomitu suo, et erit in derisum etiam ipse.
       EN: Make him drunk, for he has exalted himself against the Lord. Moab will dash his hand into his own vomit and become an object of ridicule himself.

31:27  LA: Fuit enim in derisum tibi Israël : quasi inter fures reperisses eum : propter verba ergo tua quæ adversum illum locutus es, captivus duceris.
       EN: For Israel was an object of ridicule to you, as though you had found him among thieves. Because of the words you spoke against him, you will be led away captive.

31:28  LA: Relinquite civitates, et habitate in petra, habitatores Moab : et estote quasi columba nidificans in summo ore foraminis.
       EN: Leave the cities and dwell in the rock, inhabitants of Moab. Be like a dove nesting high at the mouth of a cleft.

31:29  LA: Audivimus superbiam Moab : superbus est valde : sublimitatem ejus, et arrogantiam, et superbiam, et altitudinem cordis ejus.
       EN: We have heard of Moab’s pride, for he is exceedingly proud: his loftiness, arrogance, pride, and haughtiness of heart.

31:30  LA: Ego scio, ait Dominus, jactantiam ejus, et quod non sit juxta eam virtus ejus, nec juxta quod poterat conata sit facere.
       EN: I know his boasting, says the Lord, and that his strength does not match it, nor has he attempted to act in proportion to what he could do.

31:31  LA: Ideo super Moab ejulabo, et ad Moab universam clamabo, ad viros muri fictilis lamentantes :
       EN: Therefore I will wail over Moab and cry out for all Moab, to the mourning men of the earthen wall.

31:32  LA: de planctu Jazer plorabo tibi, vinea Sabama. Propagines tuæ transierunt mare ; usque ad mare Jazer pervenerunt : super messem tuam et vindemiam tuam prædo irruit.
       EN: With the mourning of Jazer I will weep for you, vine of Sibmah. Your shoots crossed the sea and reached as far as the sea of Jazer. The destroyer has fallen upon your harvest and your vintage.

31:33  LA: Ablata est lætitia et exsultatio de Carmelo et de terra Moab, et vinum de torcularibus sustuli : nequaquam calcator uvæ solitum celeuma cantabit.
       EN: Gladness and exultation have been taken away from Carmel and the land of Moab. I have removed wine from the presses; the grape treader will no longer sing his customary shout.

31:34  LA: De clamore Hesebon usque Eleale et Jasa, dederunt vocem suam ; a Segor usque ad Oronaim, vitula conternante : aquæ quoque Nemrim pessimæ erunt.
       EN: From the cry of Heshbon as far as Elealeh and Jahaz, they have raised their voices; from Zoar as far as Horonaim, like a three-year-old heifer. The waters of Nimrim too will be utterly bad.

31:35  LA: Et auferam de Moab, ait Dominus, offerentem in excelsis, et sacrificantem diis ejus.
       EN: I will remove from Moab, says the Lord, the one who makes offerings on the high places and sacrifices to his gods.

31:36  LA: Propterea cor meum ad Moab quasi tibiæ resonabit, et cor meum ad viros muri fictilis dabit sonitum tibiarum : quia plus fecit quam potuit, idcirco perierunt.
       EN: Therefore my heart will sound like pipes for Moab, and my heart will give the sound of pipes for the men of the earthen wall. Because he has done more than he could, therefore they have perished.

31:37  LA: Omne enim caput calvitium, et omnis barba rasa erit : in cunctis manibus colligatio, et super omne dorsum cilicium :
       EN: Every head will be bald and every beard shaved; every hand will be bound and every back covered in sackcloth.

31:38  LA: super omnia tecta Moab, et in plateis ejus, omnis planctus : quoniam contrivi Moab sicut vas inutile, ait Dominus.
       EN: On all the roofs of Moab and in her streets there is mourning everywhere, for I have broken Moab like a useless vessel, says the Lord.

31:39  LA: Quomodo victa est, et ululaverunt ? quomodo dejecit cervicem Moab, et confusus est ? eritque Moab in derisum, et in exemplum omnibus in circuitu suo.
       EN: How she has been defeated, and they have wailed! How Moab has bowed his neck and been put to shame! Moab will become an object of ridicule and a warning to all around him.

31:40  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce quasi aquila volabit, et extendet alas suas ad Moab.
       EN: Thus says the Lord: Behold, he will fly like an eagle and spread his wings over Moab.

31:41  LA: Capta est Carioth, et munitiones comprehensæ sunt : et erit cor fortium Moab in die illa sicut cor mulieris parturientis,
       EN: Kerioth has been captured and the strongholds seized. On that day the heart of Moab’s mighty men will be like the heart of a woman in labor.

31:42  LA: et cessabit Moab esse populus, quoniam contra Dominum gloriatus est.
       EN: Moab will cease to be a people, because he boasted against the Lord.

31:43  LA: Pavor, et fovea, et laqueus super te, o habitator Moab, dicit Dominus.
       EN: Terror, pit, and snare are upon you, inhabitant of Moab, says the Lord.

31:44  LA: Qui fugerit a facie pavoris cadet in foveam, et qui conscenderit de fovea capietur laqueo : adducam enim super Moab annum visitationis eorum, ait Dominus.
       EN: Whoever flees from terror will fall into the pit, and whoever climbs out of the pit will be caught in the snare. For I will bring upon Moab the year of their visitation, says the Lord.

JEREMIAH 32

32:13  LA: 
       EN: 

32:15  LA: Quia sic dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Sume calicem vini furoris hujus de manu mea, et propinabis de illo cunctis gentibus ad quas ego mittam te.
       EN: For thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Take from My hand this cup of the wine of fury, and give it to all the nations to whom I send you to drink.

32:16  LA: Et bibent, et turbabuntur et insanient a facie gladii quem ego mittam inter eos.
       EN: They will drink, be troubled, and go mad before the sword that I send among them.”

32:17  LA: Et accepi calicem de manu Domini, et propinavi cunctis gentibus ad quas misit me Dominus,
       EN: I took the cup from the Lord's hand and gave it to all the nations to whom the Lord sent me to drink:

32:18  LA: Jerusalem, et civitatibus Juda, et regibus ejus, et principibus ejus, ut darem eos in solitudinem, et in stuporem, et in sibilum, et in maledictionem, sicut est dies ista :
       EN: Jerusalem, the cities of Judah, its kings and rulers, to make them a desolation, an object of horror and hissing, and a curse, as it is this day;

32:19  LA: Pharaoni regi Ægypti, et servis ejus, et principibus ejus, et omni populo ejus :
       EN: Pharaoh king of Egypt, his servants, his rulers, and all his people;

32:20  LA: et universis generaliter : cunctis regibus terræ Ausitidis, et cunctis regibus terræ Philisthiim, et Ascaloni, et Gazæ, et Accaron, et reliquiis Azoti :
       EN: and everyone in general, all the kings of the land of Uz, all the kings of the land of the Philistines, Ashkelon, Gaza, Ekron, and the remnant of Ashdod;

32:21  LA: et Idumææ, et Moab, et filiis Ammon :
       EN: Edom, Moab, and the children of Ammon;

32:22  LA: et cunctis regibus Tyri, et universis regibus Sidonis, et regibus terræ insularum qui sunt trans mare :
       EN: all the kings of Tyre, all the kings of Sidon, and the kings of the island lands beyond the sea;

32:23  LA: et Dedan, et Thema, et Buz, et universis qui attonsi sunt in comam :
       EN: Dedan, Tema, Buz, and all those with cropped hair;

32:24  LA: et cunctis regibus Arabiæ, et cunctis regibus occidentis, qui habitant in deserto :
       EN: all the kings of Arabia and all the kings of the west who dwell in the wilderness;

32:25  LA: et cunctis regibus Zambri, et cunctis regibus Elam, et cunctis regibus Medorum :
       EN: all the kings of Zimri, all the kings of Elam, and all the kings of the Medes;

32:26  LA: cunctis quoque regibus aquilonis, de prope et de longe, unicuique contra fratrem suum : et omnibus regnis terræ quæ super faciem ejus sunt : et rex Sesach bibet post eos.
       EN: all the kings of the north, near and far, each against his brother, and all the kingdoms of the earth upon its face. And the king of Sheshach will drink after them.

32:27  LA: Et dices ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Bibite, et inebriamini, et vomite : et cadite, neque surgatis a facie gladii quem ego mittam inter vos.
       EN: “You shall tell them: Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Drink, become drunk, and vomit; fall and do not rise before the sword that I send among you.

32:28  LA: Cumque noluerint accipere calicem de manu tua ut bibant, dices ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Bibentes bibetis :
       EN: If they refuse to take the cup from your hand and drink, you shall tell them: Thus says the Lord of hosts: You shall surely drink!

32:29  LA: quia ecce in civitate in qua invocatum est nomen meum ego incipiam affligere, et vos quasi innocentes et immunes eritis ? non eritis immunes : gladium enim ego voco super omnes habitatores terræ, dicit Dominus exercituum.
       EN: For look, I begin to afflict the city upon which My name has been invoked, and will you be innocent and go unpunished? You will not go unpunished, for I am summoning a sword against all the inhabitants of the earth, says the Lord of hosts.

32:30  LA: Et tu prophetabis ad eos omnia verba hæc, et dices ad illos : [Dominus de excelso rugiet, et de habitaculo sancto suo dabit vocem suam : rugiens rugiet super decorem suum : celeuma quasi calcantium concinetur adversus omnes habitatores terræ.
       EN: You shall prophesy all these words to them and say: The Lord will roar from on high and raise His voice from His holy dwelling. He will roar mightily over His splendor; a rhythmic cry like that of grape treaders will be sung against all the inhabitants of the earth.

32:31  LA: Pervenit sonitus usque ad extrema terræ, quia judicium Domino cum gentibus : judicatur ipse cum omni carne. Impios tradidi gladio, dicit Dominus.
       EN: The noise has reached the ends of the earth, for the Lord has a case against the nations. He enters into judgment with all flesh. I have handed the wicked over to the sword, says the Lord.

32:32  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Ecce afflictio egredietur de gente in gentem, et turbo magnus egredietur a summitatibus terræ.
       EN: Thus says the Lord of hosts: Look, affliction will pass from nation to nation, and a great whirlwind will rise from the ends of the earth.

32:33  LA: Et erunt interfecti Domini in die illa, a summo terræ usque ad summum ejus : non plangentur, et non colligentur, neque sepelientur : in sterquilinium super faciem terræ jacebunt.
       EN: On that day those slain by the Lord will lie from one end of the earth to the other. They will not be mourned, gathered, or buried; they will lie like dung upon the face of the earth.

32:34  LA: Ululate, pastores, et clamate, et aspergite vos cinere, optimates gregis : quia completi sunt dies vestri ut interficiamini, et dissipationes vestræ : et cadetis quasi vasa pretiosa.
       EN: Wail, shepherds, and cry out; sprinkle yourselves with ashes, leaders of the flock. For your days for slaughter and your dispersal have arrived, and you will fall like precious vessels.

32:35  LA: Et peribit fuga a pastoribus, et salvatio ab optimatibus gregis.
       EN: Escape will fail the shepherds, and deliverance the leaders of the flock.

32:36  LA: Vox clamoris pastorum, et ululatus optimatum gregis, quia vastavit Dominus pascua eorum :
       EN: Listen, the shepherds' cry and the wailing of the flock's leaders, because the Lord has devastated their pastures!

32:37  LA: et conticuerunt arva pacis a facie iræ furoris Domini.
       EN: The peaceful fields have fallen silent before the Lord's fierce anger.

32:38  LA: Dereliquit quasi leo umbraculum suum, quia facta est terra eorum in desolationem a facie iræ columbæ, et a facie iræ furoris Domini.]
       EN: Like a lion He has left His shelter, for their land has become desolate before the anger of the dove and before the Lord's fierce anger.”

JEREMIAH 33

33:1  LA: In principio regni Joakim filii Josiæ regis Juda, factum est verbum istud a Domino, dicens :
      EN: At the beginning of the reign of Jehoiakim son of Josiah, king of Judah, this word came from the Lord, saying:

33:2  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Sta in atrio domus Domini, et loqueris ad omnes civitates Juda, de quibus veniunt ut adorent in domo Domini universos sermones quos ego mandavi tibi ut loquaris ad eos : noli subtrahere verbum,
      EN: “Thus says the Lord: Stand in the court of the Lord's house and speak to all the cities of Judah from which people come to worship in the Lord's house. Speak all the words that I commanded you to tell them; do not omit a word.

33:3  LA: si forte audiant, et convertantur unusquisque a via sua mala, et pœniteat me mali quod cogito facere eis propter malitiam studiorum eorum.
      EN: Perhaps they will listen and each turn from his evil way, so that I may relent concerning the disaster I intend to bring upon them because of the wickedness of their deeds.

33:4  LA: Et dices ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus : Si non audieritis me, ut ambuletis in lege mea quam dedi vobis,
      EN: You shall tell them: Thus says the Lord: If you do not listen to Me by walking in My law that I gave you,

33:5  LA: ut audiatis sermones servorum meorum prophetarum quos ego misi ad vos, de nocte consurgens, et dirigens, et non audistis :
      EN: by hearing the words of My servants the prophets whom I sent you, rising in the night and sending them, though you did not listen,

33:6  LA: dabo domum istam sicut Silo, et urbem hanc dabo in maledictionem cunctis gentibus terræ.
      EN: I will make this house like Shiloh and this city a curse among all the nations of the earth.”

33:7  LA: Et audierunt sacerdotes, et prophetæ, et omnis populus, Jeremiam loquentem verba hæc in domo Domini.
      EN: The priests, prophets, and all the people heard Jeremiah speaking these words in the Lord's house.

33:8  LA: Cumque complesset Jeremias, loquens omnia quæ præceperat ei Dominus ut loqueretur ad universum populum, apprehenderunt eum sacerdotes, et prophetæ, et omnis populus, dicens : Morte moriatur.
      EN: When Jeremiah finished speaking everything that the Lord had commanded him to say to all the people, the priests, prophets, and all the people seized him, saying, “He must die!

33:9  LA: Quare prophetavit in nomine Domini, dicens : Sicut Silo erit domus hæc, et urbs ista desolabitur eo quod non sit habitator ? Et congregatus est omnis populus adversus Jeremiam in domo Domini.
      EN: Why has he prophesied in the Lord's name, saying, ‘This house will be like Shiloh, and this city will be desolate, without an inhabitant’?” All the people gathered against Jeremiah in the Lord's house.

33:10  LA: Et audierunt principes Juda verba hæc, et ascenderunt de domo regis in domum Domini, et sederunt in introitu portæ domus Domini novæ.
       EN: The rulers of Judah heard these things, went up from the king's house to the Lord's house, and sat at the entrance of the New Gate of the Lord's house.

33:11  LA: Et locuti sunt sacerdotes et prophetæ ad principes, et ad omnem populum, dicentes : Judicium mortis est viro huic, quia prophetavit adversus civitatem istam, sicut audistis auribus vestris.
       EN: The priests and prophets said to the rulers and all the people, “This man deserves a sentence of death, because he has prophesied against this city, as you have heard with your own ears.”

33:12  LA: Et ait Jeremias ad omnes principes, et ad universum populum, dicens : Dominus misit me ut prophetarem ad domum istam, et ad civitatem hanc, omnia verba quæ audistis.
       EN: Jeremiah said to all the rulers and all the people, “The Lord sent me to prophesy against this house and this city all the words you have heard.

33:13  LA: Nunc ergo bonas facite vias vestras et studia vestra, et audite vocem Domini Dei vestri, et pœnitebit Dominum mali quod locutus est adversum vos.
       EN: So now make your ways and deeds good, and listen to the voice of the Lord your God, and the Lord will relent concerning the disaster He has pronounced against you.

33:14  LA: Ego autem ecce in manibus vestris sum : facite mihi quod bonum et rectum est in oculis vestris.
       EN: As for me, look, I am in your hands. Do to me what seems good and right to you.

33:15  LA: Verumtamen scitote et cognoscite quod, si occideritis me, sanguinem innocentem tradetis contra vosmetipsos, et contra civitatem istam, et habitatores ejus : in veritate enim misit me Dominus ad vos, ut loquerer in auribus vestris omnia verba hæc.
       EN: But know and understand that if you kill me, you will bring innocent blood upon yourselves, this city, and its inhabitants, for the Lord truly sent me to you to speak all these words in your hearing.”

33:16  LA: Et dixerunt principes et omnis populus ad sacerdotes et ad prophetas : Non est viro huic judicium mortis, quia in nomine Domini Dei nostri locutus est ad nos.
       EN: Then the rulers and all the people said to the priests and prophets, “This man does not deserve a sentence of death, for he has spoken to us in the name of the Lord our God.”

33:17  LA: Surrexerunt ergo viri de senioribus terræ, et dixerunt ad omnem cœtum populi, loquentes :
       EN: Some of the elders of the land rose and addressed the whole assembly of the people, saying:

33:18  LA: Michæas de Morasthi fuit propheta in diebus Ezechiæ regis Juda, et ait ad omnem populum Juda, dicens : [Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Sion quasi ager arabitur, et Jerusalem in acervum lapidum erit, et mons domus in excelsa silvarum.]
       EN: “Micah of Moresheth was a prophet in the days of Hezekiah king of Judah, and he said to all the people of Judah: ‘Thus says the Lord of hosts: Zion will be plowed like a field, Jerusalem will become a heap of stones, and the mountain of the house will become wooded heights.’

33:19  LA: Numquid morte condemnavit eum Ezechias rex Juda, et omnis Juda ? numquid non timuerunt Dominum, et deprecati sunt faciem Domini, et pœnituit Dominum mali quod locutus fuerat adversum eos ? Itaque nos facimus malum grande contra animas nostras.
       EN: Did Hezekiah king of Judah and all Judah condemn him to death? Did they not fear the Lord and entreat His favor, and did the Lord not relent concerning the disaster He had pronounced against them? Yet we are bringing great evil upon our own lives.”

33:20  LA: Fuit quoque vir prophetans in nomine Domini, Urias filius Semei de Cariathiarim, et prophetavit adversus civitatem istam, et adversus terram hanc, juxta omnia verba Jeremiæ.
       EN: There was also a man prophesying in the Lord's name, Uriah son of Shemaiah from Kiriath-jearim. He prophesied against this city and this land in accordance with all Jeremiah's words.

33:21  LA: Et audivit rex Joakim, et omnes potentes et principes ejus, verba hæc, et quæsivit rex interficere eum : et audivit Urias, et timuit, fugitque, et ingressus est Ægyptum.
       EN: King Jehoiakim, all his mighty men, and his rulers heard these words, and the king sought to kill him. Uriah heard, was afraid, fled, and entered Egypt.

33:22  LA: Et misit rex Joakim viros in Ægyptum, Elnathan filium Achobor, et viros cum eo, in Ægyptum,
       EN: King Jehoiakim sent men to Egypt, Elnathan son of Achbor and men with him, to Egypt.

33:23  LA: et eduxerunt Uriam de Ægypto, et adduxerunt eum ad regem Joakim, et percussit eum gladio, et projecit cadaver ejus in sepulchris vulgi ignobilis.
       EN: They brought Uriah out of Egypt and led him to King Jehoiakim, who struck him with the sword and threw his corpse into the graves of the lowly common people.

33:24  LA: Igitur manus Ahicam filii Saphan fuit cum Jeremia, ut non traderetur in manus populi, et interficerent eum.
       EN: But the hand of Ahikam son of Shaphan was with Jeremiah, so that he was not handed over to the people to be killed.

JEREMIAH 34

34:2  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus ad me : Fac tibi vincula et catenas, et pones eas in collo tuo,
      EN: “Thus says the Lord to me: Make yourself bonds and chains, and put them around your neck.

34:3  LA: et mittes eas ad regem Edom, et ad regem Moab, et ad regem filiorum Ammon, et ad regem Tyri, et ad regem Sidonis, in manu nuntiorum qui venerunt Jerusalem ad Sedeciam regem Juda.
      EN: Send them to the kings of Edom, Moab, the children of Ammon, Tyre, and Sidon, by the messengers who have come to Jerusalem to Zedekiah king of Judah.

34:4  LA: Et præcipies eis ut ad dominos suos loquantur : Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Hæc dicetis ad dominos vestros :
      EN: Command them to say to their masters: Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: This is what you shall tell your masters:

34:5  LA: Ego feci terram, et homines, et jumenta quæ sunt super faciem terræ, in fortitudine mea magna, et in brachio meo extento, et dedi eam ei qui placuit in oculis meis.
      EN: I made the earth, the people, and the animals upon the face of the earth by My great strength and My outstretched arm, and gave it to whoever pleased Me.

34:6  LA: Et nunc itaque ego dedi omnes terras istas in manu Nabuchodonosor regis Babylonis servi mei : insuper et bestias agri dedi ei, ut serviant illi :
      EN: Now, therefore, I have given all these lands into the hand of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon, My servant. I have also given him the beasts of the field to serve him.

34:8  LA: Gens autem et regnum quod non servierit Nabuchodonosor regi Babylonis, et quicumque non curvaverit collum suum sub jugo regis Babylonis, in gladio, et in fame, et in peste visitabo super gentem illam, ait Dominus, donec consumam eos in manu ejus.
      EN: But the nation and kingdom that will not serve Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon, and anyone who will not bend his neck beneath the yoke of the king of Babylon, I will punish that nation with sword, famine, and pestilence, says the Lord, until I consume them by his hand.

34:9  LA: Vos ergo nolite audire prophetas vestros, et divinos, et somniatores, et augures, et maleficos, qui dicunt vobis : Non servietis regi Babylonis :
      EN: So do not listen to your prophets, diviners, dreamers, augurs, and sorcerers who tell you, ‘You will not serve the king of Babylon.’

34:10  LA: quia mendacium prophetant vobis, ut longe vos faciant de terra vestra, et ejiciant vos, et pereatis.
       EN: For they prophesy lies to you, to remove you far from your land, drive you out, and cause you to perish.

34:11  LA: Porro gens quæ subjecerit cervicem suam sub jugo regis Babylonis, et servierit ei, dimittam eam in terra sua, dicit Dominus, et colet eam, et habitabit in ea.
       EN: But the nation that puts its neck beneath the yoke of the king of Babylon and serves him, I will leave in its own land, says the Lord; it will cultivate it and dwell in it.”

34:12  LA: Et ad Sedeciam regem Juda locutus sum secundum omnia verba hæc, dicens : Subjicite colla vestra sub jugo regis Babylonis, et servite ei et populo ejus, et vivetis.
       EN: I spoke to Zedekiah king of Judah according to all these words, saying, “Put your necks beneath the yoke of the king of Babylon; serve him and his people, and you will live.

34:14  LA: Nolite audire verba prophetarum dicentium vobis : Non servietis regi Babylonis : quia mendacium ipsi loquuntur vobis :
       EN: Do not listen to the words of the prophets who tell you, ‘You will not serve the king of Babylon,’ for they speak lies to you.

34:15  LA: quia non misi eos, ait Dominus, et ipsi prophetant in nomine meo mendaciter, ut ejiciant vos, et pereatis, tam vos quam prophetæ qui vaticinantur vobis.
       EN: For I did not send them, says the Lord, and they prophesy falsely in My name, to drive you out and cause you to perish, both you and the prophets who prophesy to you.”

34:16  LA: Et ad sacerdotes, et ad populum istum, locutus sum, dicens : Hæc dicit Dominus : Nolite audire verba prophetarum vestrorum, qui prophetant vobis, dicentes : Ecce vasa Domini revertentur de Babylone nunc cito : mendacium enim prophetant vobis.
       EN: I spoke to the priests and this people, saying, “Thus says the Lord: Do not listen to the words of your prophets who prophesy to you, ‘Look, the Lord's vessels will now soon return from Babylon,’ for they prophesy lies to you.

34:18  LA: Et si prophetæ sunt, et est verbum Domini in eis, occurrant Domino exercituum, ut non veniant vasa quæ derelicta fuerant in domo Domini, et in domo regis Juda, et in Jerusalem, in Babylonem.
       EN: If they are prophets and the word of the Lord is in them, let them entreat the Lord of hosts that the vessels left in the Lord's house, in the house of Judah's king, and in Jerusalem may not go to Babylon.

34:19  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus exercituum ad columnas, et ad mare, et ad bases, et ad reliqua vasorum quæ remanserunt in civitate hac,
       EN: For thus says the Lord of hosts concerning the pillars, the sea, the stands, and the rest of the vessels remaining in this city,

34:20  LA: quæ non tulit Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis cum transferret Jechoniam filium Joakim regem Juda de Jerusalem in Babylonem, et omnes optimates Juda et Jerusalem :
       EN: which Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon did not take when he deported Jeconiah son of Jehoiakim, king of Judah, from Jerusalem to Babylon, along with all the nobles of Judah and Jerusalem:

34:22  LA: In Babylonem transferentur, et ibi erunt usque ad diem visitationis suæ, dicit Dominus, et afferri faciam ea, et restitui in loco isto.
       EN: They will be carried to Babylon and remain there until the day of their visitation, says the Lord. Then I will have them brought back and restored to this place.”

JEREMIAH 35

35:1  LA: Et factum est in anno illo, in principio regni Sedeciæ regis Juda, in anno quarto, in mense quinto, dixit ad me Hananias filius Azur, propheta de Gabaon, in domo Domini, coram sacerdotibus et omni populo, dicens :
      EN: In that year, at the beginning of the reign of Zedekiah king of Judah, in the fourth year, in the fifth month, Hananiah son of Azzur, the prophet from Gibeon, spoke to me in the Lord's house before the priests and all the people, saying:

35:2  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Contrivi jugum regis Babylonis.
      EN: “Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: I have broken the yoke of the king of Babylon.

35:3  LA: Adhuc duo anni dierum, et ego referri faciam ad locum istum omnia vasa domus Domini, quæ tulit Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis de loco isto, et transtulit ea in Babylonem.
      EN: Within two full years I will have all the vessels of the Lord's house brought back to this place, which Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon took from here and carried to Babylon.

35:4  LA: Et Jechoniam filium Joakim regem Juda, et omnem transmigrationem Juda, qui ingressi sunt in Babylonem, ego convertam ad locum istum, ait Dominus : conteram enim jugum regis Babylonis.
      EN: I will bring Jeconiah son of Jehoiakim, king of Judah, and all Judah's exiles who went to Babylon back to this place, says the Lord, for I will break the yoke of the king of Babylon.”

35:5  LA: Et dixit Jeremias propheta ad Hananiam prophetam, in oculis sacerdotum, et in oculis omnis populi qui stabat in domo Domini :
      EN: Jeremiah the prophet spoke to Hananiah the prophet in the sight of the priests and all the people standing in the Lord's house.

35:6  LA: et ait Jeremias propheta : Amen ! sic faciat Dominus : suscitet Dominus verba tua quæ prophetasti, ut referantur vasa in domum Domini, et omnis transmigratio de Babylone ad locum istum.
      EN: Jeremiah the prophet said, “Amen! May the Lord do so! May the Lord fulfill the words you have prophesied, bringing the vessels back to the Lord's house and all the exiles from Babylon to this place.

35:7  LA: Verumtamen audi verbum hoc quod ego loquor in auribus tuis, et in auribus universi populi :
      EN: Nevertheless, hear this word that I speak in your hearing and in the hearing of all the people:

35:8  LA: prophetæ qui fuerunt ante me et ante te, ab initio, et prophetaverunt super terras multas et super regna magna de prælio, et de afflictione, et de fame :
      EN: the prophets who were before me and before you from the beginning prophesied against many lands and great kingdoms of war, affliction, and famine.

35:9  LA: propheta qui vaticinatus est pacem, cum venerit verbum ejus, scietur propheta quem misit Dominus in veritate.
      EN: As for the prophet who prophesies peace, when his word comes to pass, he will be known as a prophet whom the Lord has truly sent.”

35:10  LA: Et tulit Hananias propheta catenam de collo Jeremiæ prophetæ, et confregit eam.
       EN: Then Hananiah the prophet took the chain from Jeremiah the prophet's neck and broke it.

35:11  LA: Et ait Hananias in conspectu omnis populi, dicens : Hæc dicit Dominus : Sic confringam jugum Nabuchodonosor regis Babylonis, post duos annos dierum de collo omnium gentium.
       EN: Hananiah spoke before all the people, saying, “Thus says the Lord: In this way I will break the yoke of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon from the necks of all the nations after two full years.”

35:12  LA: Et abiit Jeremias propheta in viam suam. Et factum est verbum Domini ad Jeremiam, postquam confregit Hananias propheta catenam de collo Jeremiæ prophetæ, dicens :
       EN: Jeremiah the prophet went his way. The word of the Lord came to Jeremiah after Hananiah the prophet had broken the chain from Jeremiah the prophet's neck, saying:

35:13  LA: Vade, et dices Hananiæ : Hæc dicit Dominus : Catenas ligneas contrivisti, et facies pro eis catenas ferreas.
       EN: “Go and tell Hananiah: Thus says the Lord: You have broken wooden chains, but you will make iron chains in their place.

35:14  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Jugum ferreum posui super collum cunctarum gentium istarum, ut serviant Nabuchodonosor regi Babylonis, et servient ei : insuper et bestias terræ dedi ei.
       EN: For thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: I have put an iron yoke upon the necks of all these nations, that they may serve Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon, and they will serve him. I have also given him the beasts of the earth.”

35:15  LA: Et dixit Jeremias propheta ad Hananiam prophetam : Audi, Hanania : non misit te Dominus, et tu confidere fecisti populum istum in mendacio.
       EN: Jeremiah the prophet said to Hananiah the prophet, “Listen, Hananiah: the Lord did not send you, and you have made this people trust in falsehood.

35:16  LA: Idcirco hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego mittam te a facie terræ : hoc anno morieris : adversum enim Dominum locutus es.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord: Look, I will send you away from the face of the earth. This year you will die, because you have spoken against the Lord.”

35:17  LA: Et mortuus est Hananias propheta in anno illo, mense septimo.
       EN: Hananiah the prophet died that year, in the seventh month.

JEREMIAH 36

36:1  LA: Et hæc sunt verba libri quem misit Jeremias propheta de Jerusalem ad reliquias seniorum transmigrationis, et ad sacerdotes, et ad prophetas, et ad omnem populum quem traduxerat Nabuchodonosor de Jerusalem in Babylonem,
      EN: These are the words of the letter that Jeremiah the prophet sent from Jerusalem to the surviving elders of the exiles, to the priests, to the prophets, and to all the people whom Nebuchadnezzar had carried from Jerusalem to Babylon,

36:2  LA: postquam egressus est Jechonias rex, et domina, et eunuchi, et principes Juda et Jerusalem, et faber et inclusor, de Jerusalem,
      EN: after King Jeconiah, the queen mother, the eunuchs, the rulers of Judah and Jerusalem, the craftsmen, and the metalworkers had left Jerusalem,

36:3  LA: in manu Elasa filii Saphan, et Gamariæ filii Helciæ, quos misit Sedecias rex Juda ad Nabuchodonosor regem Babylonis in Babylonem, dicens :
      EN: by the hand of Elasah son of Shaphan and Gemariah son of Hilkiah, whom Zedekiah king of Judah sent to Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon in Babylon, saying:

36:4  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël, omni transmigrationi quam transtuli de Jerusalem in Babylonem :
      EN: “Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel, to all the exiles whom I deported from Jerusalem to Babylon:

36:5  LA: Ædificate domos, et habitate : et plantate hortos, et comedite fructum eorum.
      EN: Build houses and live in them; plant gardens and eat their fruit.

36:6  LA: Accipite uxores, et generate filios et filias : et date filiis vestris uxores, et filias vestras date viris, et pariant filios et filias : et multiplicamini ibi, et nolite esse pauci numero.
      EN: Take wives and beget sons and daughters. Give wives to your sons and husbands to your daughters, so that they may bear sons and daughters. Multiply there and do not become few in number.

36:7  LA: Et quærite pacem civitatis ad quam transmigrare vos feci, et orate pro ea ad Dominum, quia in pace illius erit pax vobis.
      EN: Seek the peace of the city to which I have deported you, and pray to the Lord for it, for in its peace you will have peace.

36:8  LA: Hæc enim dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Non vos seducant prophetæ vestri qui sunt in medio vestrum, et divini vestri, et ne attendatis ad somnia vestra quæ vos somniatis :
      EN: For thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Do not let your prophets and diviners among you mislead you, and do not pay attention to the dreams that you yourselves dream.

36:9  LA: quia falso ipsi prophetant vobis in nomine meo, et non misi eos, dicit Dominus.
      EN: For they prophesy falsely to you in My name, and I did not send them, says the Lord.

36:10  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus : Cum cœperint impleri in Babylone septuaginta anni, visitabo vos, et suscitabo super vos verbum meum bonum, ut reducam vos ad locum istum.
       EN: For thus says the Lord: When seventy years in Babylon begin to reach their fulfillment, I will visit you and fulfill My good word concerning you, to bring you back to this place.

36:11  LA: Ego enim scio cogitationes quas ego cogito super vos, ait Dominus, cogitationes pacis et non afflictionis, ut dem vobis finem et patientiam.
       EN: For I know the thoughts that I think concerning you, says the Lord, thoughts of peace and not affliction, to give you an outcome and patient waiting.

36:12  LA: Et invocabitis me, et ibitis : et orabitis me, et ego exaudiam vos.
       EN: You will call upon Me and go; you will pray to Me, and I will hear you.

36:13  LA: Quæretis me, et invenietis, cum quæsieritis me in toto corde vestro.
       EN: You will seek Me and find Me when you seek Me with your whole heart.

36:14  LA: Et inveniar a vobis, ait Dominus : et reducam captivitatem vestram, et congregabo vos de universis gentibus et de cunctis locis ad quæ expuli vos, dicit Dominus, et reverti vos faciam de loco ad quem transmigrare vos feci.
       EN: I will be found by you, says the Lord. I will bring back your captives and gather you from all the nations and all the places to which I drove you, says the Lord, and cause you to return from the place to which I deported you.

36:15  LA: Quia dixistis : Suscitavit nobis Dominus prophetas in Babylone :
       EN: Because you have said, ‘The Lord has raised up prophets for us in Babylon,’

36:21  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël, ad Achab filium Coliæ, et ad Sedeciam filium Maasiæ, qui prophetant vobis in nomine meo mendaciter : Ecce ego tradam eos in manus Nabuchodonosor regis Babylonis, et percutiet eos in oculis vestris :
       EN: Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel, concerning Ahab son of Kolaiah and Zedekiah son of Maaseiah, who prophesy falsely to you in My name: Look, I will hand them over to Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon, and he will strike them down before your eyes.

36:22  LA: et assumetur ex eis maledictio omni transmigrationi Juda quæ est in Babylone, dicentium : Ponat te Dominus sicut Sedeciam et sicut Achab, quos frixit rex Babylonis in igne :
       EN: From them a curse will be taken up by all Judah's exiles in Babylon, saying, ‘May the Lord make you like Zedekiah and Ahab, whom the king of Babylon roasted in the fire,’

36:23  LA: pro eo quod fecerint stultitiam in Israël, et mœchati sunt in uxores amicorum suorum, et locuti sunt verbum in nomine meo mendaciter, quod non mandavi eis. Ego sum judex et testis, dicit Dominus.
       EN: because they committed folly in Israel, committed adultery with their friends' wives, and falsely spoke a word in My name that I had not commanded them. I am judge and witness, says the Lord.

36:24  LA: Et ad Semeiam Nehelamiten dices :
       EN: To Shemaiah the Nehelamite you shall say:

36:25  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Pro eo quod misisti in nomine tuo libros ad omnem populum qui est in Jerusalem, et ad Sophoniam filium Maasiæ sacerdotem, et ad universos sacerdotes, dicens :
       EN: Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Because you sent letters in your own name to all the people in Jerusalem, to the priest Zephaniah son of Maaseiah, and to all the priests, saying,

36:26  LA: Dominus dedit te sacerdotem pro Jojade sacerdote, ut sis dux in domo Domini, super omnem virum arreptitium et prophetantem, ut mittas eum in nervum et in carcerem :
       EN: ‘The Lord has made you priest in place of Jehoiada the priest, to be an officer in the Lord's house over every frenzied man who prophesies, to put him in the stocks and in prison.

36:27  LA: et nunc quare non increpasti Jeremiam Anathothiten, qui prophetat vobis ?
       EN: So now, why have you not rebuked Jeremiah of Anathoth, who prophesies to you?

36:28  LA: Quia super hoc misit in Babylonem ad nos, dicens : Longum est : ædificate domos, et habitate : et plantate hortos, et comedite fructus eorum.
       EN: For he has sent this message to us in Babylon: “It will be long; build houses and live in them, plant gardens and eat their fruit.”’”

36:29  LA: Legit ergo Sophonias sacerdos librum istum in auribus Jeremiæ prophetæ.
       EN: The priest Zephaniah read this letter in Jeremiah the prophet's hearing.

36:30  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad Jeremiam, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to Jeremiah, saying:

36:31  LA: Mitte ad omnem transmigrationem, dicens : Hæc dicit Dominus ad Semeiam Nehelamiten : Pro eo quod prophetavit vobis Semeias, et ego non misi eum, et fecit vos confidere in mendacio,
       EN: “Send to all the exiles, saying: Thus says the Lord concerning Shemaiah the Nehelamite: Because Shemaiah prophesied to you, though I did not send him, and made you trust in falsehood,

36:32  LA: idcirco hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego visitabo super Semeiam Nehelamiten, et super semen ejus : non erit ei vir sedens in medio populi hujus, et non videbit bonum quod ego faciam populo meo, ait Dominus, quia prævaricationem locutus est adversus Dominum.
       EN: therefore thus says the Lord: Look, I will punish Shemaiah the Nehelamite and his offspring. He will have no man dwelling among this people and will not see the good I will do for My people, says the Lord, because he has spoken rebellion against the Lord.”

JEREMIAH 37

37:1  LA: Hoc verbum quod factum est ad Jeremiam a Domino, dicens :
      EN: This is the word that came to Jeremiah from the Lord:

37:2  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus Israël, dicens : Scribe tibi omnia verba quæ locutus sum ad te, in libro.
      EN: “Thus says the Lord, the God of Israel: Write for yourself in a book all the words I have spoken to you.

37:3  LA: Ecce enim dies veniunt, dicit Dominus, et convertam conversionem populi mei Israël et Juda, ait Dominus : et convertam eos ad terram quam dedi patribus eorum, et possidebunt eam.
      EN: For behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will restore the fortunes of My people Israel and Judah, says the Lord. I will bring them back to the land I gave their fathers, and they will possess it.”

37:4  LA: Et hæc verba quæ locutus est Dominus ad Israël et ad Judam :
      EN: These are the words the Lord spoke to Israel and Judah:

37:5  LA: [Quoniam hæc dicit Dominus : Vocem terroris audivimus : formido, et non est pax.
      EN: “Thus says the Lord: We have heard a cry of terror, fear, and no peace.

37:6  LA: Interrogate, et videte si generat masculus : quare ergo vidi omnis viri manum super lumbum suum, quasi parturientis, et conversæ sunt universæ facies in auruginem ?
      EN: Ask and see whether a male gives birth. Why, then, have I seen every man with his hand on his loins like a woman in labor, and every face turned yellow?

37:7  LA: Væ ! quia magna dies illa, nec est similis ejus : tempusque tribulationis est Jacob, et ex ipso salvabitur.
      EN: Alas! That day is great, and none is like it. It is a time of distress for Jacob, yet he will be saved from it.

37:8  LA: Et erit in die illa, ait Dominus exercituum : conteram jugum ejus de collo tuo, et vincula ejus dirumpam, et non dominabuntur ei amplius alieni :
      EN: On that day, says the Lord of hosts, I will break his yoke from your neck and tear apart his bonds. Foreigners will no longer rule over him,

37:9  LA: sed servient Domino Deo suo, et David regi suo, quem suscitabo eis.
      EN: but they will serve the Lord their God and David their king, whom I will raise up for them.

37:12  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus : Insanabilis fractura tua ; pessima plaga tua :
       EN: For thus says the Lord: Your fracture is incurable, your wound grievous.

37:13  LA: non est qui judicet judicium tuum ad alligandum : curationum utilitas non est tibi.
       EN: There is no one to plead your case for binding up your wound; no healing treatment avails you.

37:14  LA: Omnes amatores tui obliti sunt tui, teque non quærent : plaga enim inimici percussi te castigatione crudeli : propter multitudinem iniquitatis tuæ dura facta sunt peccata tua.
       EN: All your lovers have forgotten you and will not seek you. For I have struck you with an enemy’s blow, with cruel discipline. Because of your many iniquities, your sins have become grievous.

37:16  LA: Quid clamas super contritione tua ? insanabilis est dolor tuus : propter multitudinem iniquitatis tuæ, et propter dura peccata tua, feci hæc tibi.
       EN: Why do you cry out over your ruin? Your pain is incurable. Because of your many iniquities and your grievous sins, I have done these things to you.

37:17  LA: Obducam enim cicatricem tibi, et a vulneribus tuis sanabo te, dicit Dominus. Quia ejectam vocaverunt te, Sion : hæc est, quæ non habebat requirentem.
       EN: For I will close your wound and heal you of your injuries, says the Lord, because they have called you an outcast, Zion: ‘This is the one whom no one seeks.’

37:18  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego convertam conversionem tabernaculorum Jacob, et tectis ejus miserebor : et ædificabitur civitas in excelso suo, et templum juxta ordinem suum fundabitur :
       EN: Thus says the Lord: Behold, I will restore the fortunes of Jacob’s tents and have mercy on his dwellings. The city will be built upon its height, and the temple will be established according to its order.

37:19  LA: et egredietur de eis laus, voxque ludentium. Et multiplicabo eos, et non minuentur : et glorificabo eos, et non attenuabuntur.
       EN: Praise will come from them, and the voices of those who make merry. I will multiply them, and they will not diminish; I will glorify them, and they will not be brought low.

37:20  LA: Et erunt filii ejus sicut a principio, et cœtus ejus coram me permanebit, et visitabo adversum omnes qui tribulant eum.
       EN: His children will be as at the beginning, and his assembly will remain before Me. I will call to account all who afflict him.

37:21  LA: Et erit dux ejus ex eo, et princeps de medio ejus producetur : et applicabo eum, et accedet ad me. Quis enim iste est qui applicet cor suum ut appropinquet mihi ? ait Dominus :
       EN: His leader will be one of his own, and his ruler will come forth from his midst. I will bring him near, and he will approach Me. For who is this who sets his heart on approaching Me? says the Lord.

37:23  LA: Ecce turbo Domini, furor egrediens, procella ruens : in capite impiorum conquiescet.
       EN: Behold the whirlwind of the Lord! Fury goes forth, a rushing storm; it will settle upon the heads of the wicked.

37:24  LA: Non avertet iram indignationis Dominus, donec faciat et compleat cogitationem cordis sui : in novissimo dierum intelligetis ea.]
       EN: The Lord will not turn back His fierce anger until He has acted and fulfilled the purpose of His heart. In the last days you will understand these things.”

JEREMIAH 38

38:1  LA: In tempore illo, dicit Dominus, ero Deus universis cognationibus Israël, et ipsi erunt mihi in populum.
      EN: “At that time, says the Lord, I will be God to all the families of Israel, and they will be My people.”

38:2  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Invenit gratiam in deserto populus qui remanserat a gladio : vadet ad requiem suam Israël.
      EN: Thus says the Lord: “The people who survived the sword found favor in the wilderness. Israel will go to its rest.”

38:3  LA: Longe Dominus apparuit mihi. Et in caritate perpetua dilexi te : ideo attraxi te, miserans.
      EN: The Lord appeared to me from afar: “I have loved you with an everlasting love; therefore in mercy I have drawn you to Myself.

38:4  LA: Rursumque ædificabo te, et ædificaberis, virgo Israël : adhuc ornaberis tympanis tuis, et egredieris in choro ludentium.
      EN: Again I will build you, and you will be rebuilt, virgin Israel. Again you will adorn yourself with your tambourines and go out in the dance of those who make merry.

38:5  LA: Adhuc plantabis vineas in montibus Samariæ : plantabunt plantantes, et donec tempus veniat, non vindemiabunt.
      EN: Again you will plant vineyards on the mountains of Samaria. The planters will plant, and they will not gather grapes until the time comes.

38:6  LA: Quia erit dies in qua clamabunt custodes in monte Ephraim : Surgite, et ascendamus in Sion ad Dominum Deum nostrum.
      EN: For a day will come when the watchmen cry out on Mount Ephraim, ‘Rise, and let us go up to Zion, to the Lord our God.’

38:7  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus : Exsultate in lætitia, Jacob, et hinnite contra caput gentium : personate, et canite, et dicite : Salva, Domine, populum tuum, reliquias Israël.
      EN: For thus says the Lord: Exult with joy, Jacob, and cry aloud against the chief of the nations. Raise your voices, sing, and say, ‘Save Your people, Lord, the remnant of Israel.’

38:8  LA: Ecce ego adducam eos de terra aquilonis, et congregabo eos ab extremis terræ : inter quos erunt cæcus et claudus, prægnans et pariens simul, cœtus magnus revertentium huc.
      EN: Behold, I will bring them from the land of the north and gather them from the ends of the earth. Among them will be the blind and the lame, the pregnant woman and the woman in labor together, a great assembly returning here.

38:9  LA: In fletu venient, et in misericordia reducam eos : et adducam eos per torrentes aquarum in via recta, et non impingent in ea, quia factus sum Israëli pater, et Ephraim primogenitus meus est.
      EN: They will come in tears, and in mercy I will lead them back. I will bring them beside streams of water on a straight path where they will not stumble, because I have become a father to Israel, and Ephraim is My firstborn.

38:10  LA: Audite verbum Domini, gentes, et annuntiate in insulis quæ procul sunt, et dicite : Qui dispersit Israël congregabit eum, et custodiet eum sicut pastor gregem suum.
       EN: Hear the word of the Lord, nations, and proclaim it in the distant islands. Say, ‘He who scattered Israel will gather him and guard him as a shepherd guards his flock.’

38:11  LA: Redemit enim Dominus Jacob, et liberavit eum de manu potentioris.
       EN: For the Lord has redeemed Jacob and freed him from the hand of one stronger than he.

38:12  LA: Et venient, et laudabunt in monte Sion : et confluent ad bona Domini, super frumento, et vino, et oleo, et fœtu pecorum et armentorum : eritque anima eorum quasi hortus irriguus, et ultra non esurient.
       EN: They will come and sing praise on Mount Zion. They will flow together toward the good things of the Lord, toward grain, wine, oil, and the young of flocks and herds. Their soul will be like a watered garden, and they will hunger no more.

38:13  LA: Tunc lætabitur virgo in choro, juvenes et senes simul : et convertam luctum eorum in gaudium, et consolabor eos, et lætificabo a dolore suo.
       EN: Then the virgin will rejoice in the dance, and young men and old together. I will turn their mourning into joy, comfort them, and give them gladness in place of their sorrow.

38:14  LA: Et inebriabo animam sacerdotum pinguedine, et populus meus bonis meis adimplebitur, ait Dominus.
       EN: I will fill the souls of the priests to overflowing with rich abundance, and My people will be filled with My good things, says the Lord.

38:15  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Vox in excelso audita est lamentationis : luctus, et fletus Rachel plorantis filios suos, et nolentis consolari super eis, quia non sunt.
       EN: Thus says the Lord: A voice of lamentation has been heard on high, mourning and the weeping of Rachel for her children. She refuses to be comforted over them, because they are no more.

38:16  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Quiescat vox tua a ploratu, et oculi tui a lacrimis, quia est merces operi tuo, ait Dominus, et revertentur de terra inimici :
       EN: Thus says the Lord: Let your voice cease from weeping and your eyes from tears, for there is a reward for your labor, says the Lord. They will return from the enemy’s land.

38:17  LA: et est spes novissimis tuis, ait Dominus, et revertentur filii ad terminos suos.
       EN: There is hope for your future, says the Lord, and your children will return to their own borders.

38:18  LA: Audiens audivi Ephraim transmigrantem : Castigasti me, et eruditus sum, quasi juvenculus indomitus : converte me, et convertar, quia tu Dominus Deus meus.
       EN: I have indeed heard Ephraim in exile: ‘You disciplined me, and I was instructed, like an untamed young bull. Turn me back, and I will return, for You are the Lord my God.

38:19  LA: Postquam enim convertisti me, egi pœnitentiam : et postquam ostendisti mihi, percussi femur meum. Confusus sum, et erubui, quoniam sustinui opprobrium adolescentiæ meæ.
       EN: After You turned me back, I repented; after You made me understand, I struck my thigh. I was ashamed and blushed, because I bore the reproach of my youth.’

38:20  LA: Si filius honorabilis mihi Ephraim, si puer delicatus ! quia ex quo locutus sum de eo, adhuc recordabor ejus. Idcirco conturbata sunt viscera mea super eum : miserans miserebor ejus, ait Dominus.
       EN: Is Ephraim not an honored son to Me, a cherished child? For ever since I spoke of him, I still remember him. Therefore My inmost being is stirred for him; I will surely have mercy on him, says the Lord.

38:21  LA: Statue tibi speculam ; pone tibi amaritudines ; dirige cor tuum in viam rectam in qua ambulasti : revertere, virgo Israël, revertere ad civitates tuas istas.
       EN: Set up a watchtower for yourself; set before yourself bitter things. Direct your heart to the straight road on which you walked. Return, virgin Israel; return to these cities of yours.

38:22  LA: Usquequo deliciis dissolveris, filia vaga ? quia creavit Dominus novum super terram : femina circumdabit virum.
       EN: How long will you be weakened by delights, wandering daughter? For the Lord has created a new thing upon the earth: a woman will surround a man.

38:23  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Adhuc dicent verbum istud in terra Juda et in urbibus ejus, cum convertero captivitatem eorum : Benedicat tibi Dominus, pulchritudo justitiæ, mons sanctus :
       EN: Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: They will again say these words in the land of Judah and its cities when I restore their captives: ‘May the Lord bless you, beauty of justice, holy mountain.’

38:24  LA: et habitabunt in eo Judas et omnes civitates ejus simul, agricolæ et minantes greges.
       EN: Judah and all its cities will dwell there together, farmers and those who drive the flocks.

38:25  LA: Quia inebriavi animam lassam, et omnem animam esurientem saturavi.
       EN: For I have filled the weary soul to overflowing and satisfied every hungry soul.”

38:26  LA: Ideo quasi de somno suscitatus sum : et vidi, et somnus meus dulcis mihi.
       EN: At this I awoke as from sleep and looked, and my sleep had been sweet to me.

38:27  LA: Ecce dies veniunt, dicit Dominus, et seminabo domum Israël et domum Juda semine hominum et semine jumentorum.
       EN: “Behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will sow the house of Israel and the house of Judah with the seed of human beings and the seed of animals.

38:28  LA: Et sicut vigilavi super eos ut evellerem, et demolirer, et dissiparem, et disperderem, et affligerem, sic vigilabo super eos ut ædificem et plantem, ait Dominus.
       EN: As I watched over them to uproot, tear down, scatter, destroy, and afflict, so I will watch over them to build and plant, says the Lord.

38:29  LA: In diebus illis non dicent ultra : Patres comederunt uvam acerbam, et dentes filiorum obstupuerunt.
       EN: In those days they will no longer say, ‘The fathers have eaten sour grapes, and the children’s teeth are set on edge.’

38:30  LA: Sed unusquisque in iniquitate sua morietur : omnis homo qui comederit uvam acerbam, obstupescent dentes ejus.
       EN: Instead, each will die for his own iniquity. Everyone who eats sour grapes will have his own teeth set on edge.

38:31  LA: Ecce dies venient, dicit Dominus, et feriam domui Israël et domui Juda fœdus novum,
       EN: Behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when I will make a new covenant with the house of Israel and the house of Judah,

38:32  LA: non secundum pactum quod pepigi cum patribus eorum in die qua apprehendi manum eorum ut educerem eos de terra Ægypti, pactum quod irritum fecerunt : et ego dominatus sum eorum, dicit Dominus.
       EN: unlike the covenant I made with their fathers on the day I took them by the hand to lead them out of the land of Egypt, the covenant they made void, although I was their master, says the Lord.

38:33  LA: Sed hoc erit pactum quod feriam cum domo Israël post dies illos, dicit Dominus : dabo legem meam in visceribus eorum, et in corde eorum scribam eam, et ero eis in Deum, et ipsi erunt mihi in populum :
       EN: But this is the covenant I will make with the house of Israel after those days, says the Lord: I will put My law within them and write it upon their heart. I will be their God, and they will be My people.

38:34  LA: et non docebit ultra vir proximum suum et vir fratrem suum, dicens : Cognosce Dominum : omnes enim cognoscent me, a minimo eorum usque ad maximum, ait Dominus : quia propitiabor iniquitati eorum, et peccati eorum non memorabor amplius.
       EN: No longer will one teach his neighbor or his brother, saying, ‘Know the Lord,’ for all will know Me, from the least of them to the greatest, says the Lord. For I will forgive their iniquity and remember their sin no more.

38:35  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Si mensurari potuerint cæli sursum, et investigari fundamenta terræ deorsum, et ego abjiciam universum semen Israël, propter omnia quæ fecerunt, dicit Dominus.
       EN: Thus says the Lord: If the heavens above can be measured and the foundations of the earth below explored, then I too will reject all the offspring of Israel for everything they have done, says the Lord.

38:36  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus qui dat solem in lumine diei, ordinem lunæ et stellarum in lumine noctis : qui turbat mare, et sonant fluctus ejus : Dominus exercituum nomen illi :
       EN: Thus says the Lord, who gives the sun for light by day and the ordered course of moon and stars for light by night, who stirs the sea so that its waves roar, whose name is the Lord of hosts:

38:37  LA: Si defecerint leges istæ coram me, dicit Dominus, tunc et semen Israël deficiet, ut non sit gens coram me cunctis diebus.
       EN: If these ordinances fail before Me, says the Lord, then the offspring of Israel too will cease to be a nation before Me forever.

38:38  LA: Ecce dies veniunt, dicit Dominus, et ædificabitur civitas Domino, a turre Hananeel usque ad portam anguli.
       EN: Behold, days are coming, says the Lord, when the city will be rebuilt for the Lord, from the tower of Hananel to the Corner Gate.

38:39  LA: Et exibit ultra norma mensuræ in conspectu ejus super collem Gareb, et circuibit Goatha,
       EN: The measuring line will extend farther before it over the hill of Gareb and turn around Goah.

38:40  LA: et omnem vallem cadaverum, et cineris, et universam regionem mortis usque ad torrentem Cedron, et usque ad angulum portæ equorum orientalis, Sanctum Domini : non evelletur, et non destruetur ultra in perpetuum.]
       EN: The whole valley of corpses and ashes and the whole region of death, as far as the Kidron torrent and as far as the corner of the Horse Gate toward the east, will be holy to the Lord. It will never again be uprooted or destroyed.”

JEREMIAH 39

39:1  LA: Verbum quod factum est ad Jeremiam a Domino, in anno decimo Sedeciæ regis Juda, ipse est annus decimusoctavus Nabuchodonosor.
      EN: The word that came to Jeremiah from the Lord in the tenth year of Zedekiah king of Judah, which was the eighteenth year of Nebuchadnezzar.

39:2  LA: Tunc exercitus regis Babylonis obsidebat Jerusalem, et Jeremias propheta erat clausus in atrio carceris qui erat in domo regis Juda.
      EN: At that time the army of the king of Babylon was besieging Jerusalem, and Jeremiah the prophet was confined in the court of the prison in the palace of the king of Judah.

39:3  LA: Clauserat enim eum Sedecias rex Juda, dicens : Quare vaticinaris, dicens : Hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego dabo civitatem istam in manus regis Babylonis, et capiet eam :
      EN: Zedekiah king of Judah had confined him, saying, “Why do you prophesy, ‘Thus says the Lord: Behold, I will give this city into the hands of the king of Babylon, and he will capture it.

39:4  LA: et Sedecias rex Juda non effugiet de manu Chaldæorum, sed tradetur in manus regis Babylonis : et loquetur os ejus cum ore illius, et oculi ejus oculos illius videbunt :
      EN: Zedekiah king of Judah will not escape from the hand of the Chaldeans but will be delivered into the hands of the king of Babylon. He will speak with him mouth to mouth and see him eye to eye.

39:5  LA: et in Babylonem ducet Sedeciam, et ibi erit donec visitem eum, ait Dominus : si autem dimicaveritis adversum Chaldæos, nihil prosperum habebitis ?
      EN: He will take Zedekiah to Babylon, and he will remain there until I visit him, says the Lord. If you fight against the Chaldeans, you will have no success’?”

39:6  LA: Et dixit Jeremias : Factum est verbum Domini ad me, dicens :
      EN: Jeremiah said, “The word of the Lord came to me:

39:7  LA: Ecce Hanameel filius Sellum, patruelis tuus, veniet ad te, dicens : Eme tibi agrum meum qui est in Anathoth, tibi enim competit ex propinquitate ut emas.
      EN: ‘Behold, Hanamel son of Shallum, your cousin, will come to you and say, “Buy my field in Anathoth, for the right to buy it belongs to you as a relative.”’

39:8  LA: Et venit ad me Hanameel filius patrui mei, secundum verbum Domini, ad vestibulum carceris, et ait ad me : Posside agrum meum qui est in Anathoth, in terra Benjamin, quia tibi competit hæreditas, et tu propinquus es ut possideas. Intellexi autem quod verbum Domini esset :
      EN: Hanamel, my uncle’s son, came to me in the entrance court of the prison, according to the word of the Lord. He said to me, ‘Acquire my field in Anathoth, in the land of Benjamin, for the inheritance belongs to you, and as the relative you have the right to acquire it.’ Then I knew that this was the word of the Lord.

39:9  LA: et emi agrum ab Hanameel filio patrui mei, qui est in Anathoth, et appendi ei argentum : septem stateres, et decem argenteos.
      EN: I bought the field in Anathoth from Hanamel, my uncle’s son, and weighed out the silver for him: seven staters and ten silver pieces.

39:10  LA: Et scripsi in libro, et signavi, et adhibui testes, et appendi argentum in statera.
       EN: I wrote the deed, sealed it, summoned witnesses, and weighed the silver in a balance.

39:11  LA: Et accepi librum possessionis signatum, et stipulationes, et rata, et signa forinsecus :
       EN: I took the sealed deed of possession, with the stipulations, ratified terms, and external marks,

39:12  LA: et dedi librum possessionis Baruch filio Neri filii Maasiæ, in oculis Hanameel patruelis mei, in oculis testium qui scripti erant in libro emptionis, et in oculis omnium Judæorum qui sedebant in atrio carceris.
       EN: and gave the deed of possession to Baruch son of Neriah, son of Maaseiah, in the sight of my cousin Hanamel, in the sight of the witnesses whose names were written in the purchase deed, and in the sight of all the Jews sitting in the court of the prison.

39:13  LA: Et præcepi Baruch coram eis, dicens :
       EN: In their presence I gave Baruch this command:

39:14  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Sume libros istos, librum emptionis hunc signatum, et librum hunc qui apertus est, et pone illos in vase fictili, ut permanere possint diebus multis :
       EN: ‘Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Take these deeds, this sealed purchase deed and this open deed, and put them in an earthenware vessel, so that they may be preserved for many days.

39:15  LA: hæc enim dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Adhuc possidebuntur domus, et agri, et vineæ in terra ista.
       EN: For thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Houses, fields, and vineyards will again be possessed in this land.’

39:16  LA: Et oravi ad Dominum, postquam tradidi librum possessionis Baruch filio Neri, dicens :
       EN: After I had given the deed of possession to Baruch son of Neriah, I prayed to the Lord:

39:17  LA: [Heu ! heu ! heu ! Domine Deus, ecce tu fecisti cælum et terram in fortitudine tua magna, et in brachio tuo extento : non erit tibi difficile omne verbum :
       EN: ‘Ah, ah, ah, Lord God! Behold, You made heaven and earth by Your great power and Your outstretched arm. Nothing will be too difficult for You.

39:18  LA: qui facis misericordiam in millibus, et reddis iniquitatem patrum in sinum filiorum eorum post eos : fortissime, magne, et potens, Dominus exercituum nomen tibi.
       EN: You show mercy to thousands and repay the iniquity of fathers into the laps of their children after them. Most strong, great, and mighty, Your name is the Lord of hosts.

39:19  LA: Magnus consilio, et incomprehensibilis cogitatu : cujus oculi aperti sunt super omnes vias filiorum Adam, ut reddas unicuique secundum vias suas, et secundum fructum adinventionum ejus.
       EN: You are great in counsel and unfathomable in thought. Your eyes are open upon all the ways of the children of Adam, to repay each according to his ways and according to the fruit of his deeds.

39:20  LA: Qui posuisti signa et portenta in terra Ægypti usque ad diem hanc, et in Israël, et in hominibus, et fecisti tibi nomen sicut est dies hæc.
       EN: You have performed signs and wonders in the land of Egypt to this day, and in Israel and among humankind, and have made for Yourself a name such as You have today.

39:21  LA: Et eduxisti populum tuum Israël de terra Ægypti, in signis et in portentis, et in manu robusta et in brachio extento, et in terrore magno :
       EN: You brought Your people Israel out of the land of Egypt with signs and wonders, with a mighty hand and an outstretched arm, and with great terror.

39:22  LA: et dedisti eis terram hanc, quam jurasti patribus eorum ut dares eis, terram fluentem lacte et melle.
       EN: You gave them this land, which You swore to their fathers You would give them, a land flowing with milk and honey.

39:23  LA: Et ingressi sunt, et possederunt eam, et non obedierunt voci tuæ, et in lege tua non ambulaverunt : omnia quæ mandasti eis ut facerent non fecerunt, et evenerunt eis omnia mala hæc.
       EN: They entered and possessed it, but did not obey Your voice or walk in Your law. They did none of the things You commanded them to do, and all these calamities came upon them.

39:24  LA: Ecce munitiones exstructæ sunt adversum civitatem ut capiatur, et urbs data est in manus Chaldæorum qui præliantur adversus eam, a facie gladii, et famis, et pestilentiæ : et quæcumque locutus es, acciderunt, ut tu ipse cernis.
       EN: Behold, siegeworks have been raised against the city to capture it, and the city has been given into the hands of the Chaldeans who fight against it, because of sword, famine, and pestilence. Everything You said has happened, as You Yourself see.

39:25  LA: Et tu dicis mihi, Domine Deus : Eme agrum argento, et adhibe testes, cum urbs data sit in manus Chaldæorum ?]
       EN: Yet You say to me, Lord God, “Buy the field with silver and summon witnesses,” although the city has been given into the hands of the Chaldeans?’”

39:26  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad Jeremiam, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to Jeremiah:

39:27  LA: [Ecce ego Dominus Deus universæ carnis : numquid mihi difficile erit omne verbum ?
       EN: “Behold, I am the Lord, the God of all flesh. Will anything be too difficult for Me?

39:28  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego tradam civitatem istam in manus Chaldæorum, et in manus regis Babylonis, et capient eam.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord: Behold, I will deliver this city into the hands of the Chaldeans and into the hands of the king of Babylon, and they will capture it.

39:29  LA: Et venient Chaldæi præliantes adversum urbem hanc, et succendent eam igni, et comburent eam, et domos in quarum domatibus sacrificabant Baal, et libabant diis alienis libamina ad irritandum me.
       EN: The Chaldeans who fight against this city will come and set it on fire. They will burn it, together with the houses on whose roofs people sacrificed to Baal and poured out drink offerings to foreign gods to provoke Me.

39:30  LA: Erant enim filii Israël et filii Juda jugiter facientes malum in oculis meis ab adolescentia sua : filii Israël, qui usque nunc exacerbant me in opere manuum suarum, dicit Dominus.
       EN: For the children of Israel and the children of Judah have continually done evil in My sight from their youth: the children of Israel, who even now embitter Me by the work of their hands, says the Lord.

39:31  LA: Quia in furore et in indignatione mea facta est mihi civitas hæc, a die qua ædificaverunt eam usque ad diem istam qua auferetur de conspectu meo,
       EN: This city has stirred My fury and indignation from the day they built it until this day when it will be removed from My sight,

39:32  LA: propter malitiam filiorum Israël et filiorum Juda, quam fecerunt ad iracundiam me provocantes, ipsi et reges eorum, principes eorum, et sacerdotes eorum, et prophetæ eorum, viri Juda et habitatores Jerusalem.
       EN: because of all the wickedness the children of Israel and the children of Judah have committed to provoke Me to anger: they, their kings, their rulers, their priests, their prophets, the men of Judah, and the inhabitants of Jerusalem.

39:33  LA: Et verterunt ad me terga, et non facies, cum docerem eos diluculo et erudirem, et nollent audire, ut acciperent disciplinam.
       EN: They turned their backs to Me and not their faces. Though I taught them from early morning and instructed them, they would not listen and receive discipline.

39:34  LA: Et posuerunt idola sua in domo in qua invocatum est nomen meum, ut polluerent eam.
       EN: They placed their idols in the house over which My name is invoked, to defile it.

39:35  LA: Et ædificaverunt excelsa Baal quæ sunt in valle filii Ennom, ut initiarent filios suos et filias suas Moloch, quod non mandavi eis, nec ascendit in cor meum ut facerent abominationem hanc : et in peccatum deducerent Judam.
       EN: They built the high places of Baal in the valley of the son of Hinnom to consecrate their sons and daughters to Molech. I did not command them to do this, nor did it enter My heart that they should commit this abomination and lead Judah into sin.

39:36  LA: Et nunc propter ista, hæc dicit Dominus Deus Israël ad civitatem hanc, de qua vos dicitis quod tradetur in manus regis Babylonis, in gladio, et in fame, et in peste :
       EN: Now, therefore, thus says the Lord, the God of Israel, concerning this city, of which you say that it will be delivered into the hands of the king of Babylon by sword, famine, and pestilence:

39:37  LA: Ecce ego congregabo eos de universis terris ad quas ejeci eos in furore meo, et in ira mea, et in indignatione grandi : et reducam eos ad locum istum, et habitare eos faciam confidenter :
       EN: Behold, I will gather them from all the lands to which I drove them in My fury, anger, and great indignation. I will bring them back to this place and make them dwell securely.

39:38  LA: et erunt mihi in populum, et ego ero eis in Deum.
       EN: They will be My people, and I will be their God.

39:39  LA: Et dabo eis cor unum, et viam unam, ut timeant me universis diebus, et bene sit eis, et filiis eorum post eos.
       EN: I will give them one heart and one way, so that they may fear Me always, for their own good and the good of their children after them.

39:40  LA: Et feriam eis pactum sempiternum, et non desinam eis benefacere : et timorem meum dabo in corde eorum, ut non recedant a me.
       EN: I will make an everlasting covenant with them and will not cease to do them good. I will put fear of Me in their heart, so that they will not depart from Me.

39:41  LA: Et lætabor super eis, cum bene eis fecero : et plantabo eos in terra ista in veritate, in toto corde meo et in tota anima mea.
       EN: I will rejoice over them when I do them good, and I will plant them in this land faithfully, with all My heart and all My soul.

39:42  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus : Sicut adduxi super populum istum omne malum hoc grande, sic adducam super eos omne bonum quod ego loquor ad eos.
       EN: For thus says the Lord: As I have brought all this great calamity upon this people, so I will bring upon them all the good that I am promising them.

39:43  LA: Et possidebuntur agri in terra ista, de qua vos dicitis quod deserta sit, eo quod non remanserit homo et jumentum, et data sit in manus Chaldæorum.
       EN: Fields will be possessed in this land of which you say, ‘It is desolate, with neither human nor animal left, and has been given into the hands of the Chaldeans.’

39:44  LA: Agri ementur pecunia, et scribentur in libro, et imprimetur signum, et testis adhibebitur, in terra Benjamin et in circuitu Jerusalem, in civitatibus Juda, et in civitatibus montanis, et in civitatibus campestribus, et in civitatibus quæ ad austrum sunt, quia convertam captivitatem eorum, ait Dominus.]
       EN: Fields will be bought with money, deeds written, a seal impressed, and a witness summoned, in the land of Benjamin and around Jerusalem, in the cities of Judah, in the cities of the hill country, in the cities of the plain, and in the cities to the south. For I will restore their captives, says the Lord.”

JEREMIAH 40

40:1  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad Jeremiam secundo, cum adhuc clausus esset in atrio carceris, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to Jeremiah a second time while he was still confined in the court of the prison:

40:2  LA: [Hæc dicit Dominus, qui facturus est, et formaturus illud, et paraturus : Dominus nomen ejus :
      EN: “Thus says the Lord, who will do this, form it, and establish it; the Lord is His name:

40:3  LA: Clama ad me, et exaudiam te, et annuntiabo tibi grandia et firma quæ nescis.
      EN: Call to Me, and I will answer you and tell you great and mighty things you do not know.

40:4  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus Deus Israël ad domos urbis hujus, et ad domos regis Juda, quæ destructæ sunt, et ad munitiones, et ad gladium
      EN: For thus says the Lord, the God of Israel, concerning the houses of this city and the houses of the king of Judah that have been torn down, and concerning the siegeworks and the sword

40:5  LA: venientium ut dimicent cum Chaldæis, et impleant eas cadaveribus hominum quos percussi in furore meo et in indignatione mea, abscondens faciem meam a civitate hac, propter omnem malitiam eorum :
      EN: of those who come to fight the Chaldeans and fill them with the corpses of people whom I struck in My fury and indignation, hiding My face from this city because of all their wickedness:

40:6  LA: Ecce ego obducam eis cicatricem et sanitatem, et curabo eos, et revelabo illis deprecationem pacis et veritatis.
      EN: Behold, I will close their wounds and bring health; I will heal them and reveal to them an appeal for peace and truth.

40:7  LA: Et convertam conversionem Juda et conversionem Jerusalem, et ædificabo eos sicut a principio.
      EN: I will restore the fortunes of Judah and the fortunes of Jerusalem and rebuild them as at the beginning.

40:8  LA: Et emundabo illos ab omni iniquitate sua in qua peccaverunt mihi, et propitius ero cunctis iniquitatibus eorum, in quibus dereliquerunt mihi et spreverunt me.
      EN: I will cleanse them of all the iniquity by which they sinned against Me, and forgive all their iniquities by which they abandoned Me and despised Me.

40:9  LA: Et erit mihi in nomen, et in gaudium, et in laudem, et in exsultationem cunctis gentibus terræ, quæ audierint omnia bona quæ ego facturus sum eis : et pavebunt et turbabuntur in universis bonis, et in omni pace quam ego faciam eis.
      EN: It will become for Me a name, a joy, a praise, and an exultation before all the nations of the earth who hear all the good I will do for them. They will stand in awe and be moved by all the goodness and peace I give them.

40:10  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Adhuc audietur in loco isto quem vos dicitis esse desertum, eo quod non sit homo nec jumentum in civitatibus Juda, et foris Jerusalem, quæ desolatæ sunt, absque homine, et absque habitatore, et absque pecore,
       EN: Thus says the Lord: Again there will be heard in this place, which you call desolate because neither human nor animal remains, in the cities of Judah and the streets of Jerusalem, deserted, without human being, inhabitant, or livestock,

40:11  LA: vox gaudii et vox lætitiæ, vox sponsi et vox sponsæ, vox dicentium : Confitemini Domino exercituum, quoniam bonus Dominus, quoniam in æternum misericordia ejus : et portantium vota in domum Domini : reducam enim conversionem terræ sicut a principio, dicit Dominus.
       EN: the voice of joy and the voice of gladness, the voice of the bridegroom and the voice of the bride, the voice of those who say, ‘Give thanks to the Lord of hosts, for the Lord is good, for His mercy endures forever,’ and of those who bring vowed offerings to the house of the Lord. For I will restore the fortunes of the land as at the beginning, says the Lord.

40:12  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum : Adhuc erit in loco isto deserto, absque homine et absque jumento, et in cunctis civitatibus ejus, habitaculum pastorum accubantium gregum.
       EN: Thus says the Lord of hosts: Again in this desolate place, without human or animal, and in all its cities there will be a dwelling for shepherds whose flocks lie at rest.

40:13  LA: In civitatibus montuosis, et in civitatibus campestribus, et in civitatibus quæ ad austrum sunt, et in terra Benjamin, et in circuitu Jerusalem, et in civitatibus Juda, adhuc transibunt greges ad manum numerantis, ait Dominus.]
       EN: In the cities of the mountains, in the cities of the plain, in the cities to the south, in the land of Benjamin, around Jerusalem, and in the cities of Judah, flocks will again pass under the hand of one who counts them, says the Lord.

JEREMIAH 41

41:1  LA: Verbum quod factum est ad Jeremiam a Domino quando Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis, et omnis exercitus ejus, universaque regna terræ quæ erant sub potestate manus ejus, et omnes populi, bellabant contra Jerusalem, et contra omnes urbes ejus, dicens :
      EN: The word that came to Jeremiah from the Lord when Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon, his whole army, all the kingdoms of the earth under his power, and all the peoples were fighting against Jerusalem and all its cities:

41:2  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus Israël : Vade, et loquere ad Sedeciam regem Juda, et dices ad eum : Hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego tradam civitatem hanc in manus regis Babylonis, et succendet eam igni :
      EN: “Thus says the Lord, the God of Israel: Go and speak to Zedekiah king of Judah. Tell him, ‘Thus says the Lord: Behold, I will deliver this city into the hands of the king of Babylon, and he will burn it with fire.

41:3  LA: et tu non effugies de manu ejus, sed comprehensione capieris, et in manu ejus traderis : et oculi tui oculos regis Babylonis videbunt, et os ejus cum ore tuo loquetur, et Babylonem introibis.
      EN: You will not escape from his hand, but will certainly be captured and delivered into his hand. Your eyes will see the eyes of the king of Babylon, and he will speak with you mouth to mouth. You will go to Babylon.

41:4  LA: Attamen audi verbum Domini, Sedecia, rex Juda : Hæc dicit Dominus ad te : Non morieris in gladio,
      EN: Yet hear the word of the Lord, Zedekiah king of Judah. Thus says the Lord concerning you: You will not die by the sword,

41:5  LA: sed in pace morieris : et secundum combustiones patrum tuorum, regum priorum qui fuerunt ante te, sic comburent te : et : Væ domine, plangent te : quia verbum ego locutus sum, dicit Dominus.
      EN: but will die in peace. As at the funeral burnings of your fathers, the earlier kings who preceded you, so they will burn for you and lament you, saying, “Alas, my lord!” For I have spoken the word, says the Lord.’”

41:6  LA: Et locutus est Jeremias propheta ad Sedeciam regem Juda universa verba hæc in Jerusalem.
      EN: Jeremiah the prophet spoke all these words to Zedekiah king of Judah in Jerusalem.

41:7  LA: Et exercitus regis Babylonis pugnabat contra Jerusalem, et contra omnes civitates Juda quæ reliquæ erant, contra Lachis et contra Azecha : hæ enim supererant de civitatibus Juda, urbes munitæ.
      EN: The army of the king of Babylon was fighting against Jerusalem and all the cities of Judah that remained, against Lachish and Azekah, for these were the fortified cities that remained among the cities of Judah.

41:8  LA: Verbum quod factum est ad Jeremiam a Domino, postquam percussit rex Sedecias fœdus cum omni populo in Jerusalem, prædicans
      EN: The word that came to Jeremiah from the Lord after King Zedekiah had made a covenant with all the people in Jerusalem, proclaiming

41:9  LA: ut dimitteret unusquisque servum suum et unusquisque ancillam suam, Hebræum et Hebræam, liberos, et nequaquam dominarentur eis, id est, in Judæo et fratre suo.
      EN: that everyone should release his male and female slaves, Hebrew men and Hebrew women, and no longer keep them in bondage, that is, his fellow Jew and brother.

41:10  LA: Audierunt ergo omnes principes et universus populus qui inierant pactum ut dimitteret unusquisque servum suum et unusquisque ancillam suam liberos, et ultra non dominarentur eis : audierunt igitur, et dimiserunt.
