       EN: All the rulers and all the people who had entered the covenant listened and agreed to release their male and female slaves and no longer keep them in bondage. They obeyed and released them.

41:11  LA: Et conversi sunt deinceps : et retraxerunt servos et ancillas suas quos dimiserant liberos, et subjugaverunt in famulos et famulas.
       EN: But afterward they changed their minds, took back the male and female slaves they had freed, and subjected them to slavery again.

41:12  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad Jeremiam a Domino, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to Jeremiah from the Lord:

41:13  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus Israël : Ego percussi fœdus cum patribus vestris in die qua eduxi eos de terra Ægypti, de domo servitutis, dicens :
       EN: “Thus says the Lord, the God of Israel: I made a covenant with your fathers on the day I brought them out of the land of Egypt, out of the house of slavery, saying,

41:14  LA: Cum completi fuerint septem anni, dimittat unusquisque fratrem suum Hebræum, qui venditus est ei : et serviet tibi sex annis, et dimittes eum a te liberum : et non audierunt patres vestri me, nec inclinaverunt aurem suam.
       EN: ‘When seven years are completed, each of you shall release his Hebrew brother who has been sold to him. He shall serve you six years, and you shall send him away free.’ But your fathers did not listen to Me or incline their ear.

41:15  LA: Et conversi estis vos hodie, et fecistis quod rectum est in oculis meis, ut prædicaretis libertatem unusquisque ad amicum suum : et inistis pactum in conspectu meo, in domo in qua invocatum est nomen meum super eam :
       EN: Today you turned back and did what was right in My sight, each proclaiming freedom to his neighbor. You entered a covenant before Me in the house over which My name is invoked.

41:16  LA: et reversi estis, et commaculastis nomen meum, et reduxistis unusquisque servum suum et unusquisque ancillam suam quos dimiseratis ut essent liberi et suæ potestatis, et subjugastis eos ut sint vobis servi et ancillæ.
       EN: But you changed your minds and profaned My name. Each of you took back the male and female slaves whom you had released to be free and their own masters, and subjected them to be your slaves again.

41:17  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus : Vos non audistis me, ut prædicaretis libertatem unusquisque fratri suo et unusquisque amico suo : ecce ego prædico vobis libertatem, ait Dominus, ad gladium, ad pestem, et ad famem, et dabo vos in commotionem cunctis regnis terræ.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord: You have not obeyed Me by proclaiming freedom, each to his brother and each to his neighbor. Behold, I proclaim freedom for you, says the Lord, to sword, pestilence, and famine. I will make you an object of dismay to all the kingdoms of the earth.

41:18  LA: Et dabo viros qui prævaricantur fœdus meum, et non observaverunt verba fœderis quibus assensi sunt in conspectu meo, vitulum quem conciderunt in duas partes, et transierunt inter divisiones ejus,
       EN: I will give over the men who transgress My covenant and have not kept the words of the covenant to which they agreed before Me, when they cut the calf in two and passed between its halves:

41:19  LA: principes Juda et principes Jerusalem, eunuchi et sacerdotes, et omnis populus terræ, qui transierunt inter divisiones vituli :
       EN: the rulers of Judah and the rulers of Jerusalem, the eunuchs, the priests, and all the people of the land who passed between the halves of the calf.

41:20  LA: et dabo eos in manus inimicorum suorum, et in manus quærentium animam eorum, et erit morticinum eorum in escam volatilibus cæli et bestiis terræ.
       EN: I will give them into the hands of their enemies and into the hands of those who seek their lives. Their corpses will become food for the birds of the sky and the beasts of the earth.

41:21  LA: Et Sedeciam regem Juda, et principes ejus, dabo in manus inimicorum suorum, et in manus quærentium animas eorum, et in manus exercituum regis Babylonis, qui recesserunt a vobis.
       EN: I will give Zedekiah king of Judah and his rulers into the hands of their enemies, into the hands of those who seek their lives, and into the hands of the armies of the king of Babylon that have withdrawn from you.

41:22  LA: Ecce ego præcipio, dicit Dominus, et reducam eos in civitatem hanc, et præliabuntur adversus eam, et capient eam, et incendent igni : et civitates Juda dabo in solitudinem, eo quod non sit habitator.
       EN: Behold, I will command, says the Lord, and bring them back to this city. They will fight against it, capture it, and burn it with fire. I will make the cities of Judah a wilderness without an inhabitant.”

JEREMIAH 42

42:1  LA: Verbum quod factum est ad Jeremiam a Domino in diebus Joakim filii Josiæ regis Juda, dicens :
      EN: The word that came to Jeremiah from the Lord in the days of Jehoiakim son of Josiah, king of Judah:

42:2  LA: Vade ad domum Rechabitarum, et loquere eis, et introduces eos in domum Domini, in unam exedram thesaurorum, et dabis eis bibere vinum.
      EN: “Go to the house of the Rechabites and speak with them. Bring them into the house of the Lord, into one of the treasury chambers, and give them wine to drink.”

42:3  LA: Et assumpsi Jezoniam filium Jeremiæ filii Habsaniæ, et fratres ejus, et omnes filios ejus, et universam domum Rechabitarum,
      EN: I took Jaazaniah son of Jeremiah, son of Habazziniah, his brothers, all his sons, and the whole house of the Rechabites,

42:4  LA: et introduxi eos in domum Domini, ad gazophylacium filiorum Hanan filii Jegedeliæ hominis Dei, quod erat juxta gazophylacium principum, super thesaurum Maasiæ filii Sellum, qui erat custos vestibuli :
      EN: and brought them into the house of the Lord, into the chamber of the sons of Hanan son of Igdaliah, the man of God. It was beside the chamber of the rulers, above the storeroom of Maaseiah son of Shallum, the keeper of the entrance.

42:5  LA: et posui coram filiis domus Rechabitarum scyphos plenos vino, et calices, et dixi ad eos : Bibite vinum.
      EN: I set before the members of the house of the Rechabites bowls full of wine and cups, and said to them, “Drink wine.”

42:6  LA: Qui responderunt Non bibemus vinum, quia Jonadab filius Rechab, pater noster, præcepit nobis, dicens : Non bibetis vinum, vos et filii vestri, usque in sempiternum :
      EN: They answered, “We will not drink wine, because Jonadab son of Rechab, our father, commanded us, ‘You and your children shall never drink wine.

42:7  LA: et domum non ædificabitis, et sementem non seretis, et vineas non plantabitis, nec habebitis : sed in tabernaculis habitabitis cunctis diebus vestris, ut vivatis diebus multis super faciem terræ in qua vos peregrinamini.
      EN: You shall not build a house, sow seed, plant vineyards, or possess them. Instead, you shall dwell in tents all your days, so that you may live many days upon the land where you sojourn.’

42:8  LA: Obedivimus ergo voci Jonadab filii Rechab, patris nostri, in omnibus quæ præcepit nobis, ita ut non biberemus vinum cunctis diebus nostris, nos, et mulieres nostræ, filii, et filiæ nostræ,
      EN: We have obeyed the voice of Jonadab son of Rechab, our father, in everything he commanded us. We have drunk no wine all our days, neither we nor our wives, our sons, or our daughters.

42:9  LA: et non ædificaremus domos ad habitandum : et vineam, et agrum, et sementem non habuimus :
      EN: We have built no houses to dwell in and have possessed no vineyard, field, or sown crop.

42:10  LA: sed habitavimus in tabernaculis, et obedientes fuimus juxta omnia quæ præcepit nobis Jonadab pater noster.
       EN: We have dwelt in tents and obeyed everything Jonadab our father commanded us.

42:11  LA: Cum autem ascendisset Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis ad terram nostram, diximus : Venite, et ingrediamur Jerusalem a facie exercitus Chaldæorum, et a facie exercitus Syriæ : et mansimus in Jerusalem.
       EN: But when Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon came up against our land, we said, ‘Come, let us enter Jerusalem to escape the army of the Chaldeans and the army of Syria.’ So we have remained in Jerusalem.”

42:12  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad Jeremiam, dicens :
       EN: The word of the Lord came to Jeremiah:

42:13  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Vade, et dic viris Juda et habitatoribus Jerusalem : Numquid non recipietis disciplinam, ut obediatis verbis meis ? dicit Dominus.
       EN: “Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Go and tell the men of Judah and the inhabitants of Jerusalem, ‘Will you not receive instruction and obey My words?’ says the Lord.

42:14  LA: Prævaluerunt sermones Jonadab filii Rechab quos præcepit filiis suis ut non biberent vinum, et non biberunt usque ad diem hanc, quia obedierunt præcepto patris sui : ego autem locutus sum ad vos, de mane consurgens et loquens, et non obedistis mihi.
       EN: The words of Jonadab son of Rechab have held firm. He commanded his children not to drink wine, and they have not drunk it to this day, because they obeyed their father’s command. But I have spoken to you, rising early and speaking, and you have not obeyed Me.

42:15  LA: Misique ad vos omnes servos meos prophetas, consurgens diluculo mittensque, et dicens : Convertimini unusquisque a via sua pessima, et bona facite studia vestra : et nolite sequi deos alienos, neque colatis eos, et habitabitis in terra quam dedi vobis et patribus vestris : et non inclinastis aurem vestram, neque audistis me.
       EN: I have sent you all My servants the prophets, rising at dawn and sending them, saying, ‘Turn back, each of you, from his evil way and make your conduct good. Do not follow foreign gods or worship them, and you will dwell in the land I gave you and your fathers.’ But you did not incline your ear or listen to Me.

42:16  LA: Firmaverunt igitur filii Jonadab filii Rechab præceptum patris sui quod præceperat eis : populus autem iste non obedivit mihi.
       EN: The children of Jonadab son of Rechab have upheld the command their father gave them, but this people has not obeyed Me.

42:17  LA: Idcirco hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Ecce ego adducam super Juda et super omnes habitatores Jerusalem universam afflictionem quam locutus sum adversum illos, eo quod locutus sum ad illos, et non audierunt ; vocavi illos, et non responderunt mihi.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Behold, I will bring upon Judah and all the inhabitants of Jerusalem all the affliction I have spoken against them, because I spoke to them and they did not listen; I called them and they did not answer Me.”

42:18  LA: Domui autem Rechabitarum dixit Jeremias : Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Pro eo quod obedistis præcepto Jonadab patris vestri, et custodistis omnia mandata ejus, et fecistis universa quæ præcepit vobis,
       EN: Jeremiah said to the house of the Rechabites, “Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Because you have obeyed the command of Jonadab your father, kept all his commandments, and done everything he commanded you,

42:19  LA: propterea hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Non deficiet vir de stirpe Jonadab filii Rechab, stans in conspectu meo cunctis diebus.
       EN: therefore thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Jonadab son of Rechab will never lack a man of his line to stand before Me always.”

JEREMIAH 43

43:1  LA: Et factum est in anno quarto Joakim filii Josiæ regis Juda, factum est verbum hoc ad Jeremiam a Domino, dicens :
      EN: In the fourth year of Jehoiakim son of Josiah, king of Judah, this word came to Jeremiah from the Lord:

43:2  LA: Tolle volumen libri, et scribes in eo omnia verba quæ locutus sum tibi adversum Israël et Judam, et adversum omnes gentes, a die qua locutus sum ad te ex diebus Josiæ usque ad diem hanc :
      EN: “Take a book scroll and write on it all the words I have spoken to you against Israel and Judah and against all the nations, from the day I first spoke to you in the days of Josiah until today.

43:3  LA: si forte, audiente domo Juda universa mala quæ ego cogito facere eis, revertatur unusquisque a via sua pessima, et propitius ero iniquitati et peccato eorum.
      EN: Perhaps when the house of Judah hears all the calamities I intend to bring upon them, each will turn back from his evil way, and I will forgive their iniquity and sin.”

43:4  LA: Vocavit ergo Jeremias Baruch filium Neriæ : et scripsit Baruch ex ore Jeremiæ omnes sermones Domini quos locutus est ad eum, in volumine libri :
      EN: Jeremiah summoned Baruch son of Neriah. At Jeremiah’s dictation Baruch wrote on a book scroll all the words the Lord had spoken to him.

43:5  LA: et præcepit Jeremias Baruch, dicens : Ego clausus sum, nec valeo ingredi domum Domini.
      EN: Jeremiah commanded Baruch, “I am confined and cannot enter the house of the Lord.

43:6  LA: Ingredere ergo tu, et lege de volumine in quo scripsisti ex ore meo verba Domini, audiente populo in domo Domini, in die jejunii : insuper et audiente universo Juda qui veniunt de civitatibus suis, leges eis,
      EN: So you must go and read from the scroll on which you have written the words of the Lord at my dictation, in the hearing of the people in the house of the Lord on the day of fasting. You must also read them in the hearing of all Judah who come from their cities.

43:7  LA: si forte cadat oratio eorum in conspectu Domini, et revertatur unusquisque a via sua pessima : quoniam magnus furor et indignatio est quam locutus est Dominus adversus populum hunc.
      EN: Perhaps their prayer will fall before the Lord and each will turn back from his evil way, for great is the fury and indignation the Lord has pronounced against this people.”

43:8  LA: Et fecit Baruch filius Neriæ juxta omnia quæ præceperat ei Jeremias propheta, legens ex volumine sermones Domini in domo Domini.
      EN: Baruch son of Neriah did everything Jeremiah the prophet commanded him, reading the words of the Lord from the scroll in the house of the Lord.

43:9  LA: Factum est autem in anno quinto Joakim filii Josiæ regis Juda, in mense nono : prædicaverunt jejunium in conspectu Domini omni populo in Jerusalem, et universæ multitudini quæ confluxerat de civitatibus Juda in Jerusalem.
      EN: In the fifth year of Jehoiakim son of Josiah, king of Judah, in the ninth month, a fast before the Lord was proclaimed for all the people in Jerusalem and for the whole multitude that had gathered in Jerusalem from the cities of Judah.

43:10  LA: Legitque Baruch ex volumine sermones Jeremiæ in domo Domini, in gazophylacio Gamariæ filii Saphan scribæ, in vestibulo superiori, in introitu portæ novæ domus Domini, audiente omni populo.
       EN: Baruch read Jeremiah’s words from the scroll in the house of the Lord, in the chamber of Gemariah son of Shaphan the scribe, in the upper court, at the entrance of the New Gate of the house of the Lord, in the hearing of all the people.

43:11  LA: Cumque audisset Michæas filius Gamariæ filii Saphan omnes sermones Domini ex libro,
       EN: When Micaiah son of Gemariah, son of Shaphan, heard all the words of the Lord from the book,

43:12  LA: descendit in domum regis, ad gazophylacium scribæ, et ecce ibi omnes principes sedebant : Elisama scriba, et Dalaias filius Semeiæ, et Elnathan filius Achobor, et Gamarias filius Saphan, et Sedecias filius Hananiæ, et universi principes :
       EN: he went down to the king’s palace, to the chamber of the scribe. There all the rulers were sitting: Elishama the scribe, Delaiah son of Shemaiah, Elnathan son of Achbor, Gemariah son of Shaphan, Zedekiah son of Hananiah, and all the rulers.

43:13  LA: et nuntiavit eis Michæas omnia verba quæ audivit, legente Baruch ex volumine in auribus populi.
       EN: Micaiah told them all the words he had heard when Baruch read from the scroll in the people’s hearing.

43:14  LA: Miserunt itaque omnes principes ad Baruch Judi filium Nathaniæ filii Selemiæ filii Chusi, dicentes : Volumen ex quo legisti, audiente populo, sume in manu tua, et veni. Tulit ergo Baruch filius Neriæ volumen in manu sua, et venit ad eos :
       EN: Then all the rulers sent Jehudi son of Nethaniah, son of Shelemiah, son of Cushi, to Baruch with this message: “Take in your hand the scroll from which you read in the people’s hearing, and come.” Baruch son of Neriah took the scroll in his hand and came to them.

43:15  LA: et dixerunt ad eum : Sede, et lege hæc in auribus nostris. Et legit Baruch in auribus eorum.
       EN: They said to him, “Sit down and read these things in our hearing.” Baruch read in their hearing.

43:16  LA: Igitur cum audissent omnia verba, obstupuerunt unusquisque ad proximum suum, et dixerunt ad Baruch : Nuntiare debemus regi omnes sermones istos.
       EN: When they heard all the words, they looked at one another in astonishment and said to Baruch, “We must tell the king all these words.”

43:17  LA: Et interrogaverunt eum, dicentes : Indica nobis quomodo scripsisti omnes sermones istos ex ore ejus.
       EN: They questioned him, saying, “Tell us how you wrote all these words from his mouth.”

43:18  LA: Dixit autem eis Baruch : Ex ore suo loquebatur quasi legens ad me omnes sermones istos, et ego scribebam in volumine atramento.
       EN: Baruch said to them, “He spoke all these words to me from his own mouth as though reading, and I wrote them on the scroll with ink.”

43:19  LA: Et dixerunt principes ad Baruch : Vade, et abscondere, tu et Jeremias, et nemo sciat ubi sitis.
       EN: The rulers said to Baruch, “Go and hide, you and Jeremiah, and let no one know where you are.”

43:20  LA: Et ingressi sunt ad regem in atrium : porro volumen commendaverunt in gazophylacio Elisamæ scribæ, et nuntiaverunt, audiente rege, omnes sermones.
       EN: They went to the king in the court, leaving the scroll in the chamber of Elishama the scribe, and reported all the words in the king’s hearing.

43:21  LA: Misitque rex Judi ut sumeret volumen : qui tollens illud de gazophylacio Elisamæ scribæ, legit, audiente rege et universis principibus qui stabant circa regem.
       EN: The king sent Jehudi to fetch the scroll. He took it from the chamber of Elishama the scribe and read it in the hearing of the king and all the rulers standing around him.

43:22  LA: Rex autem sedebat in domo hiemali, in mense nono, et posita erat arula coram eo plena prunis.
       EN: The king was sitting in the winter house, in the ninth month, with a brazier full of burning coals before him.

43:23  LA: Cumque legisset Judi tres pagellas vel quatuor, scidit illud scalpello scribæ, et projecit in ignem qui erat super arulam, donec consumeretur omne volumen igni qui erat in arula.
       EN: Whenever Jehudi had read three or four columns, the king cut them off with a scribe’s knife and threw them into the fire in the brazier, until the whole scroll was consumed by the fire in the brazier.

43:24  LA: Et non timuerunt, neque sciderunt vestimenta sua, rex et omnes servi ejus qui audierunt universos sermones istos.
       EN: Neither the king nor any of his servants who heard all these words were afraid or tore their garments.

43:25  LA: Verumtamen Elnathan, et Dalaias, et Gamarias, contradixerunt regi, ne combureret librum : et non audivit eos.
       EN: Yet Elnathan, Delaiah, and Gemariah pleaded with the king not to burn the book, but he did not listen to them.

43:26  LA: Et præcepit rex Jeremiel filio Amelech, et Saraiæ filio Ezriel, et Selemiæ filio Abdeel, ut comprehenderent Baruch scribam, et Jeremiam prophetam : abscondit autem eos Dominus.
       EN: The king commanded Jerahmeel son of Hammelech, Seraiah son of Azriel, and Shelemiah son of Abdeel to seize Baruch the scribe and Jeremiah the prophet, but the Lord hid them.

43:27  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad Jeremiam prophetam, postquam combusserat rex volumen et sermones quos scripserat Baruch ex ore Jeremiæ, dicens :
       EN: After the king had burned the scroll containing the words Baruch had written at Jeremiah’s dictation, the word of the Lord came to Jeremiah the prophet:

43:28  LA: Rursum tolle volumen aliud, et scribe in eo omnes sermones priores qui erant in primo volumine, quod combussit Joakim rex Juda.
       EN: “Take another scroll and write on it all the former words that were on the first scroll, which Jehoiakim king of Judah burned.

43:29  LA: Et ad Joakim regem Juda dices : Hæc dicit Dominus : Tu combussisti volumen illud, dicens : Quare scripsisti in eo annuntians : Festinus veniet rex Babylonis, et vastabit terram hanc, et cessare faciet ex illa hominem et jumentum ?
       EN: You shall say to Jehoiakim king of Judah, ‘Thus says the Lord: You burned that scroll, saying, “Why have you written on it this announcement: The king of Babylon will come swiftly, lay this land waste, and make human and animal cease from it?”

43:30  LA: Propterea hæc dicit Dominus contra Joakim regem Juda : Non erit ex eo qui sedeat super solium David : et cadaver ejus projicietur ad æstum per diem, et ad gelu per noctem.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord against Jehoiakim king of Judah: He will have no one to sit upon David’s throne. His corpse will be cast out to the heat by day and to the frost by night.

43:31  LA: Et visitabo contra eum, et contra semen ejus, et contra servos ejus, iniquitates suas : et adducam super eos, et super habitatores Jerusalem, et super viros Juda, omne malum quod locutus sum ad eos, et non audierunt.
       EN: I will call him, his descendants, and his servants to account for their iniquities. I will bring upon them, upon the inhabitants of Jerusalem, and upon the men of Judah all the calamity I spoke to them, but they did not listen.’”

43:32  LA: Jeremias autem tulit volumen aliud, et dedit illud Baruch filio Neriæ scribæ : qui scripsit in eo ex ore Jeremiæ omnes sermones libri quem combusserat Joakim rex Juda igni : et insuper additi sunt sermones multo plures quam antea fuerant.
       EN: Jeremiah took another scroll and gave it to Baruch son of Neriah, the scribe, who wrote on it at Jeremiah’s dictation all the words of the book Jehoiakim king of Judah had burned in the fire. Many more words than before were added to them.

JEREMIAH 44

44:1  LA: Et regnavit rex Sedecias filius Josiæ pro Jechonia filio Joakim, quem constituit regem Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis in terra Juda :
      EN: Zedekiah son of Josiah reigned as king in place of Jeconiah son of Jehoiakim. Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon had appointed him king in the land of Judah.

44:2  LA: et non obedivit ipse, et servi ejus, et populus terræ, verbis Domini, quæ locutus est in manu Jeremiæ prophetæ.
      EN: Neither he nor his servants nor the people of the land obeyed the words of the Lord spoken through Jeremiah the prophet.

44:3  LA: Et misit rex Sedecias Juchal filium Selemiæ, et Sophoniam filium Maasiæ, sacerdotem, ad Jeremiam prophetam, dicens : Ora pro nobis Dominum Deum nostrum.
      EN: King Zedekiah sent Jucal son of Shelemiah and Zephaniah son of Maaseiah, the priest, to Jeremiah the prophet, saying, “Pray to the Lord our God for us.”

44:4  LA: Jeremias autem libere ambulabat in medio populi : non enim miserant eum in custodiam carceris. Igitur exercitus Pharaonis egressus est de Ægypto, et audientes Chaldæi qui obsidebant Jerusalem, hujuscemodi nuntium, recesserunt ab Jerusalem.
      EN: Jeremiah still moved freely among the people, for they had not put him under guard in prison. Pharaoh’s army had come out of Egypt, and when the Chaldeans besieging Jerusalem heard this report, they withdrew from Jerusalem.

44:5  LA: 
      EN: 

44:6  LA: Et factum est verbum Domini ad Jeremiam prophetam, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to Jeremiah the prophet:

44:7  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus Israël : Sic dicetis regi Juda, qui misit vos ad me interrogandum : Ecce exercitus Pharaonis, qui egressus est vobis in auxilium, revertetur in terram suam in Ægyptum :
      EN: “Thus says the Lord, the God of Israel: Say this to the king of Judah, who sent you to inquire of Me: Behold, Pharaoh’s army, which came out to help you, will return to its own land, to Egypt.

44:8  LA: et redient Chaldæi, et bellabunt contra civitatem hanc, et capient eam, et succendent eam igni.
      EN: The Chaldeans will return, fight against this city, capture it, and burn it with fire.

44:9  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Nolite decipere animas vestras, dicentes : Euntes abibunt, et recedent a nobis Chaldæi : quia non abibunt.
      EN: Thus says the Lord: Do not deceive yourselves by saying, ‘The Chaldeans will surely go away and withdraw from us,’ for they will not go away.

44:10  LA: Sed etsi percusseritis omnem exercitum Chaldæorum qui præliantur adversum vos, et derelicti fuerint ex eis aliqui vulnerati, singuli de tentorio suo consurgent, et incendent civitatem hanc igni.
       EN: Even if you struck down the whole Chaldean army fighting against you, and only some wounded men remained, each would rise from his tent and burn this city with fire.”

44:11  LA: Ergo cum recessisset exercitus Chaldæorum ab Jerusalem, propter exercitum Pharaonis,
       EN: When the Chaldean army had withdrawn from Jerusalem because of Pharaoh’s army,

44:12  LA: egressus est Jeremias de Jerusalem ut iret in terram Benjamin, et divideret ibi possessionem in conspectu civium.
       EN: Jeremiah went out of Jerusalem to go into the land of Benjamin and divide property there in the presence of the townspeople.

44:13  LA: Cumque pervenisset ad portam Benjamin, erat ibi custos portæ per vices, nomine Jerias filius Selemiæ filii Hananiæ : et apprehendit Jeremiam prophetam, dicens : Ad Chaldæos profugis.
       EN: When he reached the Benjamin Gate, the gatekeeper on duty there was named Irijah son of Shelemiah, son of Hananiah. He seized Jeremiah the prophet, saying, “You are deserting to the Chaldeans!”

44:14  LA: Et respondit Jeremias : Falsum est : non fugio ad Chaldæos. Et non audivit eum, sed comprehendit Jerias Jeremiam, et adduxit eum ad principes :
       EN: Jeremiah answered, “That is false! I am not deserting to the Chaldeans.” But he did not listen to him. Irijah seized Jeremiah and brought him to the rulers.

44:15  LA: quam ob rem irati principes contra Jeremiam, cæsum eum miserunt in carcerem qui erat in domo Jonathan scribæ : ipse enim præpositus erat super carcerem.
       EN: The rulers grew angry with Jeremiah, beat him, and put him in the prison in the house of Jonathan the scribe, for Jonathan was in charge of the prison.

44:16  LA: Itaque ingressus est Jeremias in domum laci et in ergastulum : et sedit ibi Jeremias diebus multis.
       EN: Jeremiah entered the dungeon and the cell and remained there many days.

44:17  LA: Mittens autem Sedecias rex, tulit eum : et interrogavit eum in domo sua abscondite, et dixit : Putasne est sermo a Domino ? Et dixit Jeremias : Est : et ait : In manus regis Babylonis traderis.
       EN: King Zedekiah sent for him and had him brought in. He questioned him secretly in his house, asking, “Is there a word from the Lord?” Jeremiah said, “There is,” and added, “You will be delivered into the hands of the king of Babylon.”

44:18  LA: Et dixit Jeremias ad regem Sedeciam : Quid peccavi tibi, et servis tuis, et populo tuo, quia misisti me in domum carceris ?
       EN: Jeremiah said to King Zedekiah, “What offense have I committed against you, your servants, or your people, that you have put me in prison?

44:19  LA: ubi sunt prophetæ vestri, qui prophetabant vobis, et dicebant : Non veniet rex Babylonis super vos, et super terram hanc ?
       EN: Where are your prophets who prophesied to you, saying, ‘The king of Babylon will not come against you or against this land’?

44:20  LA: Nunc ergo audi, obsecro, domine mi rex : valeat deprecatio mea in conspectu tuo, et ne me remittas in domum Jonathan scribæ, ne moriar ibi.
       EN: Now please listen, my lord the king. Let my plea find favor before you, and do not send me back to the house of Jonathan the scribe, or I will die there.”

44:21  LA: Præcepit ergo rex Sedecias ut traderetur Jeremias in vestibulo carceris, et daretur ei torta panis quotidie, excepto pulmento, donec consumerentur omnes panes de civitate : et mansit Jeremias in vestibulo carceris.
       EN: King Zedekiah commanded that Jeremiah be placed in the entrance court of the prison and given a loaf of bread every day, besides his food, until all the bread in the city was gone. So Jeremiah remained in the entrance court of the prison.

JEREMIAH 45

45:1  LA: Audivit autem Saphatias filius Mathan, et Gedelias filius Phassur, et Juchal filius Selemiæ, et Phassur filius Melchiæ, sermones quos Jeremias loquebatur ad omnem populum, dicens :
      EN: Shephatiah son of Mattan, Gedaliah son of Pashhur, Jucal son of Shelemiah, and Pashhur son of Malchiah heard the words Jeremiah was speaking to all the people:

45:2  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Quicumque manserit in civitate hac, morietur gladio, et fame, et peste : qui autem profugerit ad Chaldæos, vivet, et erit anima ejus sospes et vivens.
      EN: “Thus says the Lord: Whoever remains in this city will die by sword, famine, and pestilence. But whoever goes over to the Chaldeans will live; his life will be safe, and he will remain alive.

45:3  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Tradendo tradetur civitas hæc in manu exercitus regis Babylonis, et capiet eam.
      EN: Thus says the Lord: This city will certainly be delivered into the hand of the army of the king of Babylon, and he will capture it.”

45:4  LA: Et dixerunt principes regi : Rogamus ut occidatur homo iste : de industria enim dissolvit manus virorum bellantium qui remanserunt in civitate hac, et manus universi populi, loquens ad eos juxta verba hæc : siquidem homo iste non quærit pacem populo huic, sed malum.
      EN: The rulers said to the king, “We ask that this man be put to death. He is deliberately weakening the hands of the fighting men who remain in this city and the hands of all the people by speaking such words to them. This man seeks harm for this people, not their welfare.”

45:5  LA: Et dixit rex Sedecias : Ecce ipse in manibus vestris est : nec enim fas est regem vobis quidquam negare.
      EN: King Zedekiah said, “Behold, he is in your hands, for the king may not deny you anything.”

45:6  LA: Tulerunt ergo Jeremiam, et projecerunt eum in lacum Melchiæ filii Amelech, qui erat in vestibulo carceris : et submiserunt Jeremiam funibus in lacum, in quo non erat aqua, sed lutum : descendit itaque Jeremias in cœnum.
      EN: They took Jeremiah and threw him into the cistern of Malchiah son of Hammelech, in the entrance court of the prison. They lowered Jeremiah by ropes into the cistern, which had no water in it, only mud, and Jeremiah sank into the mire.

45:7  LA: Audivit autem Abdemelech Æthiops, vir eunuchus, qui erat in domo regis, quod misissent Jeremiam in lacum. Porro rex sedebat in porta Benjamin :
      EN: Ebed-melech the Ethiopian, a eunuch in the king’s household, heard that they had put Jeremiah into the cistern. The king was sitting at the Benjamin Gate.

45:8  LA: et egressus est Abdemelech de domo regis, et locutus est ad regem, dicens :
      EN: Ebed-melech went out of the king’s palace and spoke to the king:

45:9  LA: Domine mi rex, male fecerunt viri isti omnia quæcumque perpetrarunt contra Jeremiam prophetam, mittentes eum in lacum, ut moriatur ibi fame : non sunt enim panes ultra in civitate.
      EN: “My lord the king, these men have done evil in everything they have done to Jeremiah the prophet, throwing him into the cistern to die there of hunger, for there is no more bread in the city.”

45:10  LA: Præcepit itaque rex Abdemelech Æthiopi, dicens : Tolle tecum hinc triginta viros, et leva Jeremiam prophetam de lacu, antequam moriatur.
       EN: The king commanded Ebed-melech the Ethiopian, “Take thirty men from here with you and lift Jeremiah the prophet out of the cistern before he dies.”

45:11  LA: Assumptis ergo Abdemelech secum viris, ingressus est domum regis, quæ erat sub cellario, et tulit inde veteres pannos, et antiqua quæ computruerant, et submisit ea ad Jeremiam in lacum per funiculos.
       EN: Ebed-melech took the men with him and entered the part of the king’s palace below the storeroom. He took old rags and worn, rotting cloths from there and lowered them by ropes to Jeremiah in the cistern.

45:12  LA: Dixitque Abdemelech Æthiops ad Jeremiam : Pone veteres pannos, et hæc scissa et putrida, sub cubito manuum tuarum, et super funes. Fecit ergo Jeremias sic,
       EN: Ebed-melech the Ethiopian said to Jeremiah, “Put these old rags and these torn, rotting cloths under the bends of your arms, over the ropes.” Jeremiah did so.

45:13  LA: et extraxerunt Jeremiam funibus, et eduxerunt eum de lacu : mansit autem Jeremias in vestibulo carceris.
       EN: They pulled Jeremiah up with the ropes and brought him out of the cistern. Jeremiah remained in the entrance court of the prison.

45:14  LA: Et misit rex Sedecias, et tulit ad se Jeremiam prophetam ad ostium tertium quod erat in domo Domini : et dixit rex ad Jeremiam : Interrogo ego te sermonem, ne abscondas a me aliquid.
       EN: King Zedekiah sent for Jeremiah the prophet and brought him to the third entrance of the house of the Lord. The king said to Jeremiah, “I am asking you about a matter; hide nothing from me.”

45:15  LA: Dixit autem Jeremias ad Sedeciam : Si annuntiavero tibi, numquid non interficies me ? et si consilium dedero tibi, non me audies.
       EN: Jeremiah said to Zedekiah, “If I tell you, will you not put me to death? And if I give you counsel, you will not listen to me.”

45:16  LA: Juravit ergo rex Sedecias Jeremiæ clam, dicens : Vivit Dominus, qui fecit nobis animam hanc, si occidero te, et si tradidero te in manus virorum istorum qui quærunt animam tuam.
       EN: King Zedekiah secretly swore to Jeremiah, “As the Lord lives, who made this life for us, I will neither kill you nor deliver you into the hands of these men who seek your life.”

45:17  LA: Et dixit Jeremias ad Sedeciam : Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Si profectus exieris ad principes regis Babylonis, vivet anima tua, et civitas hæc non succendetur igni : et salvus eris tu, et domus tua.
       EN: Jeremiah said to Zedekiah, “Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: If you go out and surrender to the officers of the king of Babylon, you will live, and this city will not be burned with fire. You and your household will be safe.

45:18  LA: Si autem non exieris ad principes regis Babylonis, tradetur civitas hæc in manus Chaldæorum, et succendent eam igni : et tu non effugies de manu eorum.
       EN: But if you do not go out to the officers of the king of Babylon, this city will be delivered into the hands of the Chaldeans, and they will burn it with fire. You will not escape from their hand.”

45:19  LA: Et dixit rex Sedecias ad Jeremiam : Sollicitus sum propter Judæos qui transfugerunt ad Chaldæos, ne forte tradar in manus eorum, et illudant mihi.
       EN: King Zedekiah said to Jeremiah, “I am worried about the Jews who have deserted to the Chaldeans. I may be delivered into their hands, and they may abuse me.”

45:20  LA: Respondit autem Jeremias : Non te tradent. Audi, quæso, vocem Domini, quam ego loquor ad te, et bene tibi erit, et vivet anima tua.
       EN: Jeremiah answered, “They will not deliver you to them. Please obey the voice of the Lord in what I say to you, and it will go well with you, and you will live.

45:21  LA: Quod si nolueris egredi, iste est sermo quem ostendit mihi Dominus :
       EN: But if you refuse to go out, this is the word the Lord has shown me:

45:22  LA: ecce omnes mulieres quæ remanserunt in domo regis Juda educentur ad principes regis Babylonis, et ipsæ dicent : Seduxerunt te, et prævaluerunt adversum te, viri pacifici tui : demerserunt in cœno et in lubrico pedes tuos, et recesserunt a te.
       EN: Behold, all the women who remain in the house of the king of Judah will be led out to the officers of the king of Babylon, and they will say, ‘Your trusted friends have deceived you and prevailed over you. They have sunk your feet in slippery mire and withdrawn from you.’

45:23  LA: Et omnes uxores tuæ et filii tui educentur ad Chaldæos : et non effugies manus eorum, sed in manu regis Babylonis capieris, et civitatem hanc comburet igni.
       EN: All your wives and children will be led out to the Chaldeans. You will not escape their hands, but will be captured by the king of Babylon, and he will burn this city with fire.”

45:24  LA: Dixit ergo Sedecias ad Jeremiam : Nullus sciat verba hæc, et non morieris.
       EN: Zedekiah said to Jeremiah, “Let no one know these words, and you will not die.

45:25  LA: Si autem audierint principes quia locutus sum tecum, et venerint ad te, et dixerint tibi : Indica nobis quid locutus sis cum rege : ne celes nos, et non te interficiemus : et quid locutus est tecum rex :
       EN: If the rulers hear that I have spoken with you, come to you, and say, ‘Tell us what you said to the king. Do not hide it from us, and we will not kill you. What did the king say to you?’

45:26  LA: dices ad eos : Prostravi ego preces meas coram rege, ne me reduci juberet in domum Jonathan, et ibi morerer.
       EN: you shall tell them, ‘I laid my plea before the king, that he would not order me to be sent back to Jonathan’s house to die there.’”

45:27  LA: Venerunt ergo omnes principes ad Jeremiam, et interrogaverunt eum, et locutus est eis juxta omnia verba quæ præceperat ei rex : et cessaverunt ab eo : nihil enim fuerat auditum.
       EN: All the rulers came to Jeremiah and questioned him. He spoke to them according to all the words the king had commanded him, and they left him alone, for nothing had been overheard.

45:28  LA: Mansit vero Jeremias in vestibulo carceris usque ad diem quo capta est Jerusalem : et factum est ut caperetur Jerusalem.
       EN: Jeremiah remained in the entrance court of the prison until the day Jerusalem was captured. And so Jerusalem was captured.

JEREMIAH 46

46:1  LA: Anno nono Sedeciæ regis Juda, mense decimo, venit Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis, et omnis exercitus ejus, ad Jerusalem, et obsidebant eam.
      EN: In the ninth year of Zedekiah king of Judah, in the tenth month, Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon and his whole army came to Jerusalem and besieged it.

46:2  LA: Undecimo autem anno Sedeciæ, mense quarto, quinta mensis, aperta est civitas :
      EN: In the eleventh year of Zedekiah, in the fourth month, on the fifth day of the month, the city was breached.

46:3  LA: et ingressi sunt omnes principes regis Babylonis, et sederunt in porta media : Neregel, Sereser, Semegarnabu, Sarsachim, Rabsares, Neregel, Sereser, Rebmag, et omnes reliqui principes regis Babylonis.
      EN: All the officers of the king of Babylon entered and sat in the Middle Gate: Nergal, Sharezer, Samgar-nebo, Sarsechim, Rab-saris, Nergal, Sharezer, Rab-mag, and all the other officers of the king of Babylon.

46:14  LA: miserunt, et tulerunt Jeremiam de vestibulo carceris, et tradiderunt eum Godoliæ filio Ahicam filii Saphan, ut intraret in domum, et habitaret in populo.
       EN: They sent and brought Jeremiah out of the entrance court of the prison and handed him over to Gedaliah son of Ahikam, son of Shaphan, so that he might go home and dwell among the people.

46:15  LA: Ad Jeremiam autem factus fuerat sermo Domini, cum clausus esset in vestibulo carceris, dicens :
       EN: While Jeremiah was confined in the entrance court of the prison, the word of the Lord had come to him:

46:16  LA: Vade, et dic Abdemelech Æthiopi, dicens : Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Ecce ego inducam sermones meos super civitatem hanc in malum, et non in bonum, et erunt in conspectu tuo in die illa.
       EN: “Go and tell Ebed-melech the Ethiopian: Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Behold, I will fulfill My words against this city for harm and not for good, and they will come to pass before your eyes on that day.

46:17  LA: Et liberabo te in die illa, ait Dominus, et non traderis in manus virorum quos tu formidas :
       EN: But I will deliver you on that day, says the Lord, and you will not be given into the hands of the men you fear.

46:18  LA: sed eruens liberabo te, et gladio non cades, sed erit tibi anima tua in salutem, quia in me habuisti fiduciam, ait Dominus.
       EN: I will surely rescue and deliver you. You will not fall by the sword, but your life will be saved, because you trusted in Me, says the Lord.”

JEREMIAH 47

47:1  LA: Sermo qui factus est ad Jeremiam a Domino, postquam dimissus est a Nabuzardan magistro militiæ de Rama, quando tulit eum vinctum catenis in medio omnium qui migrabant de Jerusalem et Juda, et ducebantur in Babylonem.
      EN: The word that came to Jeremiah from the Lord after Nebuzaradan, commander of the army, released him from Ramah. He had taken him bound in chains among all those being deported from Jerusalem and Judah and led to Babylon.

47:2  LA: Tollens ergo princeps militiæ Jeremiam, dixit ad eum : Dominus Deus tuus locutus est malum hoc super locum istum :
      EN: The commander of the army took Jeremiah aside and said to him, “The Lord your God pronounced this calamity against this place.

47:3  LA: et adduxit, et fecit Dominus sicut locutus est, quia peccastis Domino, et non audistis vocem ejus : et factus est vobis sermo hic.
      EN: The Lord has brought it about and done as He said, because you sinned against the Lord and did not obey His voice. So this word has come to pass for you.

47:4  LA: Nunc ergo ecce solvi te hodie de catenis quæ sunt in manibus tuis : si placet tibi ut venias mecum in Babylonem, veni, et ponam oculos meos super te : si autem displicet tibi venire mecum in Babylonem, reside : ecce omnis terra in conspectu tuo est : quod elegeris, et quo placuerit tibi ut vadas, illuc perge :
      EN: Now, behold, today I release you from the chains on your hands. If you wish to come with me to Babylon, come, and I will watch over you. But if you do not wish to come with me to Babylon, remain here. Behold, the whole land lies before you. Go wherever you choose and wherever it pleases you to go.

47:5  LA: et mecum noli venire, sed habita apud Godoliam filium Ahicam filii Saphan, quem præposuit rex Babylonis civitatibus Juda : habita ergo cum eo in medio populi : vel quocumque placuerit tibi ut vadas, vade. Dedit quoque ei magister militiæ cibaria et munuscula, et dimisit eum.
      EN: Do not come with me, but dwell with Gedaliah son of Ahikam, son of Shaphan, whom the king of Babylon has appointed over the cities of Judah. Dwell with him among the people, or go wherever it pleases you to go.” The commander of the army gave him provisions and small gifts and released him.

47:6  LA: Venit autem Jeremias ad Godoliam filium Ahicam in Masphath, et habitavit cum eo in medio populi qui relictus fuerat in terra.
      EN: Jeremiah came to Gedaliah son of Ahikam at Mizpah and dwelt with him among the people who had been left in the land.

47:7  LA: Cumque audissent omnes principes exercitus, qui dispersi fuerant per regiones, ipsi et socii eorum, quod præfecisset rex Babylonis Godoliam filium Ahicam terræ, et quod commendasset ei viros, et mulieres, et parvulos, et de pauperibus terræ, qui non fuerant translati in Babylonem,
      EN: All the army commanders who had been scattered through the countryside, together with their companions, heard that the king of Babylon had appointed Gedaliah son of Ahikam over the land and entrusted to him the men, women, little children, and poor of the land who had not been carried away to Babylon.

47:8  LA: venerunt ad Godoliam in Masphath, et Ismahel filius Nathaniæ, et Johanan et Jonathan filii Caree, et Sareas filius Thanehumeth, et filii Ophi, qui erant de Netophathi, et Jezonias filius Maachathi, ipsi et viri eorum.
      EN: They came to Gedaliah at Mizpah: Ishmael son of Nethaniah, Johanan and Jonathan sons of Kareah, Seraiah son of Tanhumeth, the sons of Ephai from Netophah, and Jaazaniah son of the Maacathite, together with their men.

47:9  LA: Et juravit eis Godolias filius Ahicam filii Saphan, et comitibus eorum, dicens : Nolite timere servire Chaldæis : habitate in terra, et servite regi Babylonis, et bene erit vobis.
      EN: Gedaliah son of Ahikam, son of Shaphan, swore to them and their companions, saying, “Do not be afraid to serve the Chaldeans. Dwell in the land and serve the king of Babylon, and it will go well with you.

47:10  LA: Ecce ego habito in Masphath, ut respondeam præcepto Chaldæorum qui mittuntur ad nos : vos autem colligite vindemiam, et messem, et oleum, et condite in vasis vestris, et manete in urbibus vestris quas tenetis.
       EN: I am staying at Mizpah to answer the commands of the Chaldeans who are sent to us. But you should gather the vintage, the harvest, and the oil, store them in your vessels, and remain in the cities you occupy.”

47:11  LA: Sed et omnes Judæi qui erant in Moab, et in filiis Ammon, et in Idumæa, et in universis regionibus, audito quod dedisset rex Babylonis reliquias in Judæa, et quod præposuisset super eos Godoliam filium Ahicam filii Saphan,
       EN: All the Jews in Moab, among the Ammonites, in Edom, and in all the other regions also heard that the king of Babylon had left a remnant in Judea and had appointed Gedaliah son of Ahikam, son of Shaphan, over them.

47:12  LA: reversi sunt, inquam, omnes Judæi de universis locis ad quæ profugerant, et venerunt in terram Juda ad Godoliam in Masphath, et collegerunt vinum et messem multam nimis.
       EN: All the Jews returned from every place to which they had fled. They came to the land of Judah, to Gedaliah at Mizpah, and gathered wine and a very abundant harvest.

47:13  LA: Johanan autem filius Caree, et omnes principes exercitus qui dispersi fuerant in regionibus, venerunt ad Godoliam in Masphath,
       EN: Johanan son of Kareah and all the army commanders who had been scattered through the countryside came to Gedaliah at Mizpah.

47:14  LA: et dixerunt ei : Scito quod Baalis, rex filiorum Ammon, misit Ismahel filium Nathaniæ percutere animam tuam. Et non credidit eis Godolias filius Ahicam.
       EN: They said to him, “Know that Baalis, king of the Ammonites, has sent Ishmael son of Nethaniah to take your life.” But Gedaliah son of Ahikam did not believe them.

47:15  LA: Johanan autem filius Caree dixit ad Godoliam seorsum in Masphath, loquens : Ibo, et percutiam Ismahel filium Nathaniæ, nullo sciente, ne interficiat animam tuam, et dissipentur omnes Judæi qui congregati sunt ad te, et peribunt reliquiæ Juda.
       EN: Johanan son of Kareah spoke privately to Gedaliah at Mizpah: “I will go and kill Ishmael son of Nethaniah without anyone knowing, lest he take your life and all the Jews gathered to you be scattered, and the remnant of Judah perish.”

47:16  LA: Et ait Godolias filius Ahicam ad Johanan filium Caree : Noli facere verbum hoc : falsum enim tu loqueris de Ismahel.
       EN: Gedaliah son of Ahikam said to Johanan son of Kareah, “Do not do this thing, for what you say about Ishmael is false.”

JEREMIAH 48

48:1  LA: Et factum est in mense septimo, venit Ismahel filius Nathaniæ filii Elisama, de semine regali, et optimates regis, et decem viri cum eo, ad Godoliam filium Ahicam, in Masphath, et comederunt ibi panes simul in Masphath.
      EN: In the seventh month Ishmael son of Nethaniah, son of Elishama, of the royal line, came with the king’s nobles and ten men to Gedaliah son of Ahikam at Mizpah. They ate bread together there at Mizpah.

48:2  LA: Surrexit autem Ismahel filius Nathaniæ, et decem viri qui cum eo erant, et percusserunt Godoliam filium Ahicam filii Saphan gladio, et interfecerunt eum quem præfecerat rex Babylonis terræ.
      EN: Ishmael son of Nethaniah and the ten men with him rose and struck Gedaliah son of Ahikam, son of Shaphan, with the sword. They killed the man whom the king of Babylon had appointed over the land.

48:3  LA: Omnes quoque Judæos qui erant cum Godolia in Masphath, et Chaldæos qui reperti sunt ibi, et viros bellatores, percussit Ismahel.
      EN: Ishmael also struck down all the Jews who were with Gedaliah at Mizpah, the Chaldeans found there, and the fighting men.

48:4  LA: Secundo autem die postquam occiderat Godoliam, nullo adhuc sciente,
      EN: On the second day after he had killed Gedaliah, while no one yet knew of it,

48:5  LA: venerunt viri de Sichem, et de Silo, et de Samaria, octoginta viri, rasi barba, et scissis vestibus, et squallentes : et munera et thus habebant in manu, ut offerrent in domo Domini.
      EN: eighty men came from Shechem, Shiloh, and Samaria, their beards shaved, their garments torn, and their appearance unkempt. They carried offerings and incense in their hands to offer in the house of the Lord.

48:6  LA: Egressus ergo Ismahel filius Nathaniæ in occursum eorum de Masphath, incedens et plorans ibat : cum autem occurrisset eis, dixit ad eos : Venite ad Godoliam filium Ahicam.
      EN: Ishmael son of Nethaniah went out from Mizpah to meet them, walking and weeping as he went. When he met them, he said, “Come to Gedaliah son of Ahikam.”

48:7  LA: Qui cum venissent ad medium civitatis, interfecit eos Ismahel filius Nathaniæ circa medium laci, ipse et viri qui erant cum eo.
      EN: When they had come into the middle of the city, Ishmael son of Nethaniah and the men with him killed them at the cistern.

48:8  LA: Decem autem viri reperti sunt inter eos, qui dixerunt ad Ismahel : Noli occidere nos, quia habemus thesauros in agro, frumenti, et hordei, et olei, et mellis : et cessavit, et non interfecit eos cum fratribus suis.
      EN: But ten men among them said to Ishmael, “Do not kill us, for we have stores hidden in the field: wheat, barley, oil, and honey.” So he stopped and did not kill them with their brothers.

48:9  LA: Lacus autem in quem projecerat Ismahel omnia cadavera virorum quos percussit propter Godoliam, ipse est quem fecit rex Asa propter Baasa regem Israël : ipsum replevit Ismahel filius Nathaniæ occisis.
      EN: The cistern into which Ishmael threw all the corpses of the men he had killed on account of Gedaliah was the one King Asa had made because of Baasha king of Israel. Ishmael son of Nethaniah filled it with the slain.

48:10  LA: Et captivas duxit Ismahel omnes reliquias populi qui erant in Masphath, filias regis, et universum populum qui remanserat in Masphath, quos commendaverat Nabuzardan princeps militiæ, Godoliæ filio Ahicam : et cepit eos Ismahel filius Nathaniæ, et abiit ut transiret ad filios Ammon.
       EN: Ishmael took captive all the rest of the people at Mizpah: the king’s daughters and all the people remaining at Mizpah whom Nebuzaradan, commander of the army, had entrusted to Gedaliah son of Ahikam. Ishmael son of Nethaniah captured them and set out to cross over to the Ammonites.

48:11  LA: Audivit autem Johanan filius Caree, et omnes principes bellatorum qui erant cum eo, omne malum quod fecerat Ismahel filius Nathaniæ,
       EN: Johanan son of Kareah and all the commanders of the fighting men with him heard of all the evil Ishmael son of Nethaniah had done.

48:12  LA: et assumptis universis viris, profecti sunt ut bellarent adversum Ismahel filium Nathaniæ : et invenerunt eum ad aquas multas quæ sunt in Gabaon.
       EN: They took all their men and set out to fight against Ishmael son of Nethaniah. They found him by the great waters at Gibeon.

48:13  LA: Cumque vidisset omnis populus qui erat cum Ismahel Johanan filium Caree, et universos principes bellatorum qui erant cum eo, lætati sunt :
       EN: When all the people with Ishmael saw Johanan son of Kareah and all the commanders of the fighting men with him, they rejoiced.

48:14  LA: et reversus est omnis populus quem ceperat Ismahel, in Masphath, reversusque abiit ad Johanan filium Caree.
       EN: All the people whom Ishmael had captured at Mizpah turned around and went over to Johanan son of Kareah.

48:15  LA: Ismahel autem filius Nathaniæ fugit cum octo viris a facie Johanan, et abiit ad filios Ammon.
       EN: But Ishmael son of Nethaniah escaped from Johanan with eight men and went to the Ammonites.

48:16  LA: Tulit ergo Johanan filius Caree, et omnes principes bellatorum qui erant cum eo, universas reliquias vulgi quas reduxerat ab Ismahel filio Nathaniæ de Masphath, postquam percussit Godoliam filium Ahicam : fortes viros ad prælium, et mulieres, et pueros, et eunuchos, quos reduxerat de Gabaon.
       EN: Johanan son of Kareah and all the commanders of the fighting men with him took all the remnant of the people whom he had recovered from Ishmael son of Nethaniah, from Mizpah, after Ishmael had struck down Gedaliah son of Ahikam: men fit for war, women, children, and eunuchs, whom he had brought back from Gibeon.

48:17  LA: Et abierunt, et sederunt peregrinantes in Chamaam, quæ est juxta Bethlehem, ut pergerent, et introirent Ægyptum,
       EN: They went and stayed as travelers at Chimham near Bethlehem, intending to continue into Egypt

48:18  LA: a facie Chaldæorum : timebant enim eos, quia percusserat Ismahel filius Nathaniæ Godoliam filium Ahicam, quem præposuerat rex Babylonis in terra Juda.
       EN: to escape the Chaldeans. They feared them because Ishmael son of Nethaniah had killed Gedaliah son of Ahikam, whom the king of Babylon had appointed over the land of Judah.

JEREMIAH 49

49:1  LA: Et accesserunt omnes principes bellatorum, et Johanan filius Caree, et Jezonias filius Osaiæ, et reliquum vulgus, a parvo usque ad magnum,
      EN: All the commanders of the fighting men, Johanan son of Kareah, Jaazaniah son of Hoshaiah, and the rest of the people, from small to great, approached

49:2  LA: dixeruntque ad Jeremiam prophetam : Cadat oratio nostra in conspectu tuo, et ora pro nobis ad Dominum Deum tuum, pro universis reliquiis istis, quia derelicti sumus pauci de pluribus, sicut oculi tui nos intuentur :
      EN: and said to Jeremiah the prophet, “Let our plea come before you. Pray for us to the Lord your God, for all this remnant, for only a few of us remain out of many, as your own eyes see.

49:3  LA: et annuntiet nobis Dominus Deus tuus viam per quam pergamus, et verbum quod faciamus.
      EN: May the Lord your God tell us the way we should go and what we should do.”

49:4  LA: Dixit autem ad eos Jeremias propheta : Audivi. Ecce ego oro ad Dominum Deum vestrum secundum verba vestra : omne verbum quodcumque responderit mihi indicabo vobis, nec celabo vos quidquam.
      EN: Jeremiah the prophet said to them, “I have heard you. Behold, I will pray to the Lord your God according to your words. Whatever the Lord answers me, I will tell you, and hide nothing from you.”

49:5  LA: Et illi dixerunt ad Jeremiam : Sit Dominus inter nos testis veritatis et fidei, si non juxta omne verbum in quo miserit te Dominus Deus tuus ad nos, sic faciemus :
      EN: They said to Jeremiah, “May the Lord be a true and faithful witness between us if we fail to do everything the Lord your God sends you to tell us.

49:6  LA: sive bonum est, sive malum, voci Domini Dei nostri, ad quem mittimus te, obediemus, ut bene sit nobis cum audierimus vocem Domini Dei nostri.
      EN: Whether it is good or bad, we will obey the voice of the Lord our God, to whom we are sending you, so that it may go well with us when we obey the voice of the Lord our God.”

49:7  LA: Cum autem completi essent decem dies, factum est verbum Domini ad Jeremiam,
      EN: When ten days had passed, the word of the Lord came to Jeremiah.

49:8  LA: vocavitque Johanan filium Caree, et omnes principes bellatorum qui erant cum eo, et universum populum, a minimo usque ad magnum.
      EN: He called Johanan son of Kareah, all the commanders of the fighting men with him, and all the people, from the least to the greatest.

49:9  LA: Et dixit ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus Deus Israël, ad quem misistis me ut prosternerem preces vestras in conspectu ejus :
      EN: He said to them, “Thus says the Lord, the God of Israel, to whom you sent me to lay your prayers before Him:

49:10  LA: Si quiescentes manseritis in terra hac, ædificabo vos, et non destruam : plantabo, et non evellam : jam enim placatus sum super malo quod feci vobis.
       EN: If you settle and remain in this land, I will build you up and not tear you down, plant you and not uproot you. For I am now appeased concerning the calamity I brought upon you.

49:11  LA: Nolite timere a facie regis Babylonis, quem vos pavidi formidatis : nolite metuere eum, dicit Dominus, quia vobiscum sum ego ut salvos vos faciam, et eruam de manu ejus :
       EN: Do not fear the king of Babylon, before whom you tremble in dread. Do not be afraid of him, says the Lord, for I am with you to save you and rescue you from his hand.

49:12  LA: et dabo vobis misericordias, et miserebor vestri, et habitare vos faciam in terra vestra.
       EN: I will grant you mercies, have compassion on you, and make you dwell in your own land.

49:13  LA: Si autem dixeritis vos : Non habitabimus in terra ista, nec audiemus vocem Domini Dei nostri,
       EN: But if you say, ‘We will not dwell in this land, nor obey the voice of the Lord our God,’

49:14  LA: dicentes : Nequaquam, sed ad terram Ægypti pergemus, ubi non videbimus bellum, et clangorem tubæ non audiemus, et famem non sustinebimus, et ibi habitabimus :
       EN: and say, ‘No! We will go to the land of Egypt, where we will not see war, hear the trumpet’s sound, or suffer hunger; there we will dwell,’

49:15  LA: propter hoc nunc audite verbum Domini, reliquiæ Juda : Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Si posueritis faciem vestram ut ingrediamini Ægyptum, et intraveritis ut ibi habitetis,
       EN: then hear the word of the Lord now, remnant of Judah. Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: If you set your faces toward entering Egypt and go there to dwell,

49:16  LA: gladius quem vos formidatis ibi comprehendet vos in terra Ægypti : et fames, pro qua estis solliciti, adhærebit vobis in Ægypto, et ibi moriemini.
       EN: the sword you fear will overtake you there in the land of Egypt, and the famine you dread will cling to you in Egypt, and there you will die.

49:17  LA: Omnesque viri qui posuerunt faciem suam ut ingrediantur Ægyptum, ut habitent ibi, morientur gladio, et fame, et peste : nullus de eis remanebit, nec effugiet a facie mali quod ego afferam super eos.
       EN: All the men who set their faces toward entering Egypt to dwell there will die by sword, famine, and pestilence. None of them will remain or escape the calamity I will bring upon them.

49:18  LA: Quia hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Sicut conflatus est furor meus et indignatio mea super habitatores Jerusalem, sic conflabitur indignatio mea super vos cum ingressi fueritis Ægyptum : et eritis in jusjurandum, et in stuporem, et in maledictum, et in opprobrium, et nequaquam ultra videbitis locum istum.
       EN: For thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: As My fury and indignation were kindled against the inhabitants of Jerusalem, so My indignation will be kindled against you when you enter Egypt. You will become an oath, an object of horror, a curse, and a reproach, and you will never see this place again.

49:19  LA: Verbum Domini super vos, reliquiæ Juda : Nolite intrare Ægyptum : scientes scietis, quia obtestatus sum vos hodie,
       EN: This is the word of the Lord concerning you, remnant of Judah: Do not enter Egypt! Know for certain that I have warned you today.

49:20  LA: quia decepistis animas vestras. Vos enim misistis me ad Dominum Deum nostrum, dicentes : Ora pro nobis ad Dominum Deum nostrum, et juxta omnia quæcumque dixerit tibi Dominus Deus noster, sic annuntia nobis, et faciemus.
       EN: For you have deceived yourselves. You sent me to the Lord our God, saying, ‘Pray for us to the Lord our God, and tell us everything the Lord our God says to you, and we will do it.’

49:21  LA: Et annuntiavi vobis hodie, et non audistis vocem Domini Dei vestri super universis pro quibus misit me ad vos.
       EN: I have told you today, but you have not obeyed the voice of the Lord your God in any of the things for which He sent me to you.

49:22  LA: Nunc ergo scientes scietis quia gladio, et fame, et peste moriemini in loco ad quem voluistis intrare ut habitaretis ibi.
       EN: Now, then, know for certain that you will die by sword, famine, and pestilence in the place you wished to enter and dwell.”

JEREMIAH 50

50:1  LA: Factum est autem, cum complesset Jeremias loquens ad populum universos sermones Domini Dei eorum, pro quibus miserat eum Dominus Deus eorum ad illos, omnia verba hæc,
      EN: When Jeremiah had finished speaking to the people all the words of the Lord their God, everything the Lord their God had sent him to say to them,

50:2  LA: dixit Azarias filius Osaiæ, et Johanan filius Caree, et omnes viri superbi, dicentes ad Jeremiam : Mendacium tu loqueris : non misit te Dominus Deus noster, dicens : Ne ingrediamini Ægyptum ut habitetis illuc.
      EN: Azariah son of Hoshaiah, Johanan son of Kareah, and all the proud men said to Jeremiah, “You are speaking a lie! The Lord our God did not send you to say, ‘Do not enter Egypt to dwell there.’

50:3  LA: Sed Baruch filius Neriæ incitat te adversum nos, ut tradat nos in manus Chaldæorum, ut interficiat nos, et traduci faciat in Babylonem.
      EN: Baruch son of Neriah is stirring you up against us, to deliver us into the hands of the Chaldeans, to have us killed and carried away to Babylon.”

50:4  LA: Et non audivit Johanan filius Caree, et omnes principes bellatorum, et universus populus, vocem Domini, ut manerent in terra Juda.
      EN: Johanan son of Kareah, all the commanders of the fighting men, and all the people did not obey the voice of the Lord by remaining in the land of Judah.

50:5  LA: Sed tollens Johanan filius Caree, et universi principes bellatorum, universos reliquiarum Juda, qui reversi fuerant de cunctis gentibus ad quas fuerant ante dispersi, ut habitarent in terra Juda,
      EN: Instead, Johanan son of Kareah and all the commanders of the fighting men took the entire remnant of Judah who had returned from all the nations among whom they had been scattered to dwell in the land of Judah:

50:6  LA: viros, et mulieres, et parvulos, et filias regis, et omnem animam quam reliquerat Nabuzardan princeps militiæ cum Godolia filio Ahicam filii Saphan, et Jeremiam prophetam, et Baruch filium Neriæ :
      EN: the men, women, little children, the king’s daughters, and every person whom Nebuzaradan, commander of the army, had left with Gedaliah son of Ahikam, son of Shaphan, together with Jeremiah the prophet and Baruch son of Neriah.

50:7  LA: et ingressi sunt terram Ægypti, quia non obedierunt voci Domini, et venerunt usque ad Taphnis.
      EN: They entered the land of Egypt, because they did not obey the voice of the Lord, and came as far as Tahpanhes.

50:8  LA: Et factus est sermo Domini ad Jeremiam in Taphnis, dicens :
      EN: The word of the Lord came to Jeremiah at Tahpanhes:

50:9  LA: Sume lapides grandes in manu tua, et abscondes eos in crypta quæ est sub muro latericio in porta domus Pharaonis in Taphnis, cernentibus viris Judæis :
      EN: “Take large stones in your hand and hide them in the chamber beneath the brick wall at the entrance of Pharaoh’s palace in Tahpanhes, in the sight of the men of Judah.

50:10  LA: et dices ad eos : Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Ecce ego mittam et assumam Nabuchodonosor regem Babylonis, servum meum : et ponam thronum ejus super lapides istos quos abscondi, et statuet solium suum super eos :
       EN: Tell them, ‘Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Behold, I will send and bring Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon, My servant. I will place his throne upon these stones I have hidden, and he will set his royal seat over them.

50:11  LA: veniensque percutiet terram Ægypti, quos in mortem, in mortem, et quos in captivitatem, in captivitatem, et quos in gladium, in gladium :
       EN: He will come and strike the land of Egypt: those destined for death, to death; those for captivity, to captivity; those for the sword, to the sword.

50:12  LA: et succendet ignem in delubris deorum Ægypti, et comburet ea, et captivos ducet illos, et amicietur terra Ægypti sicut amicitur pastor pallio suo, et egredietur inde in pace :
       EN: He will kindle a fire in the shrines of the gods of Egypt and burn them, and carry the gods away captive. He will wrap himself in the land of Egypt as a shepherd wraps himself in his cloak, and he will depart from there in peace.

50:13  LA: et conteret statuas domus solis quæ sunt in terra Ægypti, et delubra deorum Ægypti comburet igni.
       EN: He will break the statues of the house of the sun in the land of Egypt and burn the shrines of the gods of Egypt with fire.’”

JEREMIAH 51

51:1  LA: Verbum quod factum est per Jeremiam ad omnes Judæos qui habitabant in terra Ægypti, habitantes in Magdalo, et in Taphnis, et in Memphis, et in terra Phatures, dicens :
      EN: The word that came through Jeremiah to all the Jews dwelling in the land of Egypt, at Migdol, Tahpanhes, Memphis, and in the land of Pathros:

51:2  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Vos vidistis omne malum istud quod adduxi super Jerusalem, et super omnes urbes Juda : et ecce desertæ sunt hodie, et non est in eis habitator,
      EN: “Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: You have seen all this calamity I brought upon Jerusalem and all the cities of Judah. Behold, today they are deserted, without an inhabitant,

51:3  LA: propter malitiam quam fecerunt ut me ad iracundiam provocarent, et irent ut sacrificarent, et colerent deos alienos quos nesciebant, et illi, et vos, et patres vestri.
      EN: because of the wickedness they committed to provoke Me to anger, going to sacrifice to and worship foreign gods whom neither they, nor you, nor your fathers knew.

51:4  LA: Et misi ad vos omnes servos meos prophetas, de nocte consurgens, mittensque et dicens : Nolite facere verbum abominationis hujuscemodi, quam odivi.
      EN: I sent you all My servants the prophets, rising in the night and sending them, saying, ‘Do not do this abominable thing that I hate.’

51:5  LA: Et non audierunt, nec inclinaverunt aurem suam, ut converterentur a malis suis, et non sacrificarent diis alienis.
      EN: But they did not listen or incline their ear, to turn from their evil ways and stop sacrificing to foreign gods.

51:6  LA: Et conflata est indignatio mea et furor meus, et succensa est in civitatibus Juda, et in plateis Jerusalem : et versæ sunt in solitudinem et vastitatem secundum diem hanc.
      EN: My indignation and fury were kindled and burned in the cities of Judah and in the streets of Jerusalem, and they became a desolation and a ruin, as they are today.

51:7  LA: Et nunc hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Quare vos facitis malum grande hoc contra animas vestras, ut intereat ex vobis vir et mulier, parvulus et lactens, de medio Judæ, nec relinquatur vobis quidquam residuum :
      EN: Now thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Why do you commit this great evil against your own lives, so that man and woman, little child and nursing infant may perish from the midst of Judah, leaving you no remnant?

51:8  LA: provocantes me in operibus manuum vestrarum, sacrificando diis alienis in terra Ægypti, in quam ingressi estis ut habitetis ibi : et dispereatis, et sitis in maledictionem et in opprobrium cunctis gentibus terræ ?
      EN: Why do you provoke Me by the works of your hands, sacrificing to foreign gods in the land of Egypt, where you have come to dwell, so that you perish and become a curse and a reproach among all the nations of the earth?

51:9  LA: Numquid obliti estis mala patrum vestrorum, et mala regum Juda, et mala uxorum ejus, et mala vestra, et mala uxorum vestrarum, quæ fecerunt in terra Juda, et in regionibus Jerusalem ?
      EN: Have you forgotten the wickedness of your fathers, the wickedness of the kings of Judah and their wives, your own wickedness, and the wickedness of your wives, committed in the land of Judah and in the regions of Jerusalem?

51:10  LA: Non sunt mundati usque ad diem hanc : et non timuerunt, et non ambulaverunt in lege Domini, et in præceptis meis quæ dedi coram vobis et coram patribus vestris.
       EN: To this day they have not been cleansed, nor have they feared or walked in the law of the Lord and in My commandments, which I set before you and your fathers.

51:11  LA: Ideo hæc dicit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël : Ecce ego ponam faciem meam in vobis in malum : et disperdam omnem Judam.
       EN: Therefore thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: Behold, I will set My face against you for harm and destroy all Judah.

51:12  LA: Et assumam reliquias Judæ, qui posuerunt facies suas ut ingrederentur terram Ægypti, et habitarent ibi, et consumentur omnes in terra Ægypti : cadent in gladio, et in fame, et consumentur a minimo usque ad maximum : in gladio et in fame morientur, et erunt in jusjurandum, et in miraculum, et in maledictionem, et in opprobrium.
       EN: I will take the remnant of Judah who set their faces toward entering the land of Egypt to dwell there, and they will all be consumed in the land of Egypt. They will fall by sword and famine and be consumed, from the least to the greatest. They will die by sword and famine and become an oath, an object of astonishment, a curse, and a reproach.

51:13  LA: Et visitabo super habitatores terræ Ægypti sicut visitavi super Jerusalem, in gladio, et fame, et peste :
       EN: I will call the inhabitants of the land of Egypt to account as I called Jerusalem to account, by sword, famine, and pestilence.

51:14  LA: et non erit qui effugiat, et sit residuus de reliquiis Judæorum qui vadunt ut peregrinentur in terra Ægypti, et revertantur in terram Juda, ad quam ipsi elevant animas suas ut revertantur, et habitent ibi : non revertentur, nisi qui fugerint.
       EN: No one of the remnant of Judah who have gone to sojourn in the land of Egypt will escape or remain to return to the land of Judah, to which they long to return and dwell. They will not return, except those who escape.”

51:15  LA: Responderunt autem Jeremiæ omnes viri scientes quod sacrificarent uxores eorum diis alienis, et universæ mulieres quarum stabat multitudo grandis, et omnis populus habitantium in terra Ægypti in Phatures, dicentes :
       EN: Then all the men who knew their wives were sacrificing to foreign gods, all the women standing there in a great multitude, and all the people dwelling in the land of Egypt at Pathros answered Jeremiah:

51:16  LA: Sermonem quem locutus es ad nos in nomine Domini, non audiemus ex te :
       EN: “We will not listen to the word you have spoken to us in the name of the Lord.

51:17  LA: sed facientes faciemus omne verbum quod egredietur de ore nostro, ut sacrificemus reginæ cæli, et libemus ei libamina, sicut fecimus nos et patres nostri, reges nostri et principes nostri, in urbibus Juda, et in plateis Jerusalem : et saturati sumus panibus, et bene nobis erat, malumque non vidimus.
       EN: We will certainly do everything that has gone forth from our mouth: sacrifice to the queen of heaven and pour out drink offerings to her, just as we, our fathers, our kings, and our rulers did in the cities of Judah and the streets of Jerusalem. Then we had bread in plenty, all went well with us, and we saw no calamity.

51:18  LA: Ex eo autem tempore quo cessavimus sacrificare reginæ cæli, et libare ei libamina, indigemus omnibus, et gladio et fame consumpti sumus.
       EN: But ever since we stopped sacrificing to the queen of heaven and pouring out drink offerings to her, we have lacked everything and been consumed by sword and famine.

51:19  LA: Quod si nos sacrificamus reginæ cæli, et libamus ei libamina, numquid sine viris nostris fecimus ei placentas ad colendum eam, et libandum ei libamina ?
       EN: When we sacrifice to the queen of heaven and pour out drink offerings to her, is it without our husbands that we make cakes to worship her and pour out drink offerings to her?”

51:20  LA: Et dixit Jeremias ad omnem populum, adversum viros, et adversum mulieres, et adversum universam plebem, qui responderant ei verbum, dicens :
       EN: Jeremiah spoke to all the people, to the men, the women, and all the multitude who had answered him:

51:21  LA: Numquid non sacrificium quod sacrificastis in civitatibus Juda, et in plateis Jerusalem, vos et patres vestri, reges vestri, et principes vestri, et populus terræ, horum recordatus est Dominus, et ascendit super cor ejus ?
       EN: “Did the Lord not remember the sacrifices you offered in the cities of Judah and the streets of Jerusalem, you, your fathers, your kings, your rulers, and the people of the land? Did they not come into His heart?

51:22  LA: Et non poterat Dominus ultra portare propter malitiam studiorum vestrorum, et propter abominationes quas fecistis : et facta est terra vestra in desolationem, et in stuporem, et in maledictum, eo quod non sit habitator, sicut est dies hæc.
       EN: The Lord could no longer bear your wicked conduct and the abominations you committed. So your land became a desolation, an object of horror, and a curse, without an inhabitant, as it is today.

51:23  LA: Propterea quod sacrificaveritis idolis, et peccaveritis Domino, et non audieritis vocem Domini, et in lege, et in præceptis, et in testimoniis ejus non ambulaveritis, idcirco evenerunt vobis mala hæc, sicut est dies hæc.
       EN: Because you sacrificed to idols, sinned against the Lord, did not obey the voice of the Lord, and did not walk in His law, His commandments, and His testimonies, these calamities have come upon you, as they have today.”

51:24  LA: Dixit autem Jeremias ad omnem populum, et ad universas mulieres : Audite verbum Domini, omnis Juda qui estis in terra Ægypti.
       EN: Jeremiah said to all the people and all the women, “Hear the word of the Lord, all Judah who are in the land of Egypt.

51:25  LA: Hæc inquit Dominus exercituum, Deus Israël, dicens : Vos et uxores vestræ locuti estis ore vestro, et manibus vestris implestis, dicentes : Faciamus vota nostra quæ vovimus, ut sacrificemus reginæ cæli, et libemus ei libamina. Implestis vota vestra, et opere perpetrastis ea.
       EN: Thus says the Lord of hosts, the God of Israel: You and your wives have spoken with your mouths and fulfilled it with your hands, saying, ‘Let us fulfill the vows we have made, to sacrifice to the queen of heaven and pour out drink offerings to her.’ You have fulfilled your vows and carried them out in deed.

51:26  LA: Ideo audite verbum Domini, omnis Juda qui habitatis in terra Ægypti : Ecce ego juravi in nomine meo magno, ait Dominus, quia nequaquam ultra vocabitur nomen meum ex ore omnis viri Judæi, dicentis : Vivit Dominus Deus, in omni terra Ægypti.
       EN: Therefore hear the word of the Lord, all Judah who dwell in the land of Egypt: Behold, I have sworn by My great name, says the Lord, that My name will no longer be invoked by the mouth of any man of Judah saying, ‘As the Lord God lives,’ throughout the land of Egypt.

51:27  LA: Ecce ego vigilabo super eos in malum, et non in bonum : et consumentur omnes viri Juda qui sunt in terra Ægypti gladio et fame, donec penitus consumantur.
       EN: Behold, I will watch over them for harm and not for good. All the men of Judah in the land of Egypt will be consumed by sword and famine until they are utterly consumed.

51:28  LA: Et qui fugerint gladium, revertentur de terra Ægypti in terram Juda viri pauci : et scient omnes reliquiæ Juda, ingredientium terram Ægypti ut habitent ibi, cujus sermo compleatur, meus an illorum.
       EN: Those who escape the sword will return from the land of Egypt to the land of Judah, only a few men. All the remnant of Judah who entered the land of Egypt to dwell there will know whose word is fulfilled, Mine or theirs.

51:29  LA: Et hoc vobis signum, ait Dominus, quod visitem ego super vos in loco isto, ut sciatis quia vere complebuntur sermones mei contra vos in malum :
       EN: This will be the sign to you, says the Lord, that I will call you to account in this place, so that you may know that My words against you for harm will certainly be fulfilled:

51:30  LA: hæc dicit Dominus : Ecce ego tradam Pharaonem Ephree regem Ægypti in manu inimicorum ejus, et in manu quærentium animam illius, sicut tradidi Sedeciam regem Juda in manu Nabuchodonosor regis Babylonis inimici sui, et quærentis animam ejus.
       EN: Thus says the Lord: Behold, I will deliver Pharaoh Hophra king of Egypt into the hands of his enemies and those who seek his life, just as I delivered Zedekiah king of Judah into the hand of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon, his enemy who sought his life.”

51:31  LA: Verbum quod locutus est Jeremias propheta ad Baruch filium Neriæ, cum scripsisset verba hæc in libro ex ore Jeremiæ, anno quarto Joakim filii Josiæ regis Juda, dicens :
       EN: The word Jeremiah the prophet spoke to Baruch son of Neriah when he had written these words in a book at Jeremiah’s dictation, in the fourth year of Jehoiakim son of Josiah, king of Judah:

51:32  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus Deus Israël ad te, Baruch :
       EN: “Thus says the Lord, the God of Israel, to you, Baruch:

51:33  LA: Dixisti : Væ misero mihi ! quoniam addidit Dominus dolorem dolori meo : laboravi in gemitu meo, et requiem non inveni.
       EN: You have said, ‘Alas, wretched me! The Lord has added sorrow to my sorrow. I am weary with my groaning and have found no rest.’

51:34  LA: Hæc dicit Dominus : Sic dices ad eum : Ecce quos ædificavi, ego destruo, et quos plantavi, ego evello, et universam terram hanc :
       EN: Thus says the Lord: Say this to him, ‘Behold, those I have built I am tearing down, and those I have planted I am uprooting, together with this whole land.

51:35  LA: et tu quæris tibi grandia ? noli quærere, quia ecce ego adducam malum super omnem carnem, ait Dominus, et dabo tibi animam tuam in salutem in omnibus locis ad quæcumque perrexeris.
       EN: And do you seek great things for yourself? Do not seek them. For behold, I am bringing calamity upon all flesh, says the Lord, but I will give you your life for safety in every place to which you go.’”

JEREMIAH 52

52:1  LA: Filius viginti et unius anni erat Sedecias cum regnare cœpisset, et undecim annis regnavit in Jerusalem. Et nomen matris ejus Amital filia Jeremiæ de Lobna.
      EN: Zedekiah was twenty-one years old when he began to reign, and he reigned eleven years in Jerusalem. His mother’s name was Hamutal, daughter of Jeremiah of Libnah.

52:4  LA: Factum est autem in anno nono regni ejus, in mense decimo, decima mensis, venit Nabuchodonosor rex Babylonis, ipse et omnis exercitus ejus, adversus Jerusalem : et obsederunt eam, et ædificaverunt contra eam munitiones in circuitu.
      EN: In the ninth year of his reign, in the tenth month, on the tenth day of the month, Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon came against Jerusalem with his whole army. They besieged it and built siegeworks against it all around.

52:5  LA: Et fuit civitas obsessa usque ad undecimum annum regis Sedeciæ.
      EN: The city remained under siege until the eleventh year of King Zedekiah.

52:6  LA: Mense autem quarto, nona mensis, obtinuit fames civitatem, et non erant alimenta populo terræ.
