      EN: In the fourth month, on the ninth day of the month, famine prevailed in the city, and there was no food for the people of the land.

52:7  LA: Et dirupta est civitas, et omnes viri bellatores ejus fugerunt, exieruntque de civitate nocte, per viam portæ quæ est inter duos muros, et ducit ad hortum regis, Chaldæis obsidentibus urbem in gyro, et abierunt per viam quæ ducit in eremum.
      EN: The city was breached, and all its fighting men fled. They went out of the city by night by way of the gate between the two walls that leads to the king’s garden, while the Chaldeans surrounded the city. They went by the road leading into the wilderness.

52:8  LA: Persecutus est autem Chadæorum exercitus regem, et apprehenderunt Sedeciam in deserto quod est juxta Jericho : et omnis comitatus ejus diffugit ab eo.
      EN: The Chaldean army pursued the king and captured Zedekiah in the wilderness near Jericho. All his companions had scattered from him.

52:9  LA: Cumque comprehendissent regem, adduxerunt eum ad regem Babylonis in Reblatha, quæ est in terra Emath, et locutus est ad eum judicia.
      EN: They seized the king and brought him to the king of Babylon at Riblah, in the land of Hamath, and he pronounced judgment upon him.

52:10  LA: Et jugulavit rex Babylonis filios Sedeciæ in oculis ejus, sed et omnes principes Juda occidit in Reblatha.
       EN: The king of Babylon slaughtered Zedekiah’s sons before his eyes and also killed all the rulers of Judah at Riblah.

52:11  LA: Et oculos Sedeciæ eruit, et vinxit eum compedibus, et adduxit eum rex Babylonis in Babylonem, et posuit eum in domo carceris usque ad diem mortis ejus.
       EN: He put out Zedekiah’s eyes and bound him in fetters. The king of Babylon took him to Babylon and put him in prison until the day of his death.

52:12  LA: In mense autem quinto, decima mensis, ipse est annus nonusdecimus Nabuchodonosor regis Babylonis, venit Nabuzardan princeps militiæ, qui stabat coram rege Babylonis, in Jerusalem,
       EN: In the fifth month, on the tenth day of the month, in the nineteenth year of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon, Nebuzaradan, commander of the army, who served before the king of Babylon, came to Jerusalem.

52:13  LA: et incendit domum Domini, et domum regis, et omnes domos Jerusalem : et omnem domum magnam igni combussit :
       EN: He burned the house of the Lord, the king’s palace, and all the houses of Jerusalem. Every great house he burned with fire.

52:14  LA: et totum murum Jerusalem per circuitum destruxit cunctus exercitus Chaldæorum qui erat cum magistro militiæ.
       EN: The whole Chaldean army with the commander of the troops demolished all the walls around Jerusalem.

52:16  LA: De pauperibus vero terræ reliquit Nabuzardan princeps militiæ vinitores et agricolas.
       EN: But Nebuzaradan, commander of the army, left some of the poor of the land as vine growers and farmers.

52:17  LA: Columnas quoque æreas quæ erant in domo Domini, et bases, et mare æneum quod erat in domo Domini, confregerunt Chaldæi, et tulerunt omne æs eorum in Babylonem,
       EN: The Chaldeans broke apart the bronze pillars of the house of the Lord, the stands, and the bronze sea in the house of the Lord, and carried all their bronze to Babylon.

52:18  LA: et lebetes, et creagras, et psalteria, et phialas, et mortariola, et omnia vasa ærea quæ in ministerio fuerant, tulerunt :
       EN: They also took the pots, forks, psalteries, bowls, small mortars, and all the bronze vessels that had been used in the service.

52:19  LA: et hydrias, et thymiamateria, et urceos, et pelves, et candelabra, et mortaria, et cyathos, quotquot aurea, aurea, et quotquot argentea, argentea, tulit magister militiæ :
       EN: The commander of the troops took the jars, censers, pitchers, basins, lampstands, mortars, and cups: whatever was gold as gold, and whatever was silver as silver.

52:20  LA: et columnas duas, et mare unum, et vitulos duodecim æreos qui erant sub basibus quas fecerat rex Salomon in domo Domini. Non erat pondus æris omnium horum vasorum.
       EN: There were also the two pillars, the one sea, and the twelve bronze oxen beneath the stands that King Solomon had made in the house of the Lord. The bronze of all these vessels was beyond weighing.

52:21  LA: De columnis autem decem et octo cubiti altitudinis erant in columna una, et funiculus duodecim cubitorum circuibat eam : porro grossitudo ejus quatuor digitorum, et intrinsecus cava erat.
       EN: As for the pillars, one pillar was eighteen cubits high, and a cord of twelve cubits encircled it. It was four fingers thick and hollow inside.

52:22  LA: Et capitella super utramque ærea : altitudo capitelli unius quinque cubitorum, et retiacula et malogranata super coronam in circuitu, omnia ærea : similiter columnæ secundæ, et malogranata.
       EN: Bronze capitals stood upon both. Each capital was five cubits high, with latticework and pomegranates around the crown, all of bronze. The second pillar and its pomegranates were the same.

52:23  LA: Et fuerunt malogranata nonaginta sex dependentia : et omnia malogranata centum, retiaculis circumdabantur.
       EN: There were ninety-six hanging pomegranates. All the pomegranates, a hundred in number, were surrounded by latticework.

52:24  LA: Et tulit magister militiæ Saraiam sacerdotem primum, et Sophoniam sacerdotem secundum, et tres custodes vestibuli :
       EN: The commander of the troops took Seraiah the chief priest, Zephaniah the second priest, and three keepers of the entrance.

52:25  LA: et de civitate tulit eunuchum unum, qui erat præpositus super viros bellatores : et septem viros de his qui videbant faciem regis, qui inventi sunt in civitate : et scribam principem militum, qui probabat tyrones : et sexaginta viros de populo terræ, qui inventi sunt in medio civitatis.
       EN: From the city he took one eunuch who was in charge of the fighting men, seven men who had access to the king and were found in the city, the scribe who was chief of the soldiers and examined recruits, and sixty men of the people of the land who were found within the city.

52:26  LA: Tulit autem eos Nabuzardan magister militiæ, et duxit eos ad regem Babylonis in Reblatha :
       EN: Nebuzaradan, commander of the troops, took them and brought them to the king of Babylon at Riblah.

52:27  LA: et percussit eos rex Babylonis, et interfecit eos in Reblatha in terra Emath : et translatus est Juda de terra sua.
       EN: The king of Babylon struck them down and killed them at Riblah in the land of Hamath. So Judah was deported from its own land.

52:31  LA: Et factum est in trigesimo septimo anno transmigrationis Joachin regis Juda, duodecimo mense, vigesima quinta mensis, elevavit Evilmerodach rex Babylonis, ipso anno regni sui, caput Joachin regis Juda, et eduxit eum de domo carceris.
       EN: In the thirty-seventh year of the exile of Jehoiachin king of Judah, in the twelfth month, on the twenty-fifth day of the month, Evil-merodach king of Babylon, in the year he began to reign, lifted up the head of Jehoiachin king of Judah and brought him out of prison.

52:32  LA: Et locutus est cum eo bona, et posuit thronum ejus super thronos regum qui erant post se in Babylone.
       EN: He spoke kindly to him and set his throne above the thrones of the kings who were under him in Babylon.

52:33  LA: Et mutavit vestimenta carceris ejus, et comedebat panem coram eo semper cunctis diebus vitæ suæ.
       EN: He changed his prison garments, and Jehoiachin ate bread before him continually, all the days of his life.

52:34  LA: Et cibaria ejus, cibaria perpetua dabantur ei a rege Babylonis, statuta per singulos dies, usque ad diem mortis suæ, cunctis diebus vitæ ejus.
       EN: A continual allowance of food was given him by the king of Babylon, a daily portion until the day of his death, all the days of his life.
